|| śrīḥ ||

<1ā>

svasti śrīgaṇādhipataye namaḥ |

nirvighnamastu |

mātāmahamahāśailaṃ mahastadapitāmaham |
kāraṇaṃ jagatāṃ vande kaṇṭhādupari vāraṇam ||

aiśvaraṃ paramaṃ tattvamādimadhyāntavarjitam |
ādhāraṃ sarvalokānāmanādhāramavikriyam ||

anantānandabodhāmbunidhimadbhutavikramam |
ambikāpatimīśānamaniśaṃ praṇamāmyaham ||

vande hālāsyanagarīṃ vande vande hemābjinīmapi |
vande sumīnanayanāṃ vande sundaranāyakam ||

śivāya paripūrṇāya śītalāya cidātmane |
cittanāthāya nāthāya tejase brahmaṇe namaḥ ||

ānandasāgararasā kulaśailakanyā
vakṣojakumbhayugalīparirambhaṇena |
romāñca (***) pariṣkṛtagātrayaṣṭiṃ
vede bhajāmi madhurāpuranāthamīśam ||

<p. 2>

ikṣukodaṇḍapuṣpeṣu pāśāṅkuśakarojvalām |
udyatsūryanibhāṃ vande mahātripurasundarīm ||

namaḥ sundaranāthāya tasmai hālāsyavāsine |
catuṣṣaṣṭividhā līlā yena pratyakṣitāḥ kṣitau ||

śrīmatsundaranāthasya devīṃ śapharalocanīm |
kalaye hṛdaye nityaṃ kadambavanavāsinīm ||

kāruṇyamadaniṣyandaṃ kaivalyavanacāriṇam |
kālāmbudaśyāmalāṅgaṃ kalabhānanamāśraye ||

āmnāyācalamañcasthamānanaiḥ ṣaḍbhiranvitam |
himādritanayāstanyakukṣiṃbharamaye śiśum ||

śrīkailāsa iti khyātaḥ śrīmatāṃ bhūbhṛtāṃvaraḥ |
śrīpatipramukhairdevaiḥ śriyai muktyai ca sevitaḥ ||

śrīdādīnāmapi śrīdaḥ śrīkaṇṭhaprathitālayaḥ |
śriyo'pi hetoḥ kṣīrābdhe svayaṃ jitvā samunnataḥ ||

janānāṃ śravaṇādeva janmamṛtyujayāpahaḥ |
<p. 3> sarvaśaktisamāyuktaḥ sarvalokanamaskṛtaḥ ||

bhūḥ surākīrṇanaṃ vāpi kurvatāṃ sarvasiddhidaḥ |
tapovanena mahatā paśyatāmapavargadaḥ ||

vividhairbhūtasaṃghātaiḥ paritaḥ parirakṣitaḥ |
vibudhairapyagamyasya viśvabharturanujñayā ||

viṣṭapatrayavikhyāto vidhumaṇḍalapāṇḍaraḥ |
vidyate bhuvi diśyaiśāṃ vidvajjanamanasyapi ||

tasya madhye mahāmerormadhyaśṛṅgaṃ mahattaram |
praśastamasti śikharaṃ mahāmaṇḍalamaṇḍitam ||

tadūrdhve pavano'tyarthaṃ darpaṇodaravatsamam |
vyomavadvimalībhūtaṃ viśālaṃ bhavati sthalam ||

tapo'ntarāyai kāmādyairamaraiḥ paripanthibhiḥ |
ajeyaṃ tatsthalaṃ pāti munīndrāṇāṃ mano yathā ||

kānanaḥ kalpavṛkṣāṇāṃ phalapuṣpasamanvitaiḥ |
cintāmaṇimayaiḥ śailaiḥ kāmadhenu bhavairapi ||

payodadhighṛtakṣaudrasudhāmayasaridvaraiḥ |
<p. 4> nānāratnavicitraiśca kuṭṭimairatimañjulaiḥ ||

pramathānāṃ mandiraiśca meroḥ śikharasaṃnibhaiḥ |
śātakumbhamayānekaprākāradvāragopuraiḥ ||

sūryakoṭipratīkāśaiścandrakoṭisamaprabhaiḥ |
va (**********) ||




|| svasti ||

| śrīgaṇādhipataye namaḥ |

śubhamastu

<1a> krodhisaṃvatsarada vaiśākhaśuddha (12?) śukravāra 
īpustakavabaravadakke prārambha | nirvighnamastu | śrīmahāgaṇapataye 
namaḥ ||

viśvarūpa namastubhyaṃ bhaktavatsala śāśvata |
mama liṅge ca bimbe vā chinne nāśādhikaṃ katham ||

yadi doṣādike prāpte pūjāṃ kartuṃ kathaṃ śiva |
vistarādbrūhi bhagavan śrotumicchāmi sāmpratam ||

śrībhagavān uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi bhinnadoṣādikān kramāt |
svayambhuve sāṃba liṅge bimbe vā yatra kutracit ||

doṣaṃ śāntiphalaṃ devi vistarācca pṛthak pṛthak |
bhinne ca sphuṭite liṅge vahnināśanināhate ||

<p. 2> paraṃ rāṣṭrabhayaṃ devi trivarṣāntaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
rājā ca duḥkhaśokārtaḥ jāyate ca balakṣayaḥ ||

khaṇḍite śakalībhūte yadi nāśe ca hīnake |
deśatyāgo bhavetsadyaḥ rājā bhaṅgatvamāpnuyāt ||

nidhanaṃ prāpnuyāddevi trimāsāntaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
pāṣāṇairāyudhādyairvā corvā śatrubhirhate ||

rājā tu maraṇaṃ yāyāt caturaṅgabalaṃ rathā |
rodhane rudhirotpattiḥ svedakampe ca nādate ||

tadrāṣṭraṃ paṭṭaṇaṃ devi rājadānī vinaśyati |
bahudhā śatrubādhāsyādanāvṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati ||

durbhikṣaṃ durbhayaṃ mārī jvarāpasmārakādayaḥ |
vivarṇedhumasaṃjāte vahiśvadudakaṃ payaḥ ||

raktasrāvaṃ kīṭakādyairyadiliṅge maheśvari |
prajāpīḍā garbhapīḍā nṛpapīḍā dvijādiṣu ||

nirghātasarpādibhayaṃ vātabādhā masūśikā |
evaṃ doṣādi saṃprāpte navaliṅge ca pārvati |
<p. 3> ātmaśūnyaṃ yathā dehaṃ saṃpraviśyeha rākṣasāḥ ||

tathaiva devatāśūnyaṃ bhinna liṅge maheśvari |
doṣāḍhye pūjite hanti nihanti tadapūjite ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena tavaliṅgaṃ rakṣayet |
yadyasmācca sa saṃprāpte sandhānaṃ śāntimācaret ||

liṅge bhinne khaṇḍite ca cihnite ca gurūttamaḥ |
ekāṅgulaṃ tadardhaṃ vā caturasraṃ ca pārvati ||

bandhena bandhayelliṅgaṃ sandhānaṃ ca gurūttamaḥ |
mahāśāntiṃ kramāṇitvā talliṅgaṃ pūjayetpunaḥ ||

pramādācchakalībhūte svarṇaraupyādisūtrataḥ |
sandhayetsudṛḍhaṃ mahāśāntiṃ samācaret ||

dahite secayenmantrī snapanādyairgurūttamam |
corairvā śatrubhirdevi bhaye prāpte ca rakṣayet ||

kumbhe kalāṃ samāvāhya sikatena nigūhayet |
tatkumbhaṃ pūjayeddevi bhayāntaṃ tava liṅgake ||

nirgate cettato mantrī śāntiṃ kuryādvidhānataḥ |
<p. 4> tatkumbhasthakalāṃ devi punarāvāhya liṅgake ||

yathāvidyuktamārgeṇa guruḥ kuryānmaheśvari |
yadi nāśo bhavelliṅgaṃ pramādācca maheśvari ||

navaliṅgaṃ ca saṃsthāpya yadvā tadguṇavatyapi |
tadrūpaṃ tanmayaṃ saumyaṃ tanmānaṃ nānyathā śubham ||

evaṃ jñātvā prayatnena jīrṇamuddhṛtya yojayet |
pratiṣṭhoktena kurvīta mahāśāntiṃ samācaret ||

rodanādiṣu liṅgeṣu jāte yadi maheśvari |
liṅgaṃ saṃkṣālya saṃśodhya pañcaśuddhiṃ yathoktavat ||

mahāśāntiṃ ca kurvīta navaliṅgaṃ ca pūjayet |
yadi bimbe ca saṃprāpte vakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati ||

balau nityoktabimbe vā mūlaberādike tathā |
yadyakasmābhūtale ca patite khaṇḍite sati ||

taskarebhyaśca śatrubhyaḥ vāgninā vā maheśvari |
bhinne ca sphuṭite bimbe sarvāṅge śithile pṛthak ||

khaṇḍite khaṇḍanaṃ rājñāṃ patite sarvanāśanam |
<p. 5> dahite dahanaṃ rāṣṭraṃ rājño bhogaṃ vinaśyati ||

bhinne bhaṅgatvamāpnoti sphuṭite ca dhanakṣayam |
sarvāṅge śithile jāte sarvārthaṃ naśyati dhruvam ||

pādo bhinne sthānanāśaṃ janujaṅghe janakṣayaḥ |
nissatirbhavedrājā kaṭibhinne maheśvari ||

bāhubhinne śatrubādhā vijayaṃ nāpnuyātkvacit |
chinne kaṇṭhe tataḥ śīghraṃ rājā maraṇamāpnuyāt ||

mūrtarūpāṅgavicchede rāṣṭranāśo dhanakṣayaḥ |
mukhyāṅgaṃ bhaṅgamāpanne tāṃ mūrtiṃ ca parityajet ||

navāṃ mūrtiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya śāntiṃ kuryādgurūttamaḥ |
tadupāṅgādi bhagne tu sandhānaṃ yattato nayet ||

svarṇapittalatāmrādyaiḥ niścalaṃ hi yathā tathā |
mahāśāntiṃ vidhāyātha sandhānātpūjayetpriye ||

aṅgulordhve yadā bhinne tāṃ mūrtiṃ salile tyajet |
pratiṣṭhoktena mārgeṇa śāntiṃ kuryādvidhānataḥ ||

navāṃ mūrtiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya karṣaṇaiśca yathoktavat |
<p. 6> taskarebhyaśca śatrubhyaḥ bimbe cāpahṛte sati ||

sarvanāśo bhavetsadyaḥ rāpyāṃ kūpataṭākayoḥ |
prāsādādiṣu sarvatra yadi sthāne samudbhavāḥ ||

dahane khaṇḍite sphoṭe calite nimnage śive |
patite nāmṛte bhinne vāhane parivārake ||

dhvajādakalaśe bhinne balipīṭhe pramādataḥ |
kṣetrapāle dvārapālau calite patite sati ||

dīpastambhe cordhvamālā bhinne jāte pramādataḥ |
prākere ca bahiścaiva antaḥprākārakeṣu ca ||

vīthimadhye bahiścaiva ārāme maṇṭape sthale |
utsave balikāle vā nityanaimittike'pi vā ||

kāmye vā yadi kāleṣu jāyate ca śṛṇu priye |
balibimbe ca patane chinne bhinne pramādataḥ ||

pādukeṃ balipātrāṇi chatracāmarakādi ca |
patākā vijanaṃ devi darpaṇaṃ jalapātrakam ||

parṇapātraṃ taducchiṣṭaṃ (**) dīnāṃ girīndraje |
<p. 7> udbhavaṃ cedidaṃ devi tattasthāne pracakṣate ||

teṣāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi utpātān śṛṇu pārvati |
svayambhuvādiliṅge ca bimbe vā yadi pārvati ||

rodhanaṃ rudhirotpattiḥ svedakampaṃ ca nādakam |
sphuṭitaṃ pātitaṃ devi vivarṇaṃ dhūmasaṃbhavaḥ ||

vahniśvedudakaṃ raktaṃ payaḥ kīṭakadarśanam |
tiṣṭhatyūrdhvamukho devaḥ karapādavihīnakaḥ ||

adhomukhādyanekāni hīnāsyaḥ kṛśavāmanaḥ |
liṅganāśe ca bimbe vā corairvā śatrubhiḥ priye ||

garbhāgārādi vakṣyāmi utpātān śṛṇu pārvati |
garbhagehe prāṅkaṇe vā lakṣmīmaṇṭapasaṃjñake ||

prākāre gopure devi śikhare śikharāntare |
maṇṭape vāhane devi parivārālayādike ||

dvāradeśe sabhāsthāne īśānyāmaṣṭakoṇakam |
aindraśāṅkarayormadhye vṛttakuṇḍaṃ tu kārayet ||

yāvanmānaṃ tu vistāraṃ tāvanmānaṃ pramāṇakam |
<p. 8> trimekhalāsamāyuktaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam ||

hastadvayayutaṃ khātaṃ ūrdhvamekhalayā saha |
ityevaṃ kārayetkuṇḍaṃ dvātriṃśa  mbhasaṃyutam ||

cūtapallavaramyaiśca patākadhvajaśobhitam |
prāgdvāre pītavarṇaṃ ca dakṣiṇe kṛṣṇavarṇakam ||

paścime raktavarṇaṃ ca uttare śvetameva ca |
pūrvādidvāradeśaṃ ca tauraṇaiścāpyalaṅkṛtam ||

kadalīṃ sthāpya matimān navavastreṇa veṣṭayet ||
paṇṭapañcāpyalaṅkṛtya saphalaṅkurapuṣpakaiḥ |
ityevaṃ maṇṭape ramye śāntiṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||

navāhaṃ saptarātraṃ vā pañcatri (**********) |
(**********)||

(*) sūrye samutpanne svasthānaṃ nāśamāpnuyāt |
rāṣṭradurgabalaṃ devi pararājñāhṛta bhavet ||

dvijādiśca trayovarṇāḥ śūdratvaṃ prāpnuvanti hi |
nissatvaṃ nirjanatvaṃ ca sarvakarmāṇi nāśanam ||

<p. 9> iti doṣaharaṃ devi darśayecca nṛpottamaḥ |
candratārānukūle ca nakṣatrakaraṇānvite ||

rājño rāṣṭrānukūle ca uddiśya divase guruḥ |
maṇṭapān kārayeddevi tava liṅgādike sthale ||

pūrve vā cottare vāpi īśānyāṃ maṇṭapaṃ guraḥ |
viṃśaddhastapramāṇaṃ tu caturaśraṃ savedikam ||

navakuṇḍaṃ ca paritaḥ pūrvādiṣu yathākramam |
tatpūrve caturaśraṃ syāt āgneyāṃ tu bhagākṛtiḥ ||

ardhacandraṃ tu yāmyāyāṃ nai(-)ṛ(-)ṛtyāṃ tu trikoṇakam |
vāruṇe vartulaṃ kuṇḍaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ caiva vāyave ||

uttare padmakuṇḍaṃ tu (**********) |
(***) ṣādisaṃprāpte śāntiṃ kuryādgurūttamaḥ ||

sarvadoṣaharaṃ sadyaḥ apamṛtyunivāraṇam |
sarvabhogāṃśca labhate satyaṃ satyaṃ macoditam ||

o [iti śrīvātulottare

prāyaścittakhaṇḍe utpātajātasthānavidhirnāma catvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||




śrīdevyuvāca (-)

samyaguktaṃ tvayā śambho utpātā (**) yodbhavāḥ |
tatsarvaṃ ca śrutaṃ deva doṣaṃ śāntiṃ pṛthak katham ||

vadasva kṛpayā deva sarvalokahitāya ca |

śivaḥ (-)

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi doṣaṃ śāntiṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||

tatphalaṃ ca krameṇaiva vistarācchṛṇu pārvati |
bhūmikampe diśāṃdāhe jāte yadi mahadbhayam ||

tadrāṣṭraṃ nāśamāpnoti trimāsāntaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
paṭṭaṇe rājadhānyādi sarvasthāne ca kampane ||

rājñastu maraṇaṃ sadyaḥ sarvanāśaṃ bhavetpriye |
prati (**********) devi rājadānī vinaśyati ||

bahudhā śatrubādhāsyuḥ anāvṛṣṭirnasaṃśayaḥ |
nadī gehe ca saṃprāpte trimāsā mṛtyumāpnuyāt ||

prajāpīḍā garbhapīḍā nṛpapīḍā dvijādiṣu |
vetālakṣetrapālāśca jvarāpasmārakādayaḥ ||

<p. 11> evamādīni cānyāni deśe tatra prajāyate |
taddoṣaśamanārthāya śāntiṃ kuryānnṛpottamaḥ ||

devyauvāca (-)

mahadutpātajanane ariṣṭe cāgate sati |
śāntiṃ kṛtāceddeveśa nāśanaṃ ca kathaṃ vada ||

śivaḥ (-)

yathā bhujagadaṣṭastu gāruḍenauṣadhena ca |
svastho bhavati deveśi tathā śāntirtṛṇā priye ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena auṣadhaṃ bhiṣajā sadā |
tathā dau(**********) raṇe sati ||

pattanādi gṛhe devi vane copavane sthale |
parvate codbhave devi śṛṇu devi yathākramam ||

govadhe strīvadheścaiva dvijādīnāṃ ca nāśane |
vyāleterpāvaduṣṭebhyaḥ śastrādyaiśca maheśvari ||

maṇḍūkena mṛtedevi kūpe kūbhasamudbhave |
anaḍvān mahiṣavyāghrakharoṣṭrādi praveśane ||

<p. 12> agādhe bhūmipatane grāmādīnāṃ maheśvari |
gambhīraiśca nadīsrotaiḥ liṅge grāmādināśane ||

caityavṛkṣādipatane vane vā sarvapādapaiḥ |
parvate śakalairbhinnairyadi bhūme nipātite ||

evamādīni nāmāni utpātāni maheśvari |
tatra doṣaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi varānane ||

rājñastu maraṇaṃ sadyaḥ caturaṅgabalakṣayaḥ |
tadrāṣṭrapattanaṃ (**) āyudhānāṃ maheśvari ||

vetraṃ ca balidīpaṃ ca śaṅkhabheryādipātane |
deśike pārake devi bimba dāruka eva vā ||

vādyake sevake devi patite ca maheśvari |
śibike rathabhaṅge ca vāhane patite tathā ||

vāhanārthaṃ gajāśvādyaiḥ vṛṣabhaiścāpyalaṅkṛtaiḥ |
senānāyakavastrāṇāṃ dhārakaiśca nṛbhiḥ priye ||

parasparavirodhena patite raktapātane |
muṣṭiyuddhe daṇḍayuddhe nakhadantakṣate sati ||

<p. 13> śastrairvāhataduṣṭebhyaḥ etaiḥ prāṇi mṛte sati |
praveśe maraṇe prāpte śṛṇudevi samudbhavam ||

vanyairvā prāṇibhirduṣṭaiḥ dūṣite ca catuṣpadaiḥ |
dvipadaiḥ kṣudrajantū nadīṃ vyālairpā haṃsagṛdhrakaiḥ ||

madhupañjarasaṃbhūtaiḥ valmīkaiśca samudbhavam |
bṛhatpipīlikā kṣudrapraveśetekameva vā ||

śāntikarmaprakurvīta dīkṣāṃ kuryādguruḥ priye |
vapanaṃ snānamaunaṃ ca śuklāmbaradharaṃ śuciḥ ||

kṣīrānnabhojanaṃ nityaṃ dussaṅgādivivarjitaḥ |
brahmacaryavrataṃ kuryāt ācāryādi dvijāṃ priye ||

dīkṣākarma prakartavyaṃ navarātrau yathāvidhi |
trirātraṃ caikarātraṃ ca dīkṣākarma na kārayet ||

yāgadravyāṇi saṃpādya hotṛbhirjāpakādibhiḥ |
prātarutthāya vidhivaddantadhāvanapūrvakam ||

prātassnānaṃ prakurvīta ācāryo yajamānakaḥ |
sandhyākarma prakurvīta nityakarmavidhāya ca ||

<p. 14> mṛttikāsnānapūrvaṃ ca kuśagomayabhasmanā |
sarvaghoṣasamāyuktaṃ puṇyastrībhiḥ samanvitam ||

nānāmaṅgalavādyaṃ ca vedaghoṣaiśca sūktakaiḥ |
snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena śāntiṃ kuru maheśvari ||

tato devālayaṃ gatvā phalapuṣpaṃ samarpya ca |
prārthayenyajamānena namaskāraiśca bhaktitaḥ ||

ācāryaḥ sahavipraiśca praviśedyāgamaṇṭapam |
ācāryavandanaṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvevā caiśakoṇake ||

darbhāsane copaviśya prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret |
pūṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca prokṣayetpraśana priye ||

daśadigbandhanaṃ kṛtvā mātṛkānyāsapūrvakam |
dinasaṅkhyaṃ samuddiśya saṅkalpya vidhipūrvakam ||

gaṇeśaṃ ca samārādhya nāndīkarma samācaret |
puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca prokṣayetprāśana priye ||

varaye bhūsurān devi ācāryādidvijottamān |
svaśākhoktaratān sarvān brahmadeśikahotṛkān ||

<p. 15> ghoṣakān jāpakān devi svasvaśākhayutāḥ priye |
varaye bhūsurān mantrī phalaṃ datvā pṛthak pṛthak ||

alaṅkāraṃ suvastraṃ ca madhuparkaṃ ca dāpayet |
aṅkurārpaṇapūrvaṃ ca śodhayenmaṇṭape gurūḥ ||

dvāratoraṇakumbhāṃśca sthāpayet pūjayet priye |
rākṣa(so)ghnaṃ vāstuhomaṃ ca pūjā bali samācaret ||

gaṇeśakṣetrapālaṃ ca bhūsūktaṃ vāstusaṃjñakam |
rākṣoghaṃ caturastraṃ ca mṛtyuñjayanavagraham ||

mahāsauraṃ saptaśatiṃ prāsādaṃ ca maheśvari |
śaktipañcākṣarīṃ japya vedapārāyaṇaṃ pṛthak ||

vinidrākarma kurvīta punaḥ sūryodaye priye |
nityakarmāṇi kurvīta puṇyāhaṃ prokṣaṇādikam ||

maṇṭapaṃ pūrvadigbhāge grahamaṇḍalamullikhet |
caturasraṃ samaṃ kṛtvā grahākāraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

grahasaṃkhyāni kurvīta tatra koṣṭhe maheśvari |
tattadvarṇavibhedaiśca likhettaṃ pañcaraṅgikam ||

<p. 16> godhūmādīni dhānyāni tattatsthāneṣu pūrayet |
karṣamātrasuvarṇaiśca ardhaṃ vāpi tadardhakam ||

tattadgrahavibhedaiśca pratimāṃ ca prakalpayet |
kumbhaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhāpya grahasaṃkhyāni pārvati ||

sūryādigrahabhedaiśca pūjayecca vidhānataḥ |
tadagre padmamālikhya pūrayecchālitaṇḍulaiḥ ||

likhedyantraṃ sūryabījaṃ dvādaśādityarūpakam |
saṃpūjya vidhinā mantrī pratisūryasya darśane ||

īśānakoṇe saṃlikhya padmamaṣṭadalaṃ priye |
dhānyadroṇamitaṃ pūrya ādhārādi yathākramam ||

suvarṇapratimāṃ kuryāllakṣaṇāni yathākramam |
kandaṃ nālaṃ ca padmaṃ ca diggajaiścāṣṭabhiryutam ||

pūrṇaṃ kūrmaṃ ca dharaṇiṃ kṛtvā sthāpayetpūjayedgurūḥ |
dikpāla paritaḥ pūjya prāgādiṣu yathāvidhi ||

bhūkampe cetpidaṃ pūjya tattannāmādibhiḥ priye |
vedimadhye ca saṃsthāpya sahasraṃ vā tadardhakam ||

<p. 17> kalaśān pūjayenmantrī pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ |
tataḥ kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā hotṛbhissaha deśikaḥ ||

brahmāṇaṃ dhanadaṃ devi upaviśya yathākramam |
kuṇḍasaṃskārapūrvaṃ ca janayetpāvakaṃ priye ||

tadagniṃ vibharenmantrī prāgādiṣu yathākramam |
agnikāryoktavatkuryāttattatkuṇḍeṣu mūrtipāḥ ||

pradhānakuṇḍe juhuyāt mūlamantraṃ maheśvari |
ayutaṃ ca tadardhaṃ vā sahasraṃ vā maheśvari ||

bhūbījaṃ bhuvaneśīṃ ca ādhārādīśca pūrvake |
aghoraṃ cāgnikoṇe ca tadardhārdhaṃ maheśvari ||

mṛtyuñjayaṃ ca yāmye ca nair(-)ṛtyāṃ ca varāhakam |
prāsādaṃ vāruṇe kuṇḍe vāyavyai śāntidaurgakam ||

pañcabrahmā ca some ca īśāne tatvamantrakān |
sahasraṃ ca huneddevi tattanmantraṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||

samidājyacarudravyaiḥ sarṣapaiśca tilaissaha |
vrīhibhissaktubhiścaiva godhūmaiḥ pṛthukairhunet || 

<p. 18> kuṇḍasya parito dikṣu hutasaṃkhyāśca hotṛkān |
vibhajya ca yathā mantrī dinasaṃkhyena pārvati ||

pūrvoktasaṃkhyayā hutvā uddiśya divase guruḥ |
upahomaṃ tataḥ kuryāt rutvigbhissaha deśikaḥ ||

gaṇeśaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ ca triyambakanavagrahān |
dikpālamantrairhutvātha sviṣṭakṛttadanantaram ||

pūrṇāhutiṃ hunedante bhūkampe cetpidaṃ priye |
pratisūrye darśane ca hunenmantrī  yathākramam ||

ādityaṃ pūrvakuṇḍe tu aghoraṃ cāgnigocare |
vāme mṛtyuñjayaṃ hutvā nair(-)ṛtyāṃ ca navagraham ||

vāruṇe dikpatīn mantrān vāyavyeśāntidaurgakām |
some ca dvādaśādityān īśe pañcākṣarīṃ hunet ||

pūrvoktadravyasaṃkhyaiśca hunenmantraṃ pṛthak priye |
homānte sviṣṭakṛnmantrī pūrṇāhutimathācaret ||

sthāpitān kalaśān mantrī secayettava liṅgake |
mahārudrāṃśca vidhivanmahāpañcāmṛtaṃ priye ||

<p. 19> secanānte ca udvartya mahāpūjāṃ samācaret |
nānāpuṣpaiśca gandhaiśca naivedyaṃ bahudhā priye ||

nīrājanaṃ ca saṃbhrāmya upacāraissamarpayet |
daśadānaṃ ca datvātha sūryaṃ svarṇena nirmitam ||

dvādaśa pratimāṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pradāpayet |
bhūcakraṃ kārayeddevi daśaniṣkapramāṇataḥ ||

brāhmaṇāya pradātavyaṃ dakṣiṇābhiḥ samanvitam |
ācāryaṃ pūjayitvātha gobhūvāso hiraṇyakaiḥ ||

tatropakaraṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ pradadyācca maheśvari |
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedbhaktyā mūrtipebhyo yathākramam ||

brāhmaṇān bhojayetpaścāt yathā vibhavavistaram |
bhūmidānaṃ tato dadyāt rātrau dīpotsavaṃ guruḥ ||

bhūtebhyaśca baliṃ datvā kṛcchradānaṃ pradāpayet |
ityevaṃ kārayedbhaktyā bhūkampe ca maheśvari ||

pratisūrye darśane ca śāntiṃ kuryādidaṃ priye |
āyurārogyamaiśvaryaṃ sarvasampatkaraṃ nṛṇām ||

<p. 20> subhikṣaṃ bhavedbhūmau āyurvṛddhikaraṃ nṛṇām |
brahmakṣatriyavaiśyānāṃ svadharmamabhivṛddhaye ||

sarvaprāṇihitaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvāghaughanivāraṇam |
prāyaścittamidaṃ devi tava prītyā mayoditam ||

[iti śrīvātulottare prāyaścittakāṇḍe 
bhūkampādipratisūryadarśanaśāntividhirnāma ekacatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||




ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi indukṣayadinaṃ śive |
niśi nakṣatranāśe ca rātrāvindradhanūtthite ||

divā nakṣatradarśe ca yadi jāte maheśvari |
māṃsau cedadhike nyūnā ubhau prāpte ca vatsare ||

krūrādi grahayoge ca pañcame vātha saptame |
dvātriṃśatketurutpanne rūdhirasrāvapātane ||

vahnivṛṣṭistathā bhūmau yadi jāte maheśvari |

<p. 21> masauce |

doṣaṃ śāntiṃ labhandevi krameṇa śṛṇu pārvati ||

indukṣaye ṛkṣanāśe rātrāvindradhanūtthite |
divā nakṣatradarśe cet yatrakutra sthale śive ||

deśe deśapatiścaiva mantrisenādhipakṣayaḥ |
māsau cedadhike nyūna ubhau prāpte ca vatsare ||

senājano grāmapaśca durbhikṣasyānmaheśvari |
krūrārigrahayoge cet jāte rāśau nṛṇāṃ priye ||

mṛtyubādhā bhavecchīghraṃ ṣaṇmāsāntaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
yadi ketuśca ye dṛṣṭe tvakasmādvividhe śive ||

kṣāmakṣayau bhavedrājye bhūpāmṛtyumṛtiṃ yayuḥ |
vahnivṛṣṭistathā bhūmau rudhirasrāvapātane ||

anāvṛṣṭistathā devi phalanāśaṃ ca sarvataḥ |
sasyanāśaṃ bhavelloke vatsarāṇāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

tasmāttaddoṣaśāntyarthaṃ rājadhānyāṃ tavālaye |
deśe deśe pṛthaggrāme śāntikarma samācaret ||

daśahastamitenādau maṇṭapaṃ kārayedguruḥ |
<p. 22> ekaṃ vā pañcakuṇḍaṃ vā hastatrayasamanvitam ||

lakṣaṇaṃ pūrvavaddevi pramāṇena vicakṣaṇaḥ |
dvārādi bhūṣaṇādīni racayenmaṇṭapaṃ gurūḥ ||

sutithau ca sunakṣatre rājagrāmānukūlake |
tataḥ prāta samutthāya snānasandhyāditarpaṇam ||

samāpya vidhinā mantrī brāhmaṇaissaha bhaktitaḥ |
devālayaṃ praviśyātha vighneśārcanapūrvakam ||

puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca nāndīkarma samācaret |
varayedbhūsurān mantrī ācāryādi yathoktavat ||

alaṅkāraṃ bhūṣaṇādivastrahemāṅgulīyakaiḥ |
tāmbūlaṃ ca phalaṃ datvā madhuparkaṃ yathoktavat ||

aṅkuraṃ tu prakurvīta sadyo vātha maheśvari |
maṇṭapaṃ śodhayedrātrau tatkumbhān sthāpya homayet ||

vāsturākṣoghnahavanaṃ balipūjāṃ samācaret |
vedapārāyaṇaṃ dikṣu vidikṣvastramanuṃ śive ||

kṣetrapālaṃ vāstumantraṃ mṛtyuñjayanavagraham |
<p. 23> mūlamantraṃ diśāmantraṃ bhūsūktaṃ viṣṇusūktakam ||

abhayaṅkaraṃ śāntidurgāprāsādaṃ bhuvaneśvarīm |
saptaśatīn ketumantrān aṣṭau vākyaṃ tadukṣakān ||

japet homāvasānāntaṃ rakṣābandhaṃ samācaret |
tataḥ prāta samutthāya nityakarma vidhāya ca ||

brāhmaṇaissaha saṃyuktaḥ saṃkalpya vidhipūrvakam |
puṇyāhaṃ vācayeddevi svakuṇḍe vidhivadguruḥ ||

jananāgniṃ ca kurvīta brahmāṇaṃ hotṛbhissaha |
tadagniṃ cānyakuṇḍeṣu sthāpayecca yathākramam ||

agnikāryoktavatkuryāt kuṇḍasya paritaḥ pṛthak |
upaviśya yathānyāyyaṃ prayutaṃ mūlamantrakam ||

pradhānakuṇḍe juhuyādaghoraṃ pūrvakuṇḍake |
grahamantraṃ dakṣiṇe ca paścimecābhayaṅkaram ||

triyambakaṃ cottare ca tatanmantraṃ hunet pṛthak |
khadiraṃ ca palāśaṃ ca arkamaudumbaraṃ tathā ||

dūrvā ca samidhaṃ hutvā prādhānādi yathākramam |
<p. 24> ghṛtaṃ caruṃ tilaṃ ceti sarṣapaṃ pṛthukaṃ priye ||

saktuṃ vrīhiṃ ca godhūmaṃ madhukṣīraṃ yathākramam |
pūrvoktasaṅkhyayā hutvā ṛtvigbhiḥ saha deśikaḥ ||

upahomaṃ tato hutvā pradhānādi yathākramam |
śāntidurgāmanuṃ homaṃ tattanmāsarkṣadevatāḥ |

pañcabrahmaṃ ca prāsādaṃ sahasraṃ ca hunetpriye |
sthāpayedvedi madhye tu sahasraṃ cottamaṃ śive ||

tadardhārdhaṃ ca saṃpūjya kalaśān vidhivadguruḥ |
japapārāyaṇaṃ kuryāt uddiśyadivasāntakam ||

pūrṇāhuti hunedante tadghaṭān secayedguruḥ |
śatarudraṃ cātirudraṃ tattatkalpoktamārgataḥ ||

secayettava liṅgaṃ ca mahāpañcāmṛtaiḥ pṛthak |
nānāsūktaiścābhiṣicya tato gandhavilepayet ||

pūjayettava liṅgaṃ ca nānāpatraiḥ prasūnakaiḥ |
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ ṣaḍrasānnaiśca bhakṣakaiḥ ||

phalapakvaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ nīlājanamanantaram |
<p. 25> balidānaṃ tato dadyāt brāhmaṇān bhojayedbahūn ||

rātrau dīpotsavaṃ devi raṅgapūjāṃ samācaret |
mahābhūtabaliṃ dadyāt daśadānaṃ pradāpayet ||

indu(-)ṛkṣādināśe ca tadrūpapratimāṃ priye |
kārayetsvarṇajaṃ dadyāddakṣiṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||

bhūridānaṃ tato dadyāt kṛcchradānaṃ tataḥ param |
ācāryaṃ pūjayedbhaktyā vāhanairbhūṣaṇādikaiḥ ||

gobhūmyādini datvā tu dakṣiṇāṃ ca svaśaktitaḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ ca sadasyaṃ ca hotṛkādi yathākramam ||

tattadaṃśaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ ca dāpayecca pṛthak pṛthak |
ityevaṃ kārayecchāntiṃ sarvamaṅgalaṃ nṛṇām ||

rājāmātyaprajānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vṛddhidaṃ śive |

[iti śrīvātulottare prāyaścittakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
indunakṣatranāśaketudarśanaśāntividhirnāma dvicatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||



śrīdevyuvāca (-)

śrutaṃ deva mayā sarvaṃ nityanaimitikādhikam |
prāyaścittavidhiṃ samyak tvayokto me sadāśiva ||

idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi utpātā bahavo bhuvi |
kathate dudbalaṃ deva kāraṇaṃ vada sāmpratam ||

teṣāṃ nāma kathaṃ deva doṣāṇāṃ ca kathaṃ pṛthak |
tacchāntiśca kathaṃ śambho vadasva kṛpayā śiva ||

īśvara uvāca (-)

sādhu devi tvayā pṛṣṭha utpātā bahavodbhavāḥ |
kathaṃ cedudbhavaṃ devi kāraṇaṃ śṛṇu tatvataḥ ||

kecit sthānavināśāya kecinmṛtyubhayaṃ śive |
kecicchatrubhayaṃ nṝṇāṃ durbhikṣaṃ jāyate bhuvi ||

kecidvasuharaṃ nṝṇāṃ grāme janapurakṣayam |
evamādiṣu kāleṣu utpādyante mayā bhuvi ||

tasmājjñātvā nṛṇāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ taddoṣaśamanāya ca |
śāntiṃ kurvīta vidhivat sarvaprāṇi hitāya ca ||

<p. 27> teṣāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi krameṇa śṛṇu pārvati |
bhūmikampaṃ diśāṃ dāhaṃ pratisūryasya darśanam ||

divā nakṣatramutpannaṃ rātrāvindradhanusthitam |
niśinakṣatranāśe ca indukṣayadine śive ||

māsau cedadhike nyūnā ubhau prāpte ca vatsare |
krūrārigrahayoge ca pañcame vātha saptame ||

dvātriṃśatketurutpanne anyaiśca bahubhiśśive |
vahnivṛṣṭistathā bhūmau rudhirasrāvapātanam ||

divyantarikṣabhūmyāṃ ca jāyate ca maheśvari |
devālaye gṛhe vāpi grāmevā nagare sthale ||

pattane rājadhānyāṃ ca vane copavane'pi vā |
nadītīre taṭāke vā kānane parvate sthale ||

sindhutīre ca diksthāne bhūmyā (**********) |
(**********) rājalakṣmībahirgate ||

tadbimbaṃ ca prayatnena punaḥ prāpte guruḥ priye |
śānti kuryācca saṃpūjya utsavaṃ ca samārabhet ||

<p. 28> yadi nāśādike bimbe yathāpūrvoktalakṣaṇam |
navaṃ bimbaṃ ca kurvīta pratiṣṭhāśāntimācaret ||

tiṣṭhatyūrdhvamukhe bimbe karapādavihīnake |
adhomukhādyanekāni hīnāsyakṛśavāmanāḥ ||

darśanaṃ cedidaṃ bimbaṃ vismayīkṛtya tatkṣaṇāt |
vetālakṣetrapālāśca bhūtabādhā ca vardhate ||

sarpavyādhibhayaṃ rājñaḥ deśe tatra prājāyate |
taddoṣaśamanārthāya prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ||

tavaliṅgādibimbe ca bimbadoṣādikān kramāt |
samyaguktaṃ mayā devi tacchāntiṃ vistarācchṛṇu |

bhinnadoṣādike prāpte yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ |
ācāryaṃ bhojayetpūrvaṃ pāyasena ghṛtena ca ||

hemaratnādikaṃ tasya dātavyaṃ vāhanādikam |
maṇṭapaṃ devadakṣasthe kalaśaṃ pūrvavadyajet ||

homaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena saṃhāroktena deśikaḥ |
liṅgamabhyarcya santarpya tritatvaṃ ca visarjayet ||

<p. 29> dvāramācchādya vastreṇa phalapakkaṃ nivedayet |
tataḥ sandhāya pūrvoktaṃ liṅgaṃ vā bimbameva vā ||

prāyaścittaṃ prakartavyaṃ guruḥ kuryānmaheśvari |
tavāgre ceśabhāge ca uttamottamamaṇṭapam ||

navakuṇḍaṃ prakalpyātha yathāpūrvoktamārgataḥ |
racayenmaṇṭapaṃ devi dvāratoraṇabhūṣaṇaiḥ ||

sutithau ca sunakṣatre suyogakaraṇānvite |
prāyaścittaṃ prakurvīta sadyovāthamaheśvari ||

tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya brāhmaṇaiḥ saha deśikaḥ |
nityakarmāṇi nirvartya mṛttikāsnānapūrvakam ||

dhautavastrāmbaracchannaḥ vibhūtyādyairalaṅkṛtaḥ |
devālayaṃ tato gatvā prārthayecca maheśvari ||

upaviśyāsane mantrī pūrve vā caiśakoṇake |
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryānmātṛkādi yathākramam ||

nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tato mantrī guruvandanapūrvakam |
dinamuddiśya saṃkalpya pañcasapta navāhakam |

<p. 30> mamātmajaṃ prapūjyātha nāndīpuṇyāhamācaret |
varayedbhūsurān devi pūrvoktalakṣaṇairyutān ||

guruṃ vidhiṃ ca dhanadaṃ sadasyaṃ cāṣṭamūrtipān |
homasaṅkhyamitān devi hotṛbhiśca yathoktavat ||

japārthaṃ ca tadardhaṃ syāt vedapāṭhārthaṣoḍaśān |
parārthaṃ daśa viprāṃśca rudrasaṃkhyāṃśca secakān ||

pūjārthaṃ daśaviprāṃśca namaskārapradakṣiṇe |
ṣaḍjanān varayenmantrī madhuparkaṃ ca dāpayet ||

vastrālaṅkārakaṃ dadyāt phalatāmbūlameva vā |
aṅkurārpaṇapūrvaṃ ca mṛttikāharaṇaṃ guruḥ ||

dhvajārohe tataḥ paścāt navarātrau maheśvari |
eka tripañcarātrau tu dhvajaṃ vā pūjayedguruḥ ||

sāyaṃ sandhyāmupāsyātha yāgagehaṃ praviśya ca |
puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca kuṇḍamaṇḍapaśodhanam ||

vidyeśādighaṭān sthāpya dvāratoraṇakumbhakam |
taṇḍulāḍhyaṃ jalāḍhyaṃ ca rākṣoghnaṃ vāstuhomakam ||

<p. 31> baliṃ ca vikiredvāsto rakṣabandhakriyāṃ nayet |
sthānaśuddhighaṭān nyastvā bimbaśuddhiṃ tathaiva ca ||

liṅgaśuddhiṃ ghaṭān sthāpya pūjayedvidhinā guruḥ |
vedapārāyaṇaṃ dikṣu vidikṣustamanuṃ japet ||

sarvamantrān japeddevi tattatsūktāṃśca mantrataḥ |
adhivāsaṃ ca kurvīta yathā pūrvoktavadguruḥ ||

liṅgaśuddhiṃ tataḥ paścāt pūrvoktadravyamantrakaiḥ |
vinidrāṃstu tataḥ kṛtvā tatpare prātarutthitaḥ ||

snānasandhyākriyā nītvā puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ |
deśikāgniṃ samānīya prādhāne ca nyaset priye ||

tadagniṃ janayenmantrī hotṛbhirjāpakādikaiḥ |
vedapārāyaṇaṃ sārdhaṃ homakarmasamācaret ||

mūlamantrapradhānaṃ syāt lakṣaṃ vātha tadardhakam |
pūrvādikuṇḍe juhuyāt kalāvṛddhiṃ ca tatvakam ||

śāntidurgāṃ mṛtyujitaṃ tadardhaṃ ca yathākramam |
vidikṣu kuṇḍe juhuyāt caturastraṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||

<p. 32> samidājyādikairmantrī daśadravyeṇa homayet |
upahomaṃ tataḥ paścāt pradhānādi yathākramam ||

liṅgaśuddhiṃ bimbaśuddhiṃ sthānaśuddhimanuṃ priye |
prāsādaṃ ca diśāmantraṃ grahahomaṃ ca mantravit ||

mūrtihomaṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca kṣetrapālamanuṃ śive |
sahasraṃ ca tadardhaṃ vā sarpiṣā juhuyāt pṛthak ||

japapārāyaṇaṃ kuryāt tattanmantrān bahūn priye |
sthāpayet kalaśān mantrī sahasraṃ ca yathoktavat ||

pūjayecca tataḥ paścāt spṛṣṭenmūlamanuṃ priye |
uddiśya divasāntaṃ ca hotṛbhiśca pṛthak pṛthak ||

pūrvoktasaṃkhyayā hutvā tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ hunet |
homānte tava liṅge ca kalāṃ saṃyojya deśikaḥ ||

sthāpitaṃ jīvakumbhaṃ ca secayenmantravittamaḥ |
nirīkṣākarma kartavyaṃ mukhavastraṃ visarjayet ||

tataḥ pañcāmṛtairliṅgaṃ secayedrudramantrataḥ |
nānāsūktaṃ pāvamānaiḥ tattanmantreṇa pārvati ||

<p. 33> snāpayetkalaśairmantrī sahasraiśca yathākramam |
secanānte tadudvṛtya aṣṭagandhaṃ vilepayet ||

mahāpūjāṃ ca kurvīta nānāpuṣpaiśca nāmabhiḥ |
bilvapatrairdrāṇapuṣpaiḥ kamalaiśca maheśvari ||

svarṇena nirmitaiḥ kuryāt pūjayettavaliṅgake |
naivedyaṃ bahudhāpānnaṃ bhakṣabhojyaiśca pāyasaiḥ ||

nivedayettato mantrī mūlamantreṇa pārvati |
tāmbūlaṃ ca samarpyātha nīrājanamanantaram ||

balidānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt utsavaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
dine dine prakartavyaṃ uddiśya divasāntakam ||

daśadānaṃ tato dadyāt liṅgaṃ svarṇamayaṃ priye |
bimbaṃ vātha prakurvīta dakṣiṇāṃ ca svaśaktitaḥ ||

dadyācca gurave bhaktyā kṛcchradānaṃ pradāpayet |
brāhmaṇān bhojayeddevi yathā cāhutisaṃkhyayā ||

dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedante ācāryādi yathoktavat |
bhūmidānaṃ tato dadyāt gurupūjāṃ samācaret ||

<p. 34> gobhūmyādīni datvā tu tantropakaraṇaissaha |
ityevaṃ kārayecchāntiṃ liṅganāśādike priye ||

bhinne ca sphuṭite dagdhe khaṇḍite yatrakutracit |
tadardhārdhaṃ ca kurvīta bimbe vātha maheśvari ||

rodanādiṣu doṣeṣu kīṭakādi nidarśane |
karapādavihīne tu darśane kṛśavāmane ||

patanādiṣu doṣeṣu tasyārdhaṃ ca tadardhakam |
śāntiṃ kurvīta pūrvoktaṃ sarvadoṣanivāraṇam ||

sarvaśāntikaraṃ loke sarvasampatkaraṃ nṛṇām |
caturaṅgābhivṛddhiḥ syāt rājño rājyābhivardhanam ||

putrapautrādi vṛddhiśca sarvatra vijayo bhavet |
sarvamṛtyuharaṃ śīghraṃ āyurvṛddhikaraṃ priye ||

sannidhānaṃ bhavelliṅge sarvābhīṣṭapradaṃ śive |
garbhāgārādi vakṣyāmi doṣaṃ śāntiṃ kramācchṛṇu ||

garbhagṛhe maṇṭape ca prākāre gopure tathā |
prāsādādiṣu sarvatra dahane sphuṭite sati ||

<p. 35> chinne bhinne khaṇḍite ca prāveśe maraṇe sati |
vāhane parivārāṇāṃ dhvaje vā kalaśe yadi ||

balipīṭhe kṣetrapāladvārapālau maheśvari |
dīpastambhe cordhvamālāśchatracāmarakādiṣu ||

śibike vāhanādīnāṃ pāduke vādyakādiṣu |
patite khaṇḍite dagdhe corairapahṛte sati ||

evamādi samutpanne rājārāṣṭrabhayaṃ bhavet |
garbhagṛhe ca saṃprāpte rāṣṭranāśaṃ janakṣayaḥ ||

maṇṭape gopure devi trimāsān sacivakṣayaḥ |
prāsādādiṣu saṃprāpte senānāyakanāśanaḥ ||

vārnane parivāreṣu yadi prāpte maheśvari |
coramāribhayaṃ tatra duṣṭabādhā ca vardhate ||

vastuvāhananaśyanti trivarṣāntaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
dhvaje vā kalaśe bhaṅge balipīṭhe pramādataḥ ||

kṣetrapāle dvārapāle dīpastambhādike priye |
śatrubādhādbhavetsadyaḥ bhūtabādhā ca vardhate ||

<p. 36> na sannidhyaṃ bhavelliṅge tatsthānaṃ nāśamāpnuyāt |
chatracāmarasevādi śibike pādukādiṣu ||

balidīpe vādyakādi patite khaṇḍite sati |
rājñāṃ bhogavināśaḥ syāt gajāśvapaśunāśanam ||

sarvabhaṅgaṃ bhavetsadyaḥ grāmalakṣmīrbahirgatā |
evaṃ doṣādike prāpte śāntiṃ pūrvoktamācaret ||

aghoraṃ niyutaṃ hutvā mūlamantraṃ ca pārvati |
diśāhomaṃ śāntidurgāmṛtyuñjayamanuṃ guruḥ ||

tadardhārdhaṃ ca hutvātha yathādoṣabalābalam |
sahasrārdhaṃ ghaṭaṃ mantrī tadardhārdhaṃ ca secayet ||

dānaṃ ca vividhaṃ dadyāt sarvaṃ pūrvoktamācaret |
śāntikarmamayetyuktaṃ liṅgabimbādināśane ||

sarvasampatpradaṃ nṝṇāṃ sarvadā maṅgalapradam |
deśakośābhivṛddhiḥ syāt mama loke vasecciram ||

[iti śrīvātulottare prāyaścittakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
liṅgabimbādidoṣaśānti sandhānavidhirnāmatricatvāriṃśat paṭalaḥ] ||




| svastu |

<p. 37> rathabhaṅgapravakṣyāmi kāmye naimittike yadi |
vatsarādyutsavādīnāṃ ādimadhyāvasānake ||

śakaṭādirathe bhinne pathite śithile sati |
dagdhe naṣṭe khaṇḍite ca tadaṅge ca maheśvari ||

tavotsave cādbhute ca yadi prāpte pramādataḥ |
doṣaṃ śāntiṃ ca sandhānaṃ tatphalaṃ śṛṇu pārvati ||

cakre hīne tvalakṣmī syāt tadgrāmaṃ nāśanaṃ bhavet |
rājñā daṇḍe balaṃ hanti dhananāśaṃ ca pārvati ||

tiryadgāṇḍe ca sacivaṃ nāśanaṃ paṭṭikā bhavet |
mūrtiyukpalake stambhe svarājyaṃ pattanakṣayam ||

pāntarāle ca madhye vā yadi namre ca pārvati |
rogabādhā ca tadrājñaḥ svastrīnāśanamāpnuyāt ||

rathadvārādike nāśe rājalakṣmī bahirgatā |
rathabhittau vihīnettanantale ca vihīnake ||

durbhikṣaṃ prāpnuyussarve mṛtyubādhāṃ ca pārvati |
<p. 38> śalāke rajjubandhe ca senānāyakamantriṇām ||

nāśanaṃ ca bhavenmṛtyuḥ baliberasthale śive |
bhaume cāntaramadhye vā khaṇḍite mriyate'pi vā ||

ākuñcite vā patane tadrājā mṛtyumāpnuyāt |
balibimbe ca patane deśikādijane sati ||

deśanāśo bhavetsadyaḥ caturaṅgabalakṣayaḥ |
rājadānī vinaśyeta śatrubādhā bhavennṛṇām ||

chatre caiva patāke vā alaṅkāravihīnake |
yaśaḥ kīrtyādikaṃ hanti rājabhogaṃ ca naśyati ||

rathāgrakalaśe bhinne rathāgre pātite sati |
tadrājñaḥ sarvabhaṅgatvaṃ daridratvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

trimāsānmṛtyumāpnoti rājalakṣmīrbahirgatā |
rajjucchede dhvaje bhaṅge tajjanena nipātite ||

jayābhigamanaṃ nāsti bandhunāśaṃ ca pārvati |
sarvāṅge śithile jāte sarvārthaṃ naśyati dhruvam ||

rathe vā patite devi khaṇḍite bhūtale yadi |
<p. 39> tadrājamaraṇaṃ sadyaḥ caturaṅgabalakṣayaḥ ||

tadrāṣṭraṃ nāśamāpnoti trivarṣāntaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
dikpālaṃ tu tato vakṣye patite ca maheśvari ||

sārvabhaumakṣayaprāpyāṃ gehāgneyyāṃ ca bhūpatiḥ |
yame kṣayaścakravarti maṇḍalikastathā sure ||

prabhukṣayaśca vāruṇyāṃ vāyavyāṃ prabhusārathiḥ |
some mṛtirmaṇḍalikaḥ īśe bhūpasatikṣayaḥ ||

tasmācchāntiṃ prakartavyā sarvalokahitāya ca |
sarvadoṣaharaṃ nṝṇāṃ śāntiṃ kuryānnṛpottamaḥ ||

rathabhaṅgaṃ tadā rājñaḥ darśayenna kadācana |
ācāryaḥ śilpibhiścaiva saśiṣyaiścārakādibhiḥ ||

rathabhaṅgaṃ tadā dṛṣṭvā punastatraiva sandhayet |
sudṛḍhaṃ śilpibhiḥ kuryāt (**********) ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra vighneśārcanapūrvakam |
cakrādimantraṃ juhuyāt sāṣṭottaraśatāhutim ||

ghoramantraṃ ca tasyārdhaṃ mūlamantraṃ ca pārvati |
<p. 40> prāyaścittaṃ ca śāntiṃ ca diśāmantraṃ hunedguruḥ ||

samidājyacarudravyai tuṭikaissarṣapaissaha |
pṛthaṅmantreṇa hotavyaṃ tadaṅge śithile guruḥ ||

snāpayedghaṭatoyena sāṣṭottaraśataṃ guruḥ |
bhinnasthānaṃ ca tadbimbaṃ snapayecca maheśvari ||

tadrathaṃ snapayetpaścāttadardhaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak |
pūjayecca tato mantrī vedapārāyaṇādikaiḥ ||

daśadhā dāpayeddānaṃ kṛcchradānaṃ maheśvari |
rathasya parito dikṣu praśastāścānnarāśayaḥ ||

kūśmāṇḍaśakalaissārdhaṃ tato bhūtabaliṃ haret |
lāṅgulyādisphalaistāḍya śeṣaṃ cotsavamācaret ||

mahāśāntiṃ tataḥ kuryāt vidhānaṃ na gurūttamaḥ |
tadrathaṃ yadi sandhānaṃ na kṛte cettadā śive ||

tava bimba cāvaropya svastisūktena deśikaḥ |
śibikādyaissamāropya utsavaṃ ca samācaret ||

śeṣaṃ karma samāpyātha navaṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhaṃ ratham |
<p. 41> yathā pūrvoktavatkuryāt trimāse saptame śive ||

tadūrdhve vatsare māse tadṛkṣe tattithāvapi |
bījakarmādi tīrthāntaṃ punarūtsavamācaret ||

mahāśāntiṃ ca pūrvedyuḥ guruḥ kuryādvidhānataḥ |
rathotsavaṃ tataḥ kuryāt dhvajārohādipūrvakam ||

mahāśāntiṃ pravakṣyāmi vistarācchṛṇu pārvati |
tavāgre maṇṭapaṃ kuryāt īśānye vātha deśikaḥ ||

dvātriṃśaddhastaparyantaṃ caturaśraṃ samantataḥ |
hastamātraṃ connataṃ syāt madhye vediṃ salakṣaṇām ||

dvātriṃśastambhasaṃyuktāṃ caturdvāraṃ prakalpayet |
daṇḍaṃ saṃsthāpya kudalīṃ toraṇaiścāpyalaṅkṛtam ||

dvihastasaṃmitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ caturaśraṃ caturdiśi |
vidikṣu vṛttakuṇḍaṃ syāt vedikoparikalpayet ||

indracaiśānayormadhye vṛttakuṇḍaṃ pradhānakam |
kuṇḍanābhisamāyuktaṃ mekhalopari śobhitam ||

ityevaṃ maṇṭapaṃ ramyaṃ śāntiṃ kuryānmaheśvari |
<p. 42> etaddineṣu niścitya satyavādī jitendriyaḥ ||

dīkṣāvān karmaniṣṭhajjñaḥ viprānāhūya deśikaḥ |
prātaḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta nityakarma samāpya ca ||

tato devālayaṃ gatvā phalapuṣpaṃ samarpya ca |
prārthayecca tato mantrī śāntiṃ kuryāttavājñayā ||

tataḥ karmāṇi kurvīta ācāryādi dvijaissaha |
niśi sāyaṃ divā prātaḥ karmārambhaṃ samācaret ||

maṇṭapaṃ ca praviśyātha āsane copaviśya ca |
guruvandanapūrvādi prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret ||

dinamuddiśya saṃkalpya yathāvibhavavistaram |
vighneśamarcanāndyādi puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyakam ||

madhuparkaṃ ca varayedācāryaṃ yajamānakam |
brahmaṇaṃ mūrtipān devi yathā cāhutisaṃkhyayā ||

dinasaṅkhyamitaṃ jñātvā varayedvṛtvijān kramāt |
vedapārāyaṇārthaṃ tu ṣoḍaśabrāhmaṇān śive ||

japārthaṃ ca śatārdhaṃ syāt sevanārthaṃ ca pārvati |
<p. 43> rudrasaṅkhyān pūjanārthaṃ daśaviprān pṛthak pṛthak ||

pradakṣiṇa namaskārān ṣaḍghanāṃśca maheśvari |
cārārthaṃ daśaviprāṃśca pratyekaṃ varayedguruḥ ||

vastraṃ ca bhūṣaṇaṃ datvā phalatāmbūlameva vā |
deśikaṃ makuṭādyaiśca svarṇayajñopavītakaiḥ ||

dukūlapādukādyaiśca chatravyajanapātrakaiḥ |
suvarṇakuṇḍalaṃ caiva aṅgulīyaṃ maheśvari ||

datvā vibhavasāreṇa deśikaṃ tu samarcayet |
maṇṭapaṃ śodhayetpaścāt saṃmārjya vipragomayaiḥ ||

devālaye vitānādyaiḥ toraṇaiścāpyalaṅkṛtam |
darbhadāmnā ca tānveṣṭya saṃskāraṃ ca daśāṣṭakān ||

maṇṭapāntasthakumbhānāṃ sthāpayetpūjayedguruḥ |
aṅkuraṃ tu prakurvīta sadyo vātha maheśvari ||

vāsturākṣoghnahavanaṃ balipūjāṃ samārabhet |
rakṣāsūtraṃ bandhayitvā ācāryādi dvijān pṛthak ||

vedapārāyaṇaṃ kuryāt tattanmantraṃ japetpriye |
<p. 44> rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā nirāhāro jitendriyaḥ ||

tataḥ prātassamutthāya nityakarma samāpya ca |
puṇyāhaṃ ca tataḥ kuryātkalaśān sādhayetpriye ||

sahasraṃ cottamaṃ devi pūjayecca yathāvidhi |
jananāgniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya gurukuṇḍasthapāvake ||

viharedanyakuṇḍeṣu agnikāryoktamācaret |
agnimadhye samāvāhya tava mūlena pārvati ||

aghoraṃ pūrvakuṇḍe tu āgneyyāṃ ca sudarśanam |
mṛtyuñjayaṃ dakṣiṇe syāt pañcadurgāṃśca rākṣase ||

devyāstraṃ vāruṇe devi prāsādaṃ ca mamātmakam |
vāyavyakuṇḍemāvāhya some tattvaṃ kalāṃ hunet ||

īśānye śāntidurgāṃ ca tattanmantreṇa pārvati |
aṣṭamūrtīssamāvāhya mūrtipāśca tato hunet ||

ghoramantraṃ cāyutaṃ syāt mūlamantraṃ tathaiva ca |
tasyārdhārdhaṃ ca juhuyāt tattatkuṇḍe ca pārvati ||

gaṇeśakṣetrapālāṃśca dikpālāṃśca hunet pṛthak |
<p. 45> navagrahān mūrtimantrān rathadevān hunedguruḥ ||

samidājyacarudravyaiḥ truṭikaissarṣapaissaha |
godhūmaṃ ca pṛthak lājān saktuṃ vrīhiṃ ca pārvati ||

tattanmantreṇa hotavyaṃ uddeśatyāgapūrvakam |
pūrṇāhutiṃ hunedante uddiśya divase guruḥ ||

snapayedghaṭasāhastraiḥ rudramantraiśca sūktakaiḥ |
mahāpūjāṃ prakūrvīta bahudhā ca nivedayet ||

balidānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt daśadānaṃ pradāpayet |
brāhmaṇān bhojayetpaścāt yathā cāhutisaṅkhyayā ||

kumbhasaṃkhyāthavā devi bhakṣabhojyairyathepsitam |
bhinnasthānaṃ ca tadgātrau saṃhāroktena mārgataḥ ||

rathapūjāṃ ca hotavyaṃ snapanaṃ kārayetpriye |
taddevāṃśca visṛjyātha mahābhūtabaliṃ haret ||

svarṇaṃ vā rājataṃ vātha rathaṃ kṛtvā tu dāpayet |
dānāni buhudhā datvā bhūridānaṃ ca kārayet ||

mahotsavaṃ tataḥ kuryādācāryaṃ pūjayetpriye |
<p. 46> gobhūmyādīni datvātha draviṇaṃ ca yathepsitam ||

tatropakaraṇaṃ devi savastrapratimānvitam |
kalaśān maṇṭapaṃ devi dāpayetpūrvakaṃ ratham ||

deśikaṃ toṣayeddevi sarvadoṣanivāraṇam |
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedbhaktyā karma kuryācca bhūsurān ||

śāntikarma samāpyātha tava liṅge gurūttamaḥ |
itthaṃ kurvīta yo bhaktyā rathabhaṅge ca pārvati ||

sarvadoṣaharaṃ nṝṇāṃ sarvāriṣṭanivāraṇam |
cirāyuṣyaṃ mudaṃ bhūpo putrapautrasamanvitaḥ ||

sthirāṃ lakṣmīmavāpnoti caturaṅgabalānvitaḥ |
bhuktvā ca vipulān bhogān kīrtimān dhanavān bhavet ||

rathabhaṅgetvidaṃ devi prāyaścitamidaṃ śubham |

[iti śrīvātulottare prāyaścittakāṇḍe rathabhaṅgaprāyaścitta 
nirūpaṇavidhirnāma catuścatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||




<p. 47> ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi yathākramam |
vāhanārthaṃ gajāśvādyaiḥ vṛṣabhaiścāpyalaṅkṛtaiḥ ||

etatprāṇivirodhena patite raktapātane |
nakhadantakṣate śṛṅgī ghāte yadi mṛte sati ||

yadi vighne ca saṃprāpte nityabalyādi cotsave |
pakṣe māsotsave devi ayane vatsarotsavaiḥ ||

ādimadhyāvasāne tu yadi prāpte maheśvari |
bahudhā śatrubādhā syādanāvṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati ||

durbhikṣaṃ saṃbhavelloke mahāmāribhayaṃ nṛṇām |
nidhanaṃ prāpnuyādrājā rājalakṣmīrbahirgatā ||

na sānnidhyaṃ bhavelliṅge yadi naṣṭe ca pārvati |
tasmāttaddoṣa śāntyarthaṃ mahāśāntiṃ samācaret ||

na kṛte karmabāhulye tatkāle śāntimācaret |
karmārambhāvasānāntaṃ śeṣaṃ cotsavamācaret ||

tadūrdhvamāse saṃprāpte tadṛkṣe tattithāvapi |
trimāse pañcame vāpi saptame navame śive ||

<p. 48> mahāśāntiṃ ca kurvīta vidhānaṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
gururdevāgrataḥ kuryāt uttamottamamaṇṭapam ||

savedikāsamāyuktaṃ sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam |
dvihastaṃ saṃmitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ sayoninābhisaṃyutam ||

vedikāyā madhyamasya paritaḥ kuṇḍakalpanam |
caturaśraṃ tu vṛttaṃ vā pradhānaṃ parikīrtitam ||

caturdikṣu prakurvīta caturaśraṃ ca kuṇḍakam |
trimekhalā samāyuktaṃ gurūstallakṣaṇoktavat ||

dvijānāhvāpayedrātrau vighneśārcanapūrvakam |
nāndīmukhaṃ ca puṇyāhaṃ madhuparkasamanvitam ||

varayedbrāhmaṇān sarvān ācāryādīn pṛthak pṛthak |
sadyo'ṅkuraṃ prakartavyaṃ kuṇḍamaṇṭapaśodhanam ||

vāsturākṣoghnahavanaṃ baliṃ vāstossamācaret |
vidyeśādi ghaṭānyaṣṭvā toraṇaṃ gaḍḍukādikam ||

sthāpanaṃ ca tataḥ kuryādrakṣābandhaṃ samācaret |
sthānaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kuryāttatsthāne ca maheśvari ||

<p. 49> khananaṃ haraṇaṃ dāhaṃ pūraṇaṃ gonivāsanam |
viprocchiṣṭaṃ ca gavyaṃ ca sthānaśuddhiriyaṃ priye ||

saṃmārjanopalepaṃ ca vikiretsarvapāṭikān |
rakṣābandhaṃ tataḥ kuryāddarbhadāmnā ca veṣṭayet ||

vāstoṣpateti mantrābhyāṃ pāvamānamudīrayet |
bhūsūktabrahmasuktābhyāṃ tatsthānaṃ prokṣayedguruḥ ||

vedapārāyaṇaṃ kuryājjapenmantraṃ yathoktavat |
tataḥ prātassamutthāya nityakarma samāpya ca ||

vighneśamarcya puṇyāhaṃ saṅkalpavidhipūrvakam |
dinamuddiśya saṅkalpya homakarma samārabhet ||

brahmāṇaṃ ca sadasyaṃ ca śatārdhān hotṛkā priye |
nānāsūktādi mantrajñān daśapañca maheśvari ||

caturo vedapāṭhārthaṃ caturdikṣu yathākramam |
parārthaṃ ṣaḍghanān devi rudrasaṃkhyaṃśca secakān ||

pūjārthaṃ daśaviprāṃśca namaskārapradakṣiṇe |
ekamekaṃ pṛthak mantrī brāhmaṇaiśca samanvitaḥ ||

<p. 50> yāgagehe copaviśya āsaneṣu yathākramam |
pradhāne madhyame kuṇḍe hotṛbhirdaśabhissaha ||

dhanadena sadasyena brahmaṇā saha deśikaḥ |
kuṇḍasaṃskārapūrvaṃ ca janayetpāvakaṃ śive ||

aghoreṇāyutaṃ homaṃ uddeśatyāgapūrvakam |
pratyekaṃ daśabhirvipraiḥ caturdikṣu pṛthak pṛthak ||

homayenmanunā devi ṛtvigbhiḥ saha deśikaḥ |
udumbaraśamīdūrvā khadiraṃ ca palāśajam ||

tattatkuṇḍeṣu juhuyātpradhānādi yathākramam |
ghṛtaṃ caru tilaṃ caiva sarṣapaṃ tuṭikaṃ tathā ||

marīcaṃ jīrakaṃ lājaṃ pṛthukaṃ ca hunetpṛthak |
pūrvoktasaṃkhyayā hutvā anyamantran yathākramam ||

mṛtyuñjayamanuṃ pūrve dakṣe pāśupataṃ manum |
śāntidurgā paścime ca mūlamantramantraṃ tu cottare ||

pratyekamayutaṃ caiva kuṇḍe kuṇḍe yathākramam |
homayenmanunā devi japeyurbrāhmaṇottamāḥ ||

<p. 51> navagrahaṃ vāstuśāntiṃ rākṣoghnaṃ bhuvaneśvarīm |
caturastraṃ kṣetrapālaṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca sarasvatīm ||

mṛtyuñjayaṃ ca prāsādaṃ saptaśatyādikaṃ japet |
snapayedghaṭasāhasraiḥ tadardhairvā maheśvari ||

pūrvoktenaiva saṃpūjya savastrapratimādikaiḥ |
auṣadhaṃ ca vinikṣipya mūlamantraṃ japetpriye ||

homānte secayeddevīṃ rudramantraiśca sūktakaiḥ |
liṅgaṃ pañcāmṛtaiḥ snāpya gandhairālipyadeśikaḥ ||

pūjayedbahudhā mantrī naivedyaṃ bahudhā śive |
valyutsavaṃ tataḥ paścādgobhūmyādīni dāpayet ||

gajāśvavṛṣabhaṃ gatvā punassandhānamācaret |
dakṣiṇāṃ ca tato dadyādyathā vibhavavistaram ||

brāhmaṇān bhojayet paścāt sahasraṃ ca maheśvari |
deśikaṃ toṣayedante tatropakaraṇaissaha ||

śāntikarmamayetyuktaṃ sarvadoṣanivāraṇam |
sarvapāpaharaṃ devi subhikṣaṃ tatkṣaṇādbhavet ||

<p. 52> rājyadurgādivṛddhissyāccaturaṅgabalapadam |
sarvaśatruharaṃ sadyaḥ sthirāṃ lakṣmīmavāpnuyāt ||

[iti śrīvātulottare prāyaścittakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
gajāśvavṛṣabhādiśāntividhirnāma pañcacatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||



athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi śastradhāranṛṇāṃ priye |
senānāyakabhṛtyānāmanyeṣāṃ samavāyinām ||

parasparavirodhena yuddhe jāte maheśvari |
muṣṭiyuddhe daṇḍayuddhe nakhadantakṣate sati ||

pāṣāṇairāyudhādyaiśca kṣate jāte maheśvari |
patite raktajāte ca manuje ca mṛte yadi ||

vighne jāte tatkāle nityanaimittike'pi vā |
utsave balikāle vā doṣaṃ śāntiṃ śṛṇu priye ||

muṣṭiyuddhe mahārogaṃ daṇḍayuddhe kulakṣayam |
nakhadantakṣaye devi durbhikṣaṃ jāyate bhuvi ||

<p. 53> pāṣāṇasaṃbhave yuddhe prajā nāśatvamāpnuyāt |
anāvṛṣṭirathānyatvaṃ śoṇite patite yadi ||

kṣate jāte manuṣyāṇāṃ mānino bhaṅgamāpnuyuḥ |
maraṇaṃ ca yadi prāpte rājā maraṇamāpnuyāt ||

tava liṅgena sānnidhyaṃ caturaṅgabalakṣayam |
śriyamāyurharecchīghraṃ grāmantaṃ nāśamāpnuyāt ||

taddoṣaśamanārthāya prāyaścittaṃ vadāmyaham |
pūrvoktamaṇṭapaṃ kuryānnavakuṇḍaṃ ca pañca vā ||

salakṣaṇaṃ ca kurvīta dvāratoraṇabhūṣitam |
tavālayaṃ ca saṃbhūṣya kṣālayedgomayodakaiḥ ||

tataḥ prātassamutthāya nityakarma samāpya ca |
ādau gaṇapatiṃ pūjya nāndīkarma tataḥ param ||

puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca prokṣayenmanunā guruḥ |
varayedbhūsurān devi pūrvoktadvijasaṃkhyayā ||

madhuparkaṃ ca datvātha śodhayenmaṇṭapo guruḥ |
dvāratoraṇakumbhāṃśca pūjayetsvasvanāmabhiḥ ||

<p. 54> vedapārāyaṇaṃ kuryāccaturastraṃ japetpriye |
vāsturakṣoghnahavanaṃ balipūjāṃ samācaret ||

dehaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kuryāttatsthānaṃ śodhayedguruḥ |
khananādi viśuddhiṃ ca saṃmārjya prokṣaṇādibhiḥ ||

bhūsūktaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca viṣṇusūktaṃ ca pārvati |
vāstusūktena vikiretsarṣapañcāstramantrataḥ ||

rakṣābandhaṃ tataḥ kuryādadhivāsanamārabhet |
pūrvoktena vidhānena tattatkuṇḍāṃśca homayet ||

bhūbījaṃ ca tato hutvā aṣṭottaraśataṃ priye |
bhūtebhyaśca baliṃ datvā gehasya parito guruḥ ||

nṛttagītasamāyuktaṃ punassūryodayāntikam |
punaḥ prātasamutthāya nityakarma samāpya ca ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācayetpaścālliṅgaśuddhiṃ samācaret |
bimbaprāsādaśuddhiṃ ca tattanmantraiśca dravyakaiḥ ||

śodhayecca tato mantrī yāgagehaṃ praviśya ca |
brahmaṇā dhanadenātha hotṛbhiśca yathoktavat ||

<p. 55> upaviśya yathānyāyaṃ pratikuṇḍe maheśvari |
jananāgniṃ ca kurvīta viharedanyakuṇḍake ||

homayenmūlamantreṇa ayutaṃ ca maheśvari |
ādhāraṃ pūrvakuṇḍe tu āgneyyāṃ tu tavāstrakam ||

yāme mṛtyuñjayaṃ caiva diśāhomaṃ tu rākṣase |
prāsādaṃ vāruṇe devi vāyavye śāntidaurgakām ||

kalāvṛddhiṃ saumyadeśe pañcabrahmāṇamīśake |
homayecca pṛthaṅmantrī yathāpūrvoktasaṅkhyayā ||

pūrvoktadravyahomāñca upahomaṃ ca pūrvavat |
pūrṇāhutiṃ hunedante snapayetkalaśaiḥ priye ||

sahasrairvidhivanmantrī tadardhārdhena vā śive |
pañcāmṛtaṃ droṇamitaṃ kṣīrādidravyapañcakam ||

phalapañcāmṛtaṃ devi mahārudrairyathāvidhi |
anyasūktaiśca saṃsnāpya udvartyāmalakaiśśive ||

aṣṭagandhaṃ samālipya nānāpuṣpaiśca pūjayet |
śālyānnaṃ pañcabhakṣādyaiḥ naivedyaṃ ca samarpayet ||

<p. 56> nīrājanaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ prasannayajanaṃ caret |
dānaṃ daśavidhaṃ datvā yathāśaktyā bahūn priye ||

tilaṃ ca puruṣaṃ kṛtvā svarṇaṃ vātha maheśvari |
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedbhaktyā yathāvibhavavistaram ||

balidānaṃ cotsavaṃ ca brāhmaṇān bhojayedbahūn |
rātrau dīpotsavaṃ kuryādbhūtebhyaśca baliṃ haret ||

kriyāsamarpaṇaṃ devi kṛcchradānaṃ ca dāpayet |
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedbhaktyā ācāryādiyathākramam ||

ityevaṃ kārayeddevi sarvadoṣaharaṃ nṛṇām |
putrapautrādisampattissarvatra vijayo bhavet ||

sarvamṛtyuharaṃ śīghraṃ āyurvṛddhikaraṃ muhuḥ |
tava liṅge ca sānnidhyaṃ sarvaprāṇisukhaṃ bhavet ||

kṣate jāte nṛṇāṃ devi nāśanaṃ ca midaṃ mahat |
catuṣpadāñca dvipadāṃ vanyānāṃ duṣṭajīvinām ||

praveśe maraṇe prāpte pūrvavaddoṣasaṃyute |
prāyaścittaṃ tadardhaṃ vā yathā doṣabalābalam ||

<p. 57> śāntiṃ kurvīta mantrajñaḥ yathāpūrvoktamārgataḥ |

[iti śrīvātulottare prāyaścittakāṇḍe 
dvipadacatuṣpadapraveśamaraṇamuṣṭiyuddhavirodhaśāntividhirnāma 
ṣaṭcatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||



ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pattanādigṛheṣu ca |
utpāte codbhave devi prāyaścittaṃ śṛṇu priye ||

pattane rājadhānyāṃ ca tava kṣetre ca durgake |
rājñāṃ gehe cānyagṛhe dvijādīnāṃ maheśvari ||

anaḍvān mahiṣa vyāghra kharoṣṭrādi praveśane |
yadi gehe cādhirohe yadyakasmānmaheśvari ||

tatsthānaṃ nāśamāpnoti mahiṣī mṛtimāpnuyāt |
praveśe maraṇe prāpte yatra kutra sthale śive ||

trimāsānmṛtyumāpnoti rājapīḍā bhavetsadā |
madhupañjarasaṃbhūte chatrakai ca pipīlike ||

<p. 58> valmīke yatra dṛśyante tadgṛhe pīḍyate sadā |
mṛtyubādhā bhavecchīghraṃ yajamānasya pārvati ||

govadhe strīvadhe caiva saraṭoḍupraveśane |
vyālājagaragṛdhrādi praveśe maraṇe sati ||

tadgrāmanāśanavathe yajamānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
tasya putrasya bhrātrā vā vyādhinā pīḍite bhṛśam ||

maṇḍūke'gnimṛte devi kuṇḍe kumbhasamudbhave |
kapotakāka(-)ṛkṣādi yadi gehe praveśane ||

gonāśaṃ jananāśaṃ ca arthanāśaṃ ca pārvati |
rājapīḍā ripubhayaṃ mṛtyubādhā ca vardhate ||

anyairvā prāṇibhirduṣṭaiḥ dūṣite ca maheśvari |
praveśe maraṇe tasmin sarvārthaṃ naśyati dhruvam ||

dikphalaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi praveśādi yathākramam |
pūrve pūtravināśaṃ syādāgneye strīvināśanam ||

dakṣiṇe śatruvṛddhisyānnai(-)ṛ(-)ṛte cārthanāśanam |
vāruṇe mṛtyubādhā syādvāyavye vyādhipīḍanam ||

<p. 59> some nṛpabhayaṃ devi īśe sarvārthanāśanam |
praveśadvārato gacche māsamekaṃ gṛhaṃ tyajet ||

yadi dvārāntaraṃ devi ṣaṇmāsaṃ tu vivarjayet |
gṛhamadhye praveśe ca vatsarāṇāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

tadgṛhaṃ varjayeddevi śayanāsanabhojanam |
gamanaṃ devi taccaiva viśeṣeṇa vivarjayet ||

dvāre kaṇṭakapāṣāṇa loṣṭānyanyāni pūrayet |
devālaye rājagṛhe varjayenna kadācana ||

sadyaḥ  śāntiṃ prakartavyaṃ yathāpūrvāktamārgataḥ |
pattane vā gṛhe devi saptarātraṃ vivarjayet ||

tataḥ śāntiṃ ca kurvīta sarvadoṣanivṛttaye |
tacchāntiṃ ca pravakṣyāmi rājñaḥ gehe dvijādiṣu ||

praveśasthānadeśe vā pūrve vātha gṛhottare |
maṇṭape vā prapāṃyāṃ vā kurvīta samalaṅkṛte ||

toraṇairaṅgavālyādi kuṇḍaṃ vā sthaṇḍilaṃ śive |
tatsthānaṃ śodhayetpūrvaṃ khananaṃ ca mṛdaṃ tyajet ||

<p. 60> lavaṇaṃ vā mṛdaṃ pūrya kṣālayedgomayodakaiḥ |
candratārānukūle ca rudrakṣetra tithāvapi ||

taddine ca prakartavyaṃ śāntiṃ caiva vidhānataḥ |
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya ācāryo bhūsuraissaha ||

mṛttikāsnānapūrvaṃ ca kuśagomayabhasmanā |
snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena sandhyādi japatarpaṇam ||

nityakarmaṃ vidhāyātha gṛhamāgatya deśikaḥ |
gurvādivandanaṃ kṛtvā vighneśārcanamācaret ||

puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca prokṣaṇaṃ prāśanaṃ priye |
nāndīṃ saṃvācya pūrvoktaṃ madhuparkaṃ yathāvidhi ||

ācāryaṃ varayetpaścādbrāhmaṇaṃ dhanadaṃ śive |
ṛtvijān varayetprājñaḥ tripañca(-)ṛṣisaṃkhyakān ||

japārthaṃ vedapāṭhārthaṃ varayecca pṛthak pṛthak |
gṛhaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kuryātprokṣaṇaiḥ pāvamānakaiḥ ||

yavaṃ ca sarṣapaṃ caiva māṣamudgaṃ vikīrya ca |
saṃmārjyālipya gandhaiśca vāyumantreṇa cāstrataḥ ||

<p. 61> bhūsūktaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca sarvatra prokṣayedguruḥ |
dvārastambhau ca saṃpūjya rākṣoghnaṃ homayettataḥ ||

vāstuhomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vikirecca baliṃ guruḥ |
rakṣābandhaṃ tataḥ kuryāttatpare divase guruḥ ||

gṛhaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kuryādācāryo ṛtvijaissaha |
kalaśasthāpanārthāya vrīhirāśiṃ prakalpayet ||

khāridvayaṃ tadardhaṃ vā tato nyūnaṃ na kārayet |
tadroṇakumbhaṃ suślakṣṇaṃ sthāpayedavraṇaṃ ghaṭam ||

navapañcaikakalaśaṃ pūjayedvidhinā guruḥ |
pañcapallavasaṃyuktaṃ pañcagavyasamanvitam ||

pañcaratnasamāyuktaṃ pūrayetīrthavāriṇā |
palārdhena suvarṇena karṣeṇātha svaśaktitaḥ ||

pratimāṃ kārayedbhaktyā tryambakaṃ ca śacīpatim |
aghoraṃ lokapālāṃśca gaṇeśaṃ ca navagrahān ||

durgāṃ ca kṣetrapālāṃśca tattadrūpān pṛthak pṛthak |
kalaśeṣu vinikṣipya vastrayugmena veṣṭayet ||

<p. 62> pūjayitvā tato mantrī tattanmantraṃ japet pṛthak |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ yadvā pañcopacārakaiḥ ||

tataḥ kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā sthaṇḍile vā gurūttamaḥ |
svagṛhyoktaprakāreṇa kuryādagnimukhāntikam ||

ayutaṃ ca sahasraṃ vā mṛtyuñjayamaghorakam |
navagrahaṃ vāstumantraṃ śāntidurgāmanuṃ hunet ||

abhayaṅkaradikpālān kṣetrapālaṃ hunet priye |
pālāśasamidājyaṃ ca caru vrīhi tilān pṛthak ||

hutvā pūrvoktavān mantrī japapārāyaṇaṃ śive |
tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā kalaśaiśca yathākramam ||

prokṣayedyajamānādīn snapayetkalaśodakaiḥ |
daśadānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā bhūridānaṃ tu dāpayet ||

brāhmaṇān bhojayeddevi yathāśaktyā bahūn tataḥ |
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedante ācāryādi yathākramam ||

ityevaṃ kārayecchāntiṃ sarvāriṣṭavināśanam |
sarvamṛtyupraśamanaṃ āyurvṛddhikaraṃ nṛṇām ||

<p. 63> [iti śrīvātulottare prāyaścittakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde devālaya 
rājagṛhadvijaveśmādiduṣṭaprāṇi praveśanamaraṇaśāntividhirnāma 
saptacatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||





devyauvāca (-)

mahādeva namastubhyaṃ mahīpatisukhāya ca |
mahājanamudārthāya rājyakośābhivṛddhaye ||

pūjakādyāśca saṃproktā tvalliṅge snānakarmaṇi |
sahasrārdhaṃ ghaṭārcāntaṃ snānakarma tvayeritam ||

navādhikasahasrāntakalaśasnapanakriyām |
kathaṃ vada mahābhāga vistareṇa ca māṃ prati ||

śrīsadāśivaḥ (-)

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi rājyārthaṃ vibhavāya ca |
sahasrasnapanaṃ karma navakumbhādhikaṃ priye ||

sūtrasaṃkhyāśca vīthiśca tatsandhīn vyūhasaṃkhyakāḥ |
<p. 64> vyūhanāmānyauṣadhāni vistarātkumbhadevatāḥ ||

tāsāṃ pūjādi sarvāṇi homakāryāṇi śaṅkari |
tava liṅgāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ cottamottamam ||

caturaśrasamāyuktamaṣṭadvārasamanvitam |
veditoraṇabhūṣādi stambhabhūmau manuttamaḥ ||

kuṇḍakhātādikaṃ sarvaṃ mama liṅge yatheritam |
tathā kurvīta matimān snānakarmavidhānavit ||

nityanaimittike kāmye pratiṣṭhāyāṃ mahotsave |
prāyaścitte tathotpāte yuddhādau tajjayādike ||

sarvasandigdhakāryeṣu bandhakarmaṇi śāmbhavi |
durbhikṣādiṣu kāleṣu snapanaṃ kārayetpriye ||

kumbhasaṃsthāpanārthāya sūtrapātaṃ samācaret |
catuścatvāriñca sūtrāṇi ṣaṭsūtrāṇyadhikāni ca ||

prāgagrāṇyudagagrāṇi susamāni likhedbudhaḥ |
teṣu sūtreṣu manuvinmadhye madhye pṛthak pṛthak ||

hitvā caiva catussūtraṃ mārjayīta padaikataḥ |
<p. 65> dṛśyante ca gatavyūhā caiva viṃśādhikāstadā ||

navakoṣṭhasamāyuktāḥ prativyūhe pṛthak pṛthak |
mārgadvādaśa pūrvāgraścottarāgrāstathaiva ca ||

catvāriṃśacca catvāratsvadhikāḥ śatasandhayaḥ |
sandhisaṃkhyāśca vīthīnāṃ mayetyuktā tavānaghe ||

uccastambha catuṣkasya madhyavedyuccatikramāt |
sarvavyūhāḥ samāḥ kuryuḥ madhyavyūhāstavātmakāḥ || 

tanmadhye ca navavyūhāḥ tadbāhye kālikā vṛtiḥ ||

tanmadhye ṣoḍaśavyūhāsteṣāṃ bāhye ramāvṛtiḥ |
caturviṃśatitanmadhye vyūhaśce parikīrtitāḥ ||

tadbāhye śāradāvyūhāḥ dvātriṃśatsaṃyutā gaje |
vanadurgāvṛtisteṣāṃ bāhye vyūhā maheśvari ||

catvāriṃśaditi khyātā madhyavyūhaṃ vinā priye |
vyūhāvaraṇa pañcātra navavyūhāśca madhyame ||

vīthiṃ vinātha saṃmārjya koṣṭhalīnaṃ ca thā bhavet |
rajobhiḥ pañcabhiḥ paścāt śvetapītādikaiḥ padam ||

<p. 66> rañjayetsarvakoṣṭhāni padmadaṣṭadalādikaiḥ |
vīthiśca śyāmalābheti rajasā pūrayettataḥ ||

tanmadhyasthāpanavyūhe madhyame ca navākṣarīm |
kālikāvṛttimadhyastha vyūhānāṃ madhyato'ṣṭake ||

svarān ṣoḍaśa saṃlikhya prācyādiṣu yathākramam |
ramāvaraṇavyūhānāṃ vyañjanaṃ madhyame likhet ||

śāradāvyūhamadhye tu digvidikṣu ca deśikaḥ |
mādi sāntākṣarān likhya bālābījatrayaṃ param ||

avaśiṣṭavyūhamadhyakoṣṭhake lekhayedbudhaḥ |
navadurgāvṛtisthānāṃ vyūhānāṃ madhyame pade ||

dhāraṇākhyāṃśca nādādi prāgādi manuvittamaḥ |
varṇanyāsakramo hyevaṃ pūrayecchālitaṇḍulaiḥ ||

tavātuka navavyūha madhye dhānyaṃ nagodbhave |
prasthatrayaṃ pṛthak pūrva dhānyārdhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ tilam ||

taṇḍulārdhaṃ ca saṃpūrya pūjayetta samūlataḥ |
kālikāvaraṇavyūha madhyakoṣṭhe manūttamaḥ ||

<p. 67> dhānyaprasthadvayaṃ devi sārdhaprasthaṃ taṇḍulam |
prasthaṃ tilaṃ ca saṃpūrya kālikāmanunā yajet ||

ramayāvaraṇavyūhamadhyakoṣṭheṣu śaṅkari |
sārdhaprasthamitaṃ dhānyaṃ taṇḍulaṃ prasthamucyate ||

taṇḍulārdhatilaṃ yojya ramābījena cārcayet |
śāradāvaraṇavyūha madhye dhānyayutaṃ guruḥ ||

pṛthak prasthaṃ taṇḍulaṃ ca dhānyārdhaṃ tatra kovidaḥ |
tilaṃ tadardhaṃ saṃproktaṃ śāradāmukhyabījakam (taḥ) ||

pṛthavyaṣṭvā subhaktyārthaḥ ṣaṇmudrāṃ darśayetparam |
navadurgāvṛtirvyūha madhyakoṣṭhe tataḥ param ||

pādaṃ nyūtamitaṃ prasthaṃ dhānyaṃ dhānyārdhamucyate |
taṇḍulaṃ taṇḍulasyārdhaṃ tilaṃ saṃpūrya buddhimān ||

tairbījairnavabhiryaṣṭvā yonimudrāṃ maheśvari |
koṣṭhajāleṣu sarveṣu prasthaṃ dhānyaṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||

dhānyārdhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ pūrya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ guruḥ |
tāni koṣṭhāni saṃpūjya tava mūlena vallabhe ||

<p. 68> tālamānayutaṃ darbhaṃ pṛthakkoṣṭhopari nyaset |
suvarṇaraupyatāmrādyaiḥ mṛdā mauktika śuktibhiḥ ||

caṣakaiḥ kalaśāḥ kāryāḥ droṇārdhārdhajalānvitāḥ |
jalena kṣālayetsarvān veṣṭayedguṇatantunā ||

grahadigraniṣaddarbhaiḥ bāṇasaṃkhyādikairguruḥ |
kūrcaṃ kurvīta mūlena tava bījena vā priye ||

ghaṭamantastadūrdhvādaḥ pṛthak kūrcaṃ trayaṃ nyaset |
madhyavyūheṣu navasu droṇamānaghaṭān nyaset ||

sarveṣu madhyakoṣṭheṣu droṇārdhārdhamitān ghaṭān |
paritaścopakoṣṭheṣu prasthatrayamitān priye ||

pramādeva ghaṭānnyasthāṃ pṛthak pūjīta koṣṭhake |
ādāvadhomukhān sarvān tataḥ sandhūpyaconmukhān ||

madhyavyūhādi sarveṣu madhyakoṣṭhādito guruḥ |
yathākrameṇa kumbhaughān nyastvā saṃpūjayet kramāt ||

navagandhāṣṭakaṃ devi lolayitvā śubhodake |
tattoyena ghaṭān sarvān pūrayenmanunā guruḥ ||

<p. 69> candanañcaiva karpūramagaruṃ kuṅkumaṃ tathā |
kapimāṃsirocanaṃ ca coragandhāṣṭakaṃ tava ||

madhyakumbheṣu navasu na varaṇaṃ pṛthak nyaset |
śeṣavyūhaghaṭeṣvantaḥ pañcaratnāni caṇḍike ||

sārdhaniṣkapramāṇena suvarṇapratimā nava |
pṛthak kṛtvātha tadbījaṃ tāsusaṃlekhya māntrikaḥ ||

madhyastha navakumbheṣu pṛthaṅnyastvā yathākramam |
pratimāniṣkamānena durgābījasamanvitaḥ ||

kālyādivyūhamadhyastha ghaṭāntarnikṣipedbudhaḥ | 
upakoṣṭhastha kumbheṣu niṣkārdhapratimāṃ nyaset ||

auṣadhāni pravakṣyāmi madhyakumbhāditaḥ param |
elāpatraṃ lavaṅgaṃ ca uṣiraṃ nāgaraṃ jaṭham ||

jātīpalaṃ ca marugaṃ davanāntaṃ nava kramam |
kumbheṣu navasu nyastvā pallavāṃśca phalāni ca ||

sapta ṣaṭpañcahastaiśca yuktaṃ vastraṃ dukūlajam |
śvetādivarṇabhedena nūtanaṃ guṇitaṃ pṛthak ||

<p. 70> navakumbhopari nyastvā yaṣṭvā mūlena bhaktitaḥ |
kālikāvaraṇavyūha madhyakumbheṣvatha priye ||

pṛthak dravyāṇi vakṣyāmi kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu |
śarkarā pañcagavyaṃ ca pañcavṛkṣodakaṃ tathā ||

tiladarbhayavāścaiva sarṣapaṃ dāḍimaṃ vacaḥ |
mātulaṅgaṃ niśākhyaṃ ca lājacūrṇaṃ tathaiva ca ||

dravyāṇi ṣoḍaśaitāni madhyageṣu ca vinyaset |
kāśmīraṃ rocanaṃ koṣṭhaṃ vilaṅgaṃ ca marīcakam ||

candanaṃ jīrakaṃ rāsnā dravyāṇyaṣṭa maheśvari |
taṃ teṣāṃ paritaścāṣṭa kumbhajāleṣu vinyaset ||

ramāyāvaraṇa vyūha madhyakumbheṣvathocyate |
sahadūrvāramābhadra viṣṇukrāntā śatāvarī ||

śakravallī tathā kāśo vārāhī bhṛṅgapatrakam |
śamīpatramapāmārgadvayaṃ karṇidvayaṃ tathā ||

gopuraṃ bhasmacaṇakamudgacūrṇaṃ tathāḍhakī |
māsapiṣṭaṃ ca panasa śrīphalaṃ nālikerakam ||

<p. 71> caturviṃśati saṅkhyāntaṃ dravyāṇyetāni śaṅkari |
prāguktamadhyakumbheṣu pṛthaṅmūlena vinyaset ||

tulasī bilvapatraṃ ca pārijātaṃ tamālakam |
hibeṃ bhadramustā ca padmaṃ nīlotpalaṃ tathā ||

ramāvyūhāṣṭakumbheṣu sarveṣu paritaḥ kramāt |
dravyāṣṭakaṃ pativyūhe nyasedbhaktyā vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

śāradāvaraṇavyūhamadhyakoṣṭhagateṣu ca |
dvātriṃśadudakaṃ vakṣye punaḥ prāgāditaḥ kramāt ||

tulasīmarugākhyaṃ ca bilvapatraṃ ca tairyutam |
patrodakamiti khyātaṃ padmaṃ nīlotpalaṃ tataḥ ||

raktākhyamutpalaṃ ceti smṛtaṃ puṣpodakaṃ tava |
nāraṅgavikacāmotthaphalairyuktaṃ phalodakam ||

kapitthasārasaṃ yuktaṃ valkaṃ bodhipalāśayoḥ |
pippalākhyamidaṃ toyaṃ śucirbhūtaṃ tavātmakam ||

palāśāśvatthacūtānāṃ pallavaiḥ pallavodakam |
prāgādipañcakavyūhamadhyakumbheṣu vinyaset ||

<p. 72> hrīberaṃ bhadramustā ca kuśākhyamiti tairyutam |
mūlodakamiti khyātaṃ tava sannidhyadāyakam ||

priyaṅguśālinīvārairyuktaṃ bījodakaṃ smṛtam |
vidāryutpalapadmānāṃ tribhiḥ kandaiśca saṃyutam ||

kandodakamiti khyātaṃ śrīgandhaṃ cāgaruṃ tathā |
nimākhyaṃ ceti tairyuktaṃ gandhāmbhastviti kīrtitam ||

tilairyavaiśca siddhārthaissiddhāmbaḥ kathitaṃ mayā |
vahnyādidakṣiṇavyūha paṃcake madhyakeṣu ca ||

nikṣipenmūlamantrā ca vadan bhaktyā manuttamaḥ |
māṇikkamuktavaiḍūryairyuktaṃ ratnodakaṃ smṛtam ||

svarṇadurvarṇatāmraiśca yutaṃ lohodakaṃ matam |
ravikāntamayaskāntaṃ candrakāntaṃ catairyutam ||

kāntāmbhastūcyate devi makṣikatrayasaṃyutam |
mākṣikāmbhastu madhyasthe tvabhrakatrayasaṃyutam ||

abhrakodakamākhyātaṃ daityeśavyūhapañcake |
madhyakoṣṭhagateṣveśi tava mūlamanuṃ vadan ||

<p. 73> nyāsasūktādimantreṇa prāṇamantraṃ vadan tadā |
kālayāñjanasaurāṣṭraissaṃyutaṃ jñānavāri ca ||

sindhūragairikābhyāṃ ca dhātunā sahitaṃ tribhiḥ |
dhātūdakamiti khyātaṃ haritālaṃ manaśśilā ||

kucandanaṃ ca tairyuktaṃ raktodakamiti smṛtam |
saritpatiḥ saritkṣetramṛdbhiryuktaṃ ca mṛjjalam ||

triphalāsaṃyutaṃ vāri kaśāyāmbhaḥ samīritaḥ |
pṛthak pañcodakānītthaṃ paścimavyūhapañcake ||

madhyakumbheṣu vinyasya durgāsūktaṃ vadan guruḥ |
kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ ceti tairyuktaṃ ca rasodakam ||

tribhiḥ kuśaiśca saṃyuktaṃ kuśodakamidaṃ bhavet |
vrīhinīvāramāṣaiśca yuktaṃ dhānyodakaṃ tvidam ||

kapikuṅkumakarpūragandhaiḥ saṃmiśratairyutam |
kardamodakamākhyātaṃ kāntimaṅgaladāyakam ||

tamālagūṃ ca dūrvāgraṃ kuśāgraṃ ceti tairyutam |
aṅgurodakamityuktaṃ mārutāśādipañcake ||

<p. 74> vyūhamadhyasthakumbheṣu vinyasedarpayettadā |
drākṣākharjūrakadalī tribhiḥ pakvaiśca saṃyutam ||

pakvodakamidaṃ mugdhe vyūhamadhye gate ghaṭe |
taṇḍulaṃ mudgagodhūmapṛthakpūrṇaiśca saṃyutam ||

cūrṇodakamiti khyātaṃ lokacañcalanāśadam |
tilairyavākṣataiścaiva taṇḍuleśvatikairyutam ||

akṣatodakamityuktaṃ vyūhamadhyasthakumbhake |
śakravalli sahālakṣmī strībhirvalyudakaṃ bhavet ||

campakāśokanāśānāṃ kudmaletaiśca saṃyutam |
kudmalodakamākhyātaṃ janasampatpradāyakam ||

jalanyaivaṃ ca somādi prativyūhaśca pañcake |
madhyakumbheṣu vinyasyet āryāsūktādikaṃ vadan ||

tintriṇīpakvasaṃpanna gulaṃ cūtagulaṃ guḍam |
guledekaṃ ca tairyuktaṃ rajodakamathocyate ||

khurāgarupaṭīrāṇāṃ rajobhiśceti tairyutam |
rajodakamidaṃ devi prāgyugmavyūhamadhyayoḥ ||

<p. 75> kumbhayorvinyasettāṃ ca bālā bījatrayanyaset |
dvātriṃśadvyūhamadhyastha kumbhānāṃ dravyamīritam ||

prāgādi paritasteṣāmaṣṭakumbheṣvathocyate |
devadāruṃ ca penaṃ ca tālīsāṃkhyaṃ ca māṃsikam ||

rāsnā rūtthābhidhānaṃ ca kurundraṃ nāgakesaram |
kumbhāṣṭakeṣu vinyasya dravyāṣṭakamidaṃ priye ||

śvetākambakulaṃ caiva mallikākaravīrakam |
padmaṃ jātiśca kaṃkoṣṭha caturaṅgulasaṃjñakam ||

dakṣiṇasthāṣṭakavyūha pṛthagaṣṭaghaṭeṣu ca |
kramāddravyāṣṭakaṃ nyastvā mādhavī kuravastathā ||

bandhūkaṃ pāṭalaṃ caiva nandyāvartaṃ ca ketakī |
jalaṃ hemākhyapuṣpaṃ ca paścimasthāṣṭavyūhake ||

dravyāṣṭakasamākhyātamaṣṭakumbheṣu vinyaset |
saktuṃ sasaktūṃ śiṃ pañca kulatthaṃ māṣacūrṇakam ||

pakvaṃ jambūdvayākhyaṃ ca lākṣācūrṇaṃ tathaiva ca |
udgvyūhāṣṭake cāṣṭakumbheṣu parito nyaset ||

<p. 76> navadurgāvṛtivyūhamadhyakumbheṣvathocyate |
campakaṃ mallikāṃ puṣpaṃ punnāgaṃ ketakī tathā ||

pārijātaṃ kadambākhyaṃ puṣpaṃ govindasaṃjñakam |
sevantikā droṇapuṣpaṃ padmaṃ nīlotpalaṃ tathā ||

maṇī ca kuñcagoraṇṭaṃ mandāraṃ karavīrakam |
japāpuṣpadvayaṃ devi nandyāvartaprasūnakam ||

aśokaṃ damanaṃ caiva pāṭaliṃ bakulaṃ tathā |
palāśākhyaṃ kusumbhaṃ ca loghrāṇyaṃ cilikāsumam ||

śvetārkaṃ caiva raktārkaṃ bṛhatī vanamallikā |
tato duttūrakusumaṃ nālikeraprasūnakam ||

pūpapuṣpadvayaṃ caiva śaṅkhapuṣpasumaṃ tathā |
karṇidvayaṃ ca kalhāraṃ paṅkajaṃ raktasaṃjñakam ||

arjunākhyaprasūnaṃ ca catvāriṃśatsumaṃ bhavet |
madhyakumbheṣu vinyasyet vyūhānāṃ balirāvṛteḥ ||

candanaṃ ghanasāraṃ ca kapimāṃsīpacorakam |
kuṅkumaṃ cāgaruṃ devi rocanāntamathāṣṭakam ||

<p. 77> teṣāṃ ca madhyakumbhānāṃ paritaḥ pṛthagaṣṭasu |
nyasetsarveṣu kumbheṣu tava mūlamanuṃ vadan ||

sarvavyūhastha kumbheṣu dravyamevaṃ mayeritam |
kūrcāni pallavāṃścaiva nālikeraphalāni ca ||

pṛthak pūgaphalāneśi yugmavastrāṇi tatvam |
ghaṭānane ca parito veṣṭayedvanyasedguruḥ ||

devatāssaṃpravakṣyāmi madhyavyūhādi śailaje |
śāmbhavī mūladurgā ca vedamātā sukhapradā ||

oṅkārarūpiṇī viśvā jaganmāyā janapriyā |
madhyādi navakumbheṣu jūṣaṇīyāntamarcayet ||

prāgādi kālikāvyūha madhyakumbheṣu devatāḥ |
kālī karālī kalyāṇī kāntidā ca kalāvatī ||

kīrtidā kāminī kāntā kamalotpalagandhinī |
kumadvatī kamalinī kṛśānutapanadyutiḥ ||

kṛśāvatī kāñcanākṣī kulavṛddhikarā kriyā |
devatāḥ ṣoḍaśaitāśca teṣu kumbheṣu pūjayet ||

<p. 78> mādhavī māninī manyā mahāmāyā maheśvari |
mātaṅgī mohadā vāṇī devatāṣṭasamīritāḥ ||

prāk ca vyūhāṣṭakumbheṣu pṛthak saṃpūjayedguruḥ |
viśvamātā ca viśveśi viśālākṣā vinodinī ||

brahmaṇyā vimalādbrāhmī varadāntāṣṭadevatāḥ |
dakṣiṇavyūhakumbheṣu pṛthagaṣṭasu pūjayet ||

jāhnavī ca janmāyā japyā jaṅghālavīcikā |
jayā janārdanaprītā hlādinī mohinī punaḥ ||

paścimavyūhakumbhānāṃ madhyasthānāṃ kalādhare |
parite'ṣṭaghaṭeṣveṣu yajedbhaktyā sukhānvitaḥ ||

gaṅgā gandhavatī gaurī guṇātītā guṇāvatī |
goparāgahinī durgā udgvyūheṣu caṇḍike ||

madhyagānāṃ ca paritaḥ pṛthagaṣṭasukhārcayet |
kālikāvaraṇavyūhadevatāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

kamalāvaraṇavyūha madhyakoṣṭhādidevatāḥ |
prāgādi saṃpravakṣyāmi ramā rājīvalocanā ||

<p. 79> rājyapradā rogahartrī mahākośapradāyakī |
mokṣapradā mahālakṣmīḥ bhāgyādā bhogavavardhinī ||

dhanyadā dhanadā devī duritaghnī durāpahā |
dhairyadā dhanadā dakṣā dharmadā viśvanāyikā ||

viśvātītā nirātaṅkā nijānandasvarūpiṇī |
niṣkalaṅkāhagotpannā ramāvyūha kāmbake ||

yajenmadhyastha kumbheṣu caturthyantaṃ namontikam |
pṛthaksvanāmabhisteṣāmaṣṭakumbheṣu devatāḥ ||

lāvaṇyā lalitā padmā lokeśī sarvasākṣigā |
kāmukī kāmyadā tṛṣṇā tatprāgādyaṣṭasu kramāt ||

koṣṭhageṣu samabhyarcya śāntā śokaharā umā |
śubhadā śaṅkhinī raudrī bhavyā bhadrāntamucyate ||

dakṣiṇavyūhajāleṣu madhyagānāṃ svanāmabhiḥ |
pūjayetparitaścāṣṭa pṛthak kumbheṣu suvrate ||

jayapradā jāḍyaharā jagajyeṣṭhā nitambinī |
bhayaṅkaraharo bhoktā bhūtidā bhūti bhūṣaṇā ||

<p. 80> paścimavyūha madhyānāṃ koṣṭhagānāṃ punarguruḥ |
saṃpūjya paritaścāṣṭakoṣṭhageṣu pṛthak priye ||

sukhandyāmaraśreṣṭhā śauryadā ca suvāsinī |
śūlinī śītalā bālā brahmiṣṭhāntāṣṭadevatāḥ ||

madhyagānāmudagvyūha paritoṣṭasu pūjayet |
evaṃ ramāvṛtisthānāṃ kumbhānāṃ devatāḥ pṛthak ||

saṃproktā samyagīrāṇi śāradāvaraṇe yathā |
prāgādi madhyagānāṃ ca vākpādā vākyanāyakī ||

vāgīśvarī vajrahastā kumārī vindhyavāsinī |
sarasvatī ca sarveśī sāmagānapriyā parā ||

kalā kāṣṭhā tuṣṭidā ca puṣṭivṛddhipradāyinī |
varcaskarī balakarī prabalā balavardhinī ||

sthairyadā subhagā saukhyastriṣu saubhāgyadāyinī |
varṇātmikā varṇamayī varṇātītā manonmanī ||

munistutā mohahantrī daityahṛtsaudarī malā |
dvātriṃśaddevatāḥ proktāḥ guṇādhārāntamambike ||

<p. 81> śāradāvṛttivyūhastha madhyakumbheṣu pūjayet |
cinmayī citirūpā ca cañcalā bodhinī prabhā ||

candrikā dhāriṇī sūkṣmā devatāṣṭa mayeritāḥ |
prākvaṃstha vyūhajāleṣu paritoṣṭaghaṭeṣu ca ||

pṛthak svanāmabhiryaṣṭvā namontaṃndentikaṃ guruḥ |
jñānātmikā kriyāśaktiricchāśaktiśca niścalā ||

nityā nirañjanā nandā maṅgalāṣṭāntadevatāḥ |
tadrakṣasthavyūhajālamadhyagānāṃ kramāt pṛthak ||

paritaścāṣṭakumbheṣu svanāmādyairyajettataḥ |
nandinī nādinī jāyā gāndhārī ca taraṅgiṇī ||

mālinī dāhinī mānyā madhyagānāṃ ca paścime |
parito'ṣṭasu kumbheṣu pṛthak taṃ nāmabhiryajet ||

dhanvī dhīrā matirmedhā śuddhā buddhistapasvinī |
ghorāntā devatāścāṣṭa udagvyūhakadambake ||

madhyagānāṃ ca parito veṣṭiteṣu prapūjayet |
śāradāvaraṇe'pyevaṃ devatāssamyagīritāḥ ||

<p. 82> navadurgāvṛtau vakṣye prāgādivyūhadevatāḥ |
durgā kapālinī cāryā siṃharūḍhā tathāmbikā ||

mahiṣasthā caṇḍikā ca vāṇī vastupradāyinī |
cāmuṇḍī sarasā sarvā vaiṣṇavī caṇḍavikramā ||

kalyāṇakāriṇī ramyā pratiṣṭhā ca jitendriyā |
vahnyātmikā caṇḍaghaṇṭā skandamātā malāpahā ||

kuśmāṇḍā śivadūtī ca śaṅkarī śaṅkarapriyā |
nirāmayā nirābhāsā sarvagā sarvagocarā ||

kātyāyinī ca kāmākṣī viṣṇumāyā vimāninī |
nārasiṃhī ca vārāhī vāmamārgapradāyinī ||

pavitrā pāvamānī ca paramaiśvaryabhāvinī |
catvāriṃśamimā māye māyā proktā tavānaghe ||

bahirāvaraṇavyūhamadhyage ca samarcayet |
sanātani sadānandā sāvitrī ca guṇāgraṇīḥ ||

gaganasthā ca nākasthā trikūṭasthākhilottamā |
bahirāvaraṇavyūhamadhya nāṃ ghaṭāṣṭasu ||

<p. 83> pūrvādiparitasteṣu yajettannāmabhirguruḥ |
dakṣiṇe tvatha vakṣyāmi rucirā rogahāriṇī ||

bhedinī daṇḍinīdrā vinidrā tripurāntake |
caṇḍavegāścāṣṭadeviśca paritaḥ pūjayetkramāt ||

mahānādāṭṭahāsī ca madhurā madhurapriyā |
pāśahastā padmahastā jñānapustakadhāriṇī ||

mahopāyāṃtadeviśvapratīcīvyūhasaṃcaye |
madhyagānāṃ ca paritaḥ pṛthagaṣṭasu pūjayet ||

jñānapradā jñānarūpā jñānapañjarasaṃsthitā |
sthāvarā jaṅgamākhyā ca kalātītā kalāmayā ||

śabdātītāṣṭade viśve udagvyūhasamūhake |
paritastanmadhyagātāmaṣṭakumbheṣu pūjayet ||

evaṃ pañcāvṛtistheṣu vyūhakumbheṣu kālike |
devatāśca mayetyuktāḥ pratikumbhe pṛthak kramāt ||

guruḥ svasāmpradāyena ndeṃtrikañca namontikam |
svanāmabhi pṛthakpūjya madhyakumbhe gaṇādikāḥ(taḥ) ||

<p. 84> āvāhanādi ṣaṇmudrāṃ yonimudrāṃ ca kālinīm |
dhenumudrāṃ ca liṅgākhyāṃ prasannākhyāṃ pradarśya ca ||

yajetsaptopapāśāṃśca pratikumbhericakṣaṇaḥ |
ādivā sughaṭān sarvān niśāyāmacaturthake ||

arghyaṃ gandhaṃ ca puṣpaṃ ca akṣataṃ dhūpameva ca |
dīpaśceti nivedyāntaṃ upacārāśca sapta ca ||

prātasnānādikaṃ kṛtvā svanityāni samāpya ca |
tavālaye paṇṭape ca nāndīpuṇyāhavācanam ||

varaṇaṃ madhuparkaṃ ca nītvā karma dvijaissaha |
vedikāṃ paritastheṣu kuṇḍeṣu navasu kramāt ||

homakarmāṇi kurvīta mūrtipaiḥ pāragādibhiḥ |
samidājya caruvrīhi yavaidūrvāgrakaistilaiḥ ||

tava mūlena mantreṇa vāruṇāstreṇa deśikaḥ |
hunetsaṅgavakālāntamaṣṭottaraśatāhutiḥ ||

punaḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ kuryāt saṃpātaṃ yojayedghaṭe |
liṅgaśuddhyādikaṃ kṛtvā nityapūjāṃ samarpayet ||

<p. 85> kumbhastha devatāḥ paścāt prasannārthaṃ yajetparam |
punaḥ ṣoḍaśanāḍyādau bhūsuraiḥ karmavittamaḥ ||

chatracāmarabhūṣādyai bhūmipādyairjanairnaraḥ |
brahmaghoṣairvādyaravairvīṇā veṇuravaissaha ||

navakumbhān samuddhṛtya śiṣyaskandhopari nyaset |
gehaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya bahirāvaraṇe tava ||

praviśedgarbhagehāntasthaṇḍilopari vinyaset |
navakumbhasthakūrcāni nyastvā tvalliṅgamastake ||

vastrāṇi vastramantreṇa tvalliṅge bhrāmayettriśaḥ |
phalamantraistatphalāni vāyubījena pallavān ||

navakumbhodakaiḥ siñcet suvarṇapratimādibhiḥ |
evaṃ samyak ghaṭān sarvān kālikāvaraṇāditaḥ ||

pūrvādi madhyakumbhāṃśca tataścāṣṭau samuddharet |
snānamaṇṭapamārabhya liṅgadvārāvasānakam ||

pārśvayorubhayorviprāḥ pantirūpeṇa saṃmukhe |
vyavasthitāḥ śucirbhūtāḥ vadantastān manūn dvijāḥ ||

<p. 86> sarvapaṅktisthakāḥ sarve ghaṭān sarvān yathākramam |
samuddhṛtyānayeyuste dāpayeyurguruṃ prati ||

tairghaṭaissaicayelliṅgaṃ sūktamantrān vadan budhaḥ |
punastūdriktakumbhāṃśca tadvastrāṇi phalānyapi ||

vāmapaṅktyā nayeyuste maṇṭape vedikopari |
nyastā samūharūpeṇa kālīsūktādikān vadan ||

ramāsūktaṃ śāradākhya ambhasyādi manūn tadā |
navadurgāsūktamantrān vadeyurvedapāragāḥ ||

secanānte mahāpūjāṃ vidhāyoktaṃ prakārataḥ |
tavotsavaṃ tataḥ kuryāt mūlabimbe niyojayet ||

kriyājālaṃ samarpyānte daśadānāni tatparam |
tadante dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādbhojayedbhūsurottamān ||

navaniṣkaṃ ghaṭān sarvān vastrāṇi ca phalānyapi |
ratnāni pratimāṃścaiva maṇṭapaṃ sarvabhūṣaṇam ||

tatropakaraṇaissārdhaṃ deśikendrāya vallabhe |
svasampatyai sukhārthāya pradadyānmanujeśvaraḥ ||

<p. 87> yajamānāya ṣaṇṇiṣkaṃ brahmaṇe pañca ucyate |
niṣkatrayaṃ sadasyāya mūrtipe ca pṛthak dvayam ||

sārdhaniṣkaṃ pāragebhyo jāpināṃ niṣkamucyate |
cārakebhyaḥ sārdhaniṣkaṃ tadante bhūridakṣiṇām ||

pṛthak pṛthak pradātavyaṃ phalaṃ vakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu |
tvaliṅge snānakarmeti saśaktyā manujādhipaḥ ||

sarvasampadbalaṃ kośaṃ vaṃśavṛddhiśriyaṃ yaśaḥ |
putra pautraprapautrāṃśca pararāṣṭrajayādikam ||

kṛpayānantarājyāni tadā prāpnotyasaṃśayaḥ |
viśeṣamatha vakṣyāmi sugopyaṃ suradurlabham ||

purato (*) dhikārārthaṃ tvatkaṭākṣākṣamambike |
tvalliṅge snapanaceti kṛtaṃ śakreṇa bhaktitaḥ ||

punardevagaṇenetthaṃ svapadarthaṃ kṛtaṃ priye |
daityasomau bhāgyārthaṃ kṛtaṃ mātṛgaṇena ca ||

sarvasaubhāgya sampattyai mahīvṛddhyai jayāya ca |
tasmādvīkṣyeti kurvīta pratyaptaṃ tava liṅgake ||

<p. 88> [iti śrīparamarahasye vātulottare śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
naimittikakarmakāṇḍe durgāsahasrakalaśakarmakathanaṃ nāma 
trayoviṃśati paṭalaḥ] ||


snāna karmamayetyuktaṃ navadurgāvidhikriyām |
tavotpatyādi vakṣyāmi pṛthagbhedādikāṃ kriyām ||

ādyutpannā mahānandā mūladurgā sukhapradā |
jayapradā śatruharā prasannā lokarakṣakā ||

śaktijālātmakaṃ devi koṭisūryasamaprabhā |
kālī lakṣmīti vāṇīti tayā mūrtitrayo bhavat ||

tasya pādādi guhyāntā kālikākhyā samudbhavā |
ramā nābhyā galāntā ca kaṇṭhādyā mastakāntakā ||

sarasvatīti sañjātā tuṣṭipuṣṭiprakīrtitā |
durgetyāryā śūlinīti kumārī cāmbikābhidhā ||

mahiṣāsuranāśī ca caṇḍiketi sarasvatī |
<p. 89> vāgītyantaḥ kramāttābhyāṃ pṛthagdānasamanvitāḥ ||

navadurgātrimūrtibhyo sadā sampatpradāyakāḥ |
durgā ca kālikā guhye āryā nābhau samudbhavā ||

kālīmukhe bhagavatī rāmā pādātkumārikā |
ramā nābhau cāmbikākhyā tanmukhe mahiṣāntakī ||

vāṇī kaṇṭhe caṇḍikākhyā jihvāyāṃ ca sarasvatī |
nāsikāyāṃ ca vāgīśī pṛthagetāḥ samudbhavāḥ ||

tābhyaḥ pṛthak navotpannāḥ prasannā nāmadhārikāḥ ||
śailajā caṇḍaghaṇṭīti sundarī brahmacāriṇī ||

kūśmāṇḍā skandamāteti kārtyākhyā kālarātrikā |
gaurīti śivadūtyākhyā māhendrī cāgnisaṃjñakā ||

vārāhī khaḍgadhārīti vāruṇī mṛgavāhikā |
kauberī śūlahastā ca brahmāṇī vaiṣṇavī parā ||

cāmuṇḍeti śavārūḍhā jayeti vijayeti ca |
jitā parā jitetyākhyā rocaneti digambarā ||

udyotanītyumāsaṃjñā mālādhārī yaśasvinī |
<p. 90> citranetrā śaṅkhinīti yamaghaṇṭā manasvinī ||

daṇḍinī vindhyasaṃstheti gahinīti trilottamā |
śaṅkhinī śarabhetyākhyā suvrateti sugandhinī ||

toyeśī somapā devī bhrāmarī haṃsavāhinī |
kārmukhā maṅgalā śoṇā hrādinī mohinī kṣamā ||

rakṣaṇī ramaṇī rambhā rogahantrī surapriyā |
dhanurdharī balā bālā prabalā sthūlanāsikā ||

vajriṇī śaktihastā ca daṇḍahastā ca māninī |
kāpālinī kāmanīti bhīmādevīti bhairavī ||

jvālāmukhī nādanīti haṃsagā ghoṣiṇī śubhā |
māheśvarī mahānandā jagadāhlādakāriṇī ||

ityetā navadurgākhyāścaikāśīti samudbhavāḥ |
pṛthak kramāḥ samutpannāḥ tābhyāścānantaśaktayaḥ ||

tavotpattiritikhyātā caṇḍībhedo mayocyate |
ekacaṇḍī tricaṇḍīti pañca sapta naveti ca ||

triṃśaccaikāśītisaṃjñā śatākhyeti sahasrakā |
<p. 91> caṇḍikābheda ityetāstvayutā lakṣasaṃjñayā (kā) ||

tathaiva daśalakṣāṇāṃ koṭyāntānantacaṇḍikā |
bhedāśca vividhā jñeyā sampatsānnidhyakārakāḥ ||

daśalakṣādi koṭyāntāḥ suraloke samīritāḥ |
bhūtaletvayutānteśi samyaguktā kriyā saha ||

ekatripañca caṇḍyāntā naittikecāthamāsikāḥ |
triṃśāntā saptacaṇḍyādi varānaimittike priye ||

ekāśīti śatetyākhyā sahasrākhyā śubhapradāḥ |
kāmyakarmaṇi saṃproktā vaṃśavṛddhipradāyakāḥ ||

ayutākhyeti lakṣāntā daśalakṣābhidhāgaje |
caṇḍikā caiva koṭyāntā saṃproktā japakarmaṇi ||

ekatripañca caṇḍyāntā naittike ca mayeritā |
naimittike ca saptādi navatriṃśāntakāḥ param ||

ekāśīti śatākhyeśi sahasrākhyā śubhapradāḥ |
kāmyākarmaṇi saṃproktāḥ daityabādhānivṛttaye ||

putradārādi vṛddhyarthaṃ prabhutvārthaṃ ca yaḥ pumān |
<p. 92> ekacaṇḍī sadā bhaktyā jape naimittikarmaṇi |

dhanarājyābhi vṛddhyarthaṃ maṇḍale kārthamambike |
tricaṇḍi vidhinā nityaṃ pararāja bhayāya ca ||

bhūpārthaṃ sarvabhogārthaṃ pañcacaṇḍījapakriyām |
saptākhyāṃ caṇḍibhūmyārthaṃ śatrunāśāya sampade ||

akaṇṭakamahīprāptyai śatrusenākṣayāya ca |
sārvabhaumādhikārārthaṃ balakośābhivṛddhaye ||

pratyabde ca śaratkāle tathā cānyādine priye |
navacaṇḍīṃ jape bhaktyā japapārāyaṇādikaiḥ ||

vaṃśavṛddhye ca rājyārthaṃ caturaṅgabalāya ca |
dhanalābhāya sampattyai triṃśaccaṇḍijapakriyā ||

kadācidadbhutotpanne saṅkaṭe viṣamādike |
mahāpāpavināśārthaṃ vāñchitārthāya kāminī ||

caṇḍiñcekāśīti saṃkhyāmapamṛtyu harāya ca |
sampajjālāya yaśase puṣṭyai rogaharāya ca ||

rājyasampādanārthāya sarvaśatrujayāya ca |
<p. 93> gatarājyepsitārthāya naṣṭadravyasya siddhaye |

subhikṣārthāya vaśyārthaṃ mahāmṛtyuharāya ca |
kāntyai sarvajaneṣṭāya śatacaṇḍitriyā śubhā ||

sahasrākhyāṃ vāñchitārthaṃ ripusenā kṣayāya ca |
paramaiśvaryakāṅkṣārthaṃ mahāmaṇigaṇāya ca ||

balavṛddhyādi dārārthaṃ magaṇyadravyasiddhaye |
rājyadurgādi vṛddhyarthaṃ durbhikṣe ripunāśane ||

ayutākhyāṃ mahāmārī bhayādau ripusaṅkaṭe |
dvīpāntaragatadravyalābhārthaṃ suravandite ||

paśujālasamuddhyarthaṃ gajavājīrathāya ca |
suratnajālasiddhyarthaṃ kāmitārthāya śaṅkari ||

caṇḍī caivātha lakṣākhyaṃ sarpabādhāsu māraṇe |
bhūgatadravyasidhyarthaṃ yadi dvīpasthameva vā ||

sindhusthaṃ parvatasthaṃ ca nadīmadhyagataṃ tu vā |
sarvasampatsamṛddhyarthaṃ bījakarmāṇi sādhayet ||

caṇḍījapavidhiṃ kartuṃ dravyalābhe ca māninī |
<p. 94> ekacaṇḍyādi pañcāntaṃ navābdāntaṃ japet priye ||

saptābdāntaṃ ca saptādi triṃśāntaṃ menakātmaje |
ekādhikamaśītyādi sahasrāntaṃ japet kriyām ||

nītvā bhaktyā vakṣyamāṇā vidhinā manuvittamaḥ |
yugmāṣṭāntaṃ cāyutākhyaṃ japaṃ kurvīta bhaktitaḥ ||

lakṣacaṇḍījapaṃ karma vatsarāṇāṃ mayocyate |
tadardhantaṃ trimāsāntaṃ yāvattāvadayāvadhi ||

caṇḍībhedādikāśceti mayākhyātā nagodbhave |
tavālayapurobhāge prākāre caiśakoṇake ||

catuṣpathe tathodyāne japavīthisthamaṇṭape |
grāmasīmni nadītīre rājadhānyāṃ ca parvate ||

sindhutīre grāmahāre japasthānamidaṃ śubham |
ekacaṇḍikriyāḥ prātaḥ tricaṇḍīsaṅgave japet ||

madhyāhne pañcasaptākhya japārcana hutakriyām |
tavayāme navatriṃśamekāśītikriyāṃ guruḥ ||

śatasaṅkhyāvidhiṃ rātrau gānarātrau sahasrakām |
<p. 95> ayutākhyaṃ madhyarātrau japahomatavārcanam ||

lakṣasaṃkhyākriyāṃ devi vāme kurvīta buddhimān |
sarvāsāṃ caiva caṇḍīnāṃ karma kartuṃ varānane ||

tava yāmaḥ śubhaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kṣipramiṣṭārthadāyakaḥ |
tasmājjñātveti deveśi sarvacaṇḍi kriyāṃ guruḥ ||

tvalliṅge tava yāme ca prakuryācchuddhacetasā |
japārcanaṃ homakarma tarpaṇaṃ bhojanaṃ pṛthak ||

pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu snigdhe tava sānnidhyahetave |

[iti śrīvātulottare naimittikakarmakāṇḍe śivapārvatī saṃvāde 
durgotpattyādi caṇḍī bhedakarma nirūpaṇaṃ nāma pañcaviṃśatiḥ 
paṭalaḥ] ||



atha vakṣyāmyahaṃ devi nitya caṇḍi japakriyām |
tvalliṅgāgre śāṅkare ca maṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam ||

pañcahastamiti kuryāccatudvārasamanvitam |
(asmin patre etāvadeva vartate | itaḥ paraṃ alekhaḥ)
<p. 96> caṇḍī bhedāḥ kriyāścāstassamyaguktā mayāgaje ||

sampadrājyajayārthāya navadurgāvidhiṃ śṛṇu |
karmārambhadinātpūrvapakṣe caiva prabhūttamaḥ ||

tavāṅge śāṅkare bhāge navahastaiśca maṇṭapam |
caturaśrasamāyuktaṃ dikṣu dvāropaśobhitam ||

pañcatribhūmabhittyādi vātāyanayutaṃ priye |
darpaṇodarasaṃkāśaṃ tadagre kalaśānvitam ||

tatribhāgaika bhāgena vedikāṃ caturaśrakām |
tanmadhyame prakurvīta paritaḥ kuṇḍakarma ca ||

caturaśrāṇi vṛttāni digvidikṣu vicakṣaṇaḥ |
śivendrayoḥ pradhānākhyaṃ vṛttakuṇḍaṃ sureśvari ||

vidhinā navakuṇḍāni yoninābhiyutāni ca |
kuṇḍasadmāni sadmāntarmekhalātritayāni ca ||

kuṇḍamānāni khātāni bhūṣaṇāni puroktavat |
krijārthaṃ dravya jālāni sarvadravyāṇi suvrate ||

sampādya bahudhā bhaktyā saṃśodhya kṣālitāni ca |
<p. 97> śubhasthale nyasettāni tanmūlamanunā guruḥ ||

devyālayamalaṃkṛtya tataḥ karma samārabhet |
āśvayujyāṃ site pakṣe navamyāntaṃ manūttamaḥ ||

pratipatprātarutthāya snātvā nadyāṃ dvijaissaha |
śuddhātmānaścārakādyāstadādau gomayodakaiḥ ||

lepanaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ kuryuḥ tava gehaṃ nagātmaje |
samyak saṃśodhya mūlena vāruṇāstreṇa haṃsahe ||

raṅgavālyai rañjayitvā tavāgre maṇṭapādike |
rajobhiḥ pañcabhistatra pūrayet kamalāni ca ||

svanityādisamālyānte gurvādyāste dvijottamaḥ |
vapayeyuśca mūlena kṣuramantreṇa sundari ||

sunadyāṃ caiva loṣṭādyaiḥ sacelaṃ snānamācaret |
tantrevācamya saṃjapya svarṇaraupyādikaṃ ghaṭam ||

nadījalena saṃpūrya gaṅgāmantreṇa taṃ(saṃ) jayet |
rathayānādike nyastvā vedaghoṣaidvijaissaha ||

sarvavādyaravairnāṭyaistava gehaṃ praviśya ca |
<p. 98> nadītoyaṃ ca vardhinyāṃ yojayenmanuvittamaḥ ||

pūjayetprārthayeddevīṃ vakṣyamāṇavidhānataḥ |
śaktijālātmakaṃ devi namaste bhogadāyakī ||

navadurgākriyāṃ kartuṃ anujñāṃ dehi caṇḍike |
iti saṃprārthya sadbhaktyā tavāgre yogamaṇṭape ||

maṇḍalāni likhetpūrvaṃ sampūjya gaṇanāyakam |
gururnāndyādikaṃ gavyaṃ puṇyāhaṃ vacayetparam ||

deśikaṃ varayedādau dhanadaṃ brahmasaṃjñakam |
homārthamaṣṭamūrtīśān vedārthaṃ ṣoḍaśa dvijān ||

viṃśaddvijān japārthāya nava sūktaprajāpakān |
tarpaṇārthaṃ caikavipraṃ rudrasaṃkhyāṃśca secakān ||

trīn viprān cārakārthaṃ ca tanmūlamanunā kramāt |
madhuparkaṃ ca kṛcchraṃ ca datvā mūlena kautukam ||

badhnīyānmūlaberādi rakṣāmantreṇa śāṅkarī |
vidhinā mṛdamānīya nyastvā tadbījamaṇṭape ||

ketuṃ mantreṇa yaṣṭvātha phalādyaiśca nivedayet |
<p. 99> yāgabhūmiṃ samāsādya brahmaṇā dhanadādikaiḥ ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācya tanmantraiḥ kuryānmaṇṭapasaṃskṛtam |
sthānaśuddhiṃ ca bimbākhyaṃ hutvā tanmaṇṭape guruḥ ||

vedikāpūrvabhāgādi dvārakumbhādikā nyaset |
koṇākhyaṃ gaṇḍakā devi vidyeśyākhya ghaṭāṣṭakam ||

kumbhatrayaṃ kālikādi yāgākhyaṃ mūlasaṃjñakam |
dvāradeśeṣu koṇeṣu dvārakumbhasamīpage ||

vedikāyāṃ cāṣṭadikṣu madhyadeśe ca madhyame |
kamalāni likhetteṣu cakramaṣṭadalāni ca ||

śaṅkhamāvartapadmāni trikoṇaṃ vartulaṃ tathā |
yathākrameṇa saṃlekhya pūrayecchālitaṇḍulaiḥ ||

pṛthakprasthamitaṃ dhānyaṃ tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ tilam |
tasyārdhaṃ caiva saṃpūrya yajettanmūlamantrataḥ ||

tantunā veṣṭitaghaṭān vinyasettaṇḍulopari |
gandhodakaiścandanādyaiḥ payobhiśca himodakaiḥ ||
