kaṣāyairmadhunā pūrya mūlakumbhaṃ tu sarpiṣā |
<p. 100> pūrayitvā kramāddevīṃ mātṛkābhirvilottamaḥ ||

maṇīcakaṃ ca mūlena kramukāni nyasetpunaḥ |
kūrcādi navaratnāni pallavāṃśca phalāni ca ||

nikṣipya mūlamantreṇa suvarṇaṃ pratimāstataḥ |
niṣkaṃ niṣkārdhamānena kartavyāḥ pratimāḥ śubhāḥ ||

dukūlaṃ tantujaṃ vāpi śvetapītādi bhedataḥ |
navavastre saptabhirvā kumbhavastramitaṃ priye ||

tenaivācchādya kalaśān procyante devatā pṛthak |
prāgādigaṅgāyamunāṃ yajedgodāvarīṃ guruḥ ||

sarasvatīṃ nirmadāṃ ca sinduṃ kāverikāṃ tataḥ |
tāmraparṇīṃ kramādvārā kumbheṣvāvāhya pūjayet ||

kāntidāṃ karuṇāṃ bālāṃ kalyāṇīṃ kogeśū ca |
gāndhārīṃ gāṇi durgāṃ ca gāyatrīṃ gānahāyakīm ||

gaḍḍukākhya ghaṭeṣvetāḥ pṛthaktaṃ nāmabhissaha |
viśveśīṃ vītarāgākhyāṃ vajrahastāṃ vaśaṅkarīm ||

vāgīśīṃ vasudāṃ(dhāṃ) vegāṃ vārāhīṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ |
<p. 101> vedikāṣvaṣṭaghaṭeṣvaṣṭa vidyeśīrarcayetpunaḥ ||

ghaṭatraye ca mūlena kālīṃ lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīm |
yāgakumbhe ca yāgeśīṃ mūladurgāṃ ca taṭghaṭe ||

āvāhanādisaṃskārairupacāraiśca pañcabhiḥ |
kumbhatrayapurobhāge svarṇaraupyādi pittalaiḥ ||

palatrayamite nālau yantraṃ kurvīta bhaktitaḥ |
likhedaṣṭadalaṃ tatra pūrayecchālitaṇḍulaiḥ ||

navākṣaraṃ likhedyantre svarayuktaḥ prakārataḥ |
prāṇākhya manunā yaṣṭvā nyasettanmaṇḍalopari ||

yajetprāguktamārgeṇa vedikāparitastvadhaḥ |
navārṇa sāmpradāyajñāścopaviśya pṛthak janaḥ ||

argalādi rahasyāntāṃ navākṣaryā samanvitām |
japeyuste saptaśatiṃ trivāraṃ saṃpuṭānvitān ||

brahmāstraṃ śaṅkare koṇe vahnau vahnyastramucyate |
daityakoṇe ca cakrāstraṃ mārutāstraṃ tathānile ||

pradhānakuṇḍasāmīpye yāgamantrajapedvijaḥ |
<p. 102> devīsūktaṃ ca tadrakṣe navavāraṃ japet param ||

tarpayet saumyadigbhāge daśāṃśaṃ ca payombubhiḥ |
catudvāre caturvedān vadeyuścāṣṭabhūsurāḥ |

tavagehe caturdikṣu tathaivāṣṭapraghoṣakāḥ |
paritaḥ prāṅkaṇe devi navadurgāprajāpakāḥ ||

vināyakamanuṃ dakṣe kālīsūktaṃ ramātmakam |
gosūktaṃ śāradākhyaṃ ca durgasūktaṃ japetparam ||

sūktaṃ pratyaṅgirākhyaṃ ca śāntidurgākhyamucyate |
pṛthak sahasraṃ prajapedyāvaddhomāvasānakam ||

secakān secanaṃ kuryuḥ tavaliṅgaṃ sudhārasaiḥ |
payombubhiśca tanmantraiḥ homakarma samārabhet ||

śodhayennavakuṇḍāni vīkṣaṇādi prabhedataḥ |
prāgāmi paritastheṣu kuṇḍeṣu navasu kramāt ||

mūrtipādigurun yaṣṭvā brahmāṇaṃ ca vasupradam |
kuṇḍādi devatāḥ sarvā vakṣyāmāṇavidhānataḥ ||

gandhapuṣpādibhiryaṣṭvā pṛthak tāsāṃ ca nāmabhiḥ |
<p. 103> vāmadevī vajriṇīti vaṃśadā balavṛddhidā ||

jayapradā jaganmātā niṣkalā ca nitambinī |
mahāmāyeti saṃproktāḥ kuṇḍānāṃ devatā imāḥ ||

pṛthagāvāhya saṃpūjya pratikuṇḍe dhanapradaḥ |
kṣitimūrtiṃ pūrvakuṇḍe agnyākhyāmagnigocare ||

dakṣiṇe yajñamūrtiṃ ca sūryākhyāṃ daityakuṇḍake |
vāruṇyāṃ jalamūrtiṃ ca mārutākhyāmathānile ||

somākhyāṃ saumyadeśasthe vyomasaṃjñāṃ ca śaṅkare |
kuṇḍeṣvaṣṭasu saṃpūjya pradhāne mūlasaṃjñakām ||

uṣādimūrtipān devi dvijeṣvaṣṭasu pūjayet |
vijayā janayedagniṃ jvālākāṣṭhādi bhedataḥ ||

pradhānakuṇḍayonau ca nyastvā saṃskāramācaret |
kuṇḍamadhye nyasedagniṃ garbhādhānādi ṣoḍaśa ||

kriyāḥ kuryādyathānyāyaṃ mūrtikuṇḍāṣṭasu kramāt |
tadagniṃ sarvakuṇḍeṣu viharecca mukhakriyāḥ ||

gururādau svakuṇḍe ca samidājya caruṃ priye |
<p. 104> sarṣapaṃ caiva godhūmaṃ tilaṃ ca tuṭikaṃ tataḥ |

jātīphalaṃ ca durvāgraṃ pāyasānnaṃ daśa kramāt |
kālikādistribhirmantraiḥ samidājyacarūn hunet ||

mūlena ca navānnena ṣaḍdravyaṃ sarṣapādikam |
khali(gi)nyādi daśaślokaiḥ pāyasānnaṃ tataḥ param ||

sarvadravyāṇi durgāṇi yāminyāmanuvittamaḥ |
homayeyurmūrtipāste pṛthak kuṇḍāṣṭake punaḥ ||

śatāhutiḥ kālikāyā ramāyā dviśatāhutiḥ |
triśatāśca sarasvatyā mūlamantrāhutistataḥ ||

sahasrārdhāśca saṃproktā navākṣaryā sahasrakāḥ |
durgāmantreṇa sarveṣu kuṇḍeṣu navaja pṛthak ||

sahasrāhuti saṃkhyātāḥ pratikuṇḍe nagodbhave |
pūrṇāhutiśca homānte hutvā kuṇḍabaliṃ haret ||

mekhalāyāḥ pṛthak kuṇḍapūrve ca haviṣā param |
navapiṇḍān prakurvīta gurukuṇḍādyathā kramam ||

nyastvā piṇḍe parisnigdhe kāṣṭhadīpān prabodhya ca |
<p. 105> homakarma samākhyātā vedikumbhān yajedguruḥ ||

tato navākṣarīyantraṃ yaṣṭvā sacātamarcayet |
vedikādhaśca paritaḥ kamalāni likhedbhuvi ||

pṛthak prasthamitaṃ dhānyaṃ tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ tilam |
saṃpūryāṣṭaghaṭān paścāddigvidikṣu nyasettataḥ ||

pradhānakuṇḍasāmīpye ghaṭaṃ vāgīśisaṃjñakam |
pūrayedgandhatoyena pallavāṃśca phalāni ca ||

ratnamantaḥ suvarṇena pratimāpaṇamānataḥ |
pṛthak pṛthak parinyastvā vastramācchādya pūjayet ||

durgākumbhaṃ samādāyā vedamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ |
mūrtipādi dvijaissārdhaṃ prākāre ca tavālayam ||

pradakṣiṇavaśānmantrī ghaṭamantaḥ praveśayet |
liṅgaṃ saṃpūjya mūlena durgākumbhodakena ca ||

tasyāḥ sūktena saṃsiñcet yathāvidhi purassaram |
mahāpūjā prakartavyā mātṛkānyāsa pūrvakam ||

golakā piṇḍikānyāsamadhvanyāsamanantaram |
<p. 106> ṣoḍhā nyāsādi sarvāṇi pūjākāle samācaret ||

tribhirmantraiḥ kālikādyaiḥ kālīṃ lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīm |
dinadurgāṃ samāvāhya ṣaṇmudrāṃ darśayedguruḥ ||

tāsāṃ ca nāmabhiḥ paścāt sahasraiśca pṛthak param |
pūjāṃ prāguktamārgeṇa vidhāya vidhivadguruḥ ||

naivedyaṃ dhūpadīpābhyāṃ mūlena ca nivedayet |
samidājyacarūn hutvā mūlenāṣṭottarāhutiḥ ||

tavāgrasthaṃ ca naivedyaṃ visarjya vidhivattataḥ |
dadyānmūlena tāmbūlaṃ dīpaughān bhrāmayetpunaḥ ||

vāhanaṃ ca dhvajaṃ yaṣṭvā parivārān samarcayet |
bhūtānāhūya ghaṇṭādyaiḥ baliṃ dadyādatho guruḥ ||

tadante cotsavaṃ kuryādyathā vibhavavistaram |
padmadīpaiḥ paribhrāmya devīmantaḥ praveśayet ||

prasannārghyaṃ ca datvā yuścandradīpaṃ pradarśayet |
karmaṃ śālaṃ cārpayitvā bhūsurān bhojayetprabhuḥ ||

śuddhātmānaśca tatsāyaṃ deśikādyā dvijottamāḥ |
<p. 107> devīṃ pañcopacārādyaiḥ bhaktyā saṃpūjya lālase ||

liṅgasyeśānadigbhāge prāṅkaṇe dvijamaṇṭape |
saṃśodhya vidhivattatra tasminnavapadaṃ likhet ||

śāliṃ ca taṇḍulaṃ pūrya prasthaṃ prasthārdhameva ca |
tava bījenārcayitvā tripade ca tripālikāḥ ||

madhya pañcamukhī nyasyet śarāvāśca padatraye |
saṃpūjya gandhapuṣpādyairnavabījāni caṇḍike ||

śāliṃ ca sarṣapaṃ śimbaṃ māṣaṃ mudgaṃ yavaṃ tilam |
niṣpāvaṃ ca kulatthāntaṃ salilāplāvitāni ca ||

sudhābījena payasā secayitvātha nirvaset pe |
navadevi samāvāhya somaṃ saṃpūjayetparam ||

pañcavādyakhaiḥ sārdhaṃ dhūpadīpau pradarśayet |
kṣīrānnaṃ bhakṣasaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtapāyasasaṃyutam || 

nivedayetsudhāmantraiḥ pṛthaṅmūlena māninī |
somakumbhaṃ ca payasā saṃpūryātha vidhānavit ||

bījakarma samāpyānte garbhagehaṃ praveśayet |
<p. 108> siñcetpañcāmṛtairliṅgaṃ śrīsūktena payodakaiḥ ||

devīsūktena durgāyāḥ sūktena ca śubhodakaiḥ |
tvaritākhyena sūktena niśāsūktena mātrikaḥ ||

secanānte mahāpūjāṃ gururāsanapūrvakam |
nyāsajālādikān devi vidhāya vidhivat priye ||

kālīṃ ramāṃ śāradāṃ ca pṛthak mūlena saṃjayet |
mūladurgāṃ dineśīṃ ca ḍāmare ca yatheritam ||

tatkalpoktaprakāreṇa pūjākarma samācaret |
nāmabhiḥ pūjayet paścāt sāhasraiḥ kālikādikāḥ ||

durgā nāmasahasraitāṃ dinadevīṃ yajetparam |
śālyānnaṃ saghṛtaṃ bhakṣaṃ pāyasaṃ ca payodadhi ||

madhuśarkarayā yuktaṃ sūpaśākasamanvitam |
citrāntaṃ kusumānnaṃ ca mūlenāgre nyaset kramāt ||

phalāni pañcajālāni navamantreṇa nāyakī |
nivedayedathāmbāya dhūpadīpādideśikaḥ ||

raṅgapūjā prakartavyā tava tṛptipradāyakī |
<p. 109> dhvajāntaṃ gopurāntaṃ vā yathāvidhi vidhānavit ||

tatho naivedyamudvāsya tāmbūlānyarpayettataḥ |
dīpaughān bhrāmayet paścāt himāntaṃ girisaṃbhave ||

mantrāśiṣā mumaṃ dadyādakṣatāṃśca niśāmayān |
tavāgre vātha paritaḥ prāṅkaṇāntarmanūttamaḥ ||

suvāsinī dampatīśca kanyakāśśatasaṃkhyakāḥ |
abhyaṅgodvartanādyaiśca toṣaye strījanādikān ||

pūjayeddhūpadīpāntaṃ tato vastrāṇi dāpayet |
navavastre saptabhirvā dānavastramidaṃ priye ||

śvetapītādi vastrāṇi kambukāni tathaiva ca |
dukūlādīni vastrāṇi navatantūdbhavāni ca ||

saṃśodhya prokṣayettāni prājāpatyādimantrakaiḥ |
gandhapuṣpādikairarcya tavamūlaṃ samuccaran ||

dānakarma tataḥ kuryādindrādīśāvadhi kramāt |
diksthānāṃ ca śvetavastraṃ kañcukāni tathaiva ca ||

kālīmantraṃ vadan bhaktyā koṇapārśvagayostataḥ |
<p. 110> kañcukaṃ pītavastraṃ ca ramāmantraṃ samuccaran ||

raktavastraṃ pradātavyaṃ antarāle pṛthak tayoḥ |
citravastraṃ kañcukādi śāradāmūlamuccaran ||

tṛptyarthaṃ mūladurgāyā mithunārcā tataḥ param |
yathāvittānusāreṇa datvā pūjāṃ vidhāya ca ||

durgādidinadevīśca navamyāntaṃ kramāt pṛthak |
ekaikottaravṛddhyā tā yaṣṭvā vastraṃ tathā guruḥ ||

kañcukaṃ kaṇṭhasūtrāṇi pradadyānnūpurādikam |
devatāvarṇavastrāṇi kañcukāni tathaiva ca ||

dampatīḥ kanyakāścaiva bhojayestrīkudambakān |
bījaṃ ketuṃ vāhanaṃ ca parivārānyajedatha ||

jayaghaṇṭādikairnādaiḥ pañcavādyaravaissaha |
balidevīssamāhūya tattaddravyasamanvitaḥ ||

dvārādi balipīṭhāntaṃ dikpālānāṃ ca mātṛkam(ṇām) |
śāstādi balidevīnāṃ dhvajāntaṃ dāpayedbalim ||

pālikāntaṃ pṛthak datvā svanāmādyairgurūttamaḥ |
<p. 111> tadante cotsavaṃ kuryāt bahudhā vibhavādikaiḥ ||

sabhāpūjāṃ vidhāyātha baliberaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ |
praviśya prāṅkaṇaṃ paścāt pradakṣiṇavaśāt priye ||

garbhagehaṃ nayedbimbaṃ nyastvā liṅgastu dakṣiṇe |
liṅge saṃyojya caitanyaṃ himadīpaṃ pradarśayet ||

namaste jagadādhāre namaste lokapāvane |
pratipatkarmajālāni gṛhāṇa parameśvari ||

prārthayitveti sadbhaktyā kṛtaṃ karmasamarpayet |
deśikādi janāssarve vīṇāveṇukhādikaiḥ ||

vedaghoṣādikaiḥ śaṅkhaiḥ vividhairvādyanissvanaiḥ |
purāṇaśravaṇairbhaktyā vandimāgadhapāṭhakaiḥ ||

kuryarvinidrāṃ bhaktyādyā nartakairhāsakaissaha |
dvārakumbhādikān sarvān gurukuṇḍasthapāvakam ||

vedimadhyagataṃ yantraṃ bījavāpaṃ taraṅgiṇīm |
navamyāntaṃ ca saṃrakṣya japapārāyaṇādikam ||

homakriyāṃ vedikastha ghaṭārcāṃ yantrapūjanam |
<p. 112> sūktādi secanaṃ karma pṛthak kalpoktapūjanam ||

vastradānaṃ cotsavaṃ ca sabhāpūjāṃ ca bhojanam |
navamyantaṃ kramādevaṃ sarvaṃ karma kudambakam ||

prakurvīta jaganmāye viśeṣamadhunocyate |
dvitīyāyāṃ tithau prātaḥ snātvā karma dvijaissaha ||

tavālayaṃ praviśyātha puṇyāhaṃ vācya bhaktitaḥ |
liṅgaṃ yantraṃ ca saṃsecya prokṣyadurgādisūktakaiḥ ||

pūjāṃ pañcopacārādyaiḥ vidhāya vidhivattamaḥ |
pūrvaghasrārcitāṃ durgāṃ tanmūlamanunāttataḥ ||

svadehe ca samāropya kālīṃ lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīm |
āryāmūlena tāṃ devīṃ liṅge yantre ca tatparam ||

yaṣṭvā bījaṃ niśātoyairūkṣṇaṃ kārayettataḥ |
secanādi kriyājālaṃ japapārāyaṇādikam ||

homaṃ prāguktavanmantrī āryāmūlena tatparam |
kumbhānyantaṃ yajetpaścāt saṃpātājyaṃ samarpya ca ||

homakarma samarpyāryaḥ āryākumbhaṃ samuddharet |
<p. 113> gehaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya ghaṭamantaḥ praveśya ca ||

āryāsūktena saṃsecya śeṣaṃ pūrvoktamārgataḥ |
tadrātrau te vaśūddhātmā kālikādyaśca devatāḥ |

kalpoktavadyajettāśca tathācāryāṃ samarcayet |
śeṣaṃ pūrvoktavatkuryāt karmajālārpaṇaṃ tataḥ ||

namastubhyaṃ sadānande jagadāhlādakāriṇī |
dvitīyā karmajālāni gṛhāṇa suravandite ||

trivāramiti saṃprārthya kṛtaṃ karma samarpayet |
tṛtīyā prātarutthāya snātvā viprottamaissaha ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācya garbhāntaṃ liṅgaṃ secya vidhānataḥ |
yantraṃ ca vedikāmadhyasthaṃ prokṣya pañcopacārakaiḥ ||

saṃpūjya mūlamantreṇa prāgāryā svayamātmani |
saṃyojya śūlinīṃ tatra samāvāhya yajedguruḥ ||

sevanādikriyājālaṃ homakarmāvasānakam |
hutaśeṣaṃ samarpyātha śūlinīghaṭamuddharan ||

liṅgaṃ prāguktamārgeṇa secayitvā samarcayet |
<p. 114> kṛtakarmāṇi tadrātrau prārthanādi maheśvari ||

namastubhyaṃ kalādhāre maṅgale maṅgalaprade |
tṛtīyāyāṃ kṛtaṃ karma gṛhāṇedaṃ sureśvari ||

bahudhā prārthayitveti kriyājālaṃ samarpayet |
bherīpaṭahaśaṅkhādyairvinidrāmācayettataḥ ||

caturthe'hniguru prātaḥ snātvā nityaṃ nivartya ca |
etadeveti mantreṇa puṇyāhaṃ yāgasadmani ||

liṅgagehe ca saṃvācya yantraliṅgaṃ samarcya ca |
śūlinīṃ yojayedehe kumārīmarcayettadā ||

bījāni gandhatoyena secayitvā vidhānataḥ |
vidhāya homakarmāntaṃ kumārīkumbhamuddharet ||

praveśya garbhagehāntaḥ kumārīsūktamuccaran |
siñcelliṅgaṃ śanairbhaktyā śeṣaṃ prāguktavat priye ||

devīṃ saṃprārthya mūlena vakṣyamāṇena māninī |
bhadrakālī namaste'stu mahābhayavināśinī ||

gṛhāṇedaṃ kāmyakarma caturthyāṃ ca mayā kṛtam |
<p. 115> vinidrākarma kartavyaṃ pañcamyāṃ prātarutthitaḥ ||

snātvā liṅgaṃ yajedyantraṃ upacāraiśca pañcabhiḥ |
kumārīṃ hṛdi saṃyujya mūlenāvāhya cāmbikām ||

sarvaṃ karmātha homāntamambikāghaṭasecanam |
bījaṃ payodakaiḥ secyaṃ rātrau karma samarpaṇam ||

sugandhīni namastubhyaṃ sampajjālapradāyikī |
pañcamyāmāmbake karma gṛhāṇedaṃ tilottame ||

iti prārthya mahādevīṃ paścājjāgaraṇaṃ nayet |
ṣaṣṭyāṃ prātaḥ samutthāya susnātvā bhūsuraissaha ||

pavamānādi mantreṇa prokṣayetparitastataḥ |
saṃprokṣya yantraliṅgaṃ ca siñcetpañcopacārakaiḥ ||

yaṣṭvāmbikāṃ ca mūlena hṛdi saṃyojayedguruḥ |
mahiṣaghniṃ samāvāhya liṅge yantre ca tāṃ yajet ||

jalodakena bījāni secayitvā kriyāḥ param |
homakarma samāpyāryaḥ prāguktavidhināgaje ||

mahiṣagnī ghaṭenātha tatsūktenaiva secayet |
<p. 116> karmaughaṃ madhyarātrāntaṃ kṛtvā sadbhaktipūrvakam ||

tuṣṭyarthaṃ prārthayeddevīṃ vaṃśavṛddhipradāyakīm |
mahiṣaghnī namastubhyaṃ kāmitārthapradāyinī ||

mayā ṣaṣṭyāṃ kṛtaṃ karma gṛhāṇa surapūjite |
ityarcayetkriyājālāmarcayenmūlamantrataḥ ||

tato nidrāṃ nivāryaiśi saptamī prātarutthitaḥ |
snānādīni svanityāni samāpya vidhinā tataḥ ||

puṇyāhasalilairādaugururliṅgālayādike |
yāgabhūmau ca sarvatra prokṣya mūlājanā varam |

yantraṃ liṅgāṃśca kumbhāṃśca saptabhiścopacārakaiḥ |
yaṣṭvā tā mahiṣaghnīṃ ca hṛdi saṃyojya mūlataḥ ||

mūlena liṅge yantre ca punarāvāhya caṇḍikām |
homāvadhi kriyājālaṃ caṇḍikāghaṭasecanam ||

niśi pūjāmarcayitvā tuṣṭyarthaṃ prārthayettadā |
namaste girisaṃbhūte sarvaśatrubhayaṅkarī ||

saptamyāṃ kṛtakarmaughaṃ gṛhāṇāśu dhanaprade |
<p. 117> iti kriyāssamarpyānte bodhayeyurmahājanāḥ ||

tathāṣṭamī prabhāte ca gururutthāya bhaktitaḥ |
cārakākādyairdijassārdhaṃ snātvā nityakriyāṃ nayet ||

puṇyāhaṃ manunā vācya liṅgaṃ yantraṃ ca secayet |
saṃprokṣyataṃ navāstena yajetsaptopacārakaiḥ ||

caṇḍikāṃ hṛdi saṃyojya mūlamantreṇa māninī |
sarasvatīṃ samāvāhya manunā liṅgayantrayoḥ ||

bījāni secayedante homakarmoktavatpunaḥ |
maṇṭapasthaghaṭān yantraṃ śāradākumbhameva ca ||

yaṣṭvā tatkumbhamādāya sarvaghoṣairdvijaissaha |
punaḥ praveśya garbhāntaḥ tatsūktena manuttamaḥ ||

siñcet prāguktavatpūjāṃ vidhāya manunāpriye |
bhūtarātrāvasānānta muktakarma kadambakaḥ ||

prārthanādyarpaṇaṃ paścāt śāradāmanunā tataḥ |
kalāvati namastubhyamakārādyakṣarātmike ||

dināṣṭake kṛtaṃ karma gṛhāṇasuranāyaki |
<p. 118> iti tvāṃ prārthya sadbhaktyā kriyāssarvāssamarpayet ||

nidrāṃ ninatihāsādyaiḥ navamyāṃ prātarutthitaḥ |
snānaṃ vidhāya viprādyaissva nityāni nivartayet ||

punarnāndīṃ ca gavyaṃ ca gehe bimbe ca maṇṭape |
pṛthak puṇyāha toyena prokṣya tanmunā guruḥ ||

liṅgaṃ yantraṃ samabhyarcya prokṣaṇaṃ secanādikam |
śāradāṃ hṛdi yojyātha vāgīśīmarcayetpunaḥ ||

bījāṅkurāṇi sampūjya sudhābījena tatparam |
homaṃ pūrvoktavatkuryuḥ pratikuṇḍe ca bhūsurāḥ |

pratiślokaiḥ pradhānākhye pāyasaṃ ca ghṛtaṃ tilam |
yathoktaṃ ḍāmare tantre sarvatantrottamottamam ||

argalādyaistribhiḥ ślokaiḥ saptaśatyā punarguruḥ |
juhuyādāhutiḥ sarvā suvastraṃ kañcukaṃ punaḥ ||

tataḥ pūrṇāhutim paścānnavakuṇḍeṣu suvrate |
samāpya homakarmāṇi saṃpātājya sureśvari |

kumbhe yantre ca liṅge ca samarcyātha pṛthagyajet |
<p. 119> yātrārthaṃ pūrṇayāgārthaṃ pūrayetkalaśadvayam ||

prāgghaṭodakamādāya vedimadhye ghaṭānvitā |
pṛthak tanmūlamantreṇa pūrvavitvekakumbhake ||

tatkumbhaṃ caiśabhāge ca nyastvā yaṣṭvā janā param |
dvāragaḍḍukakoṇasthān vidyeśyantū ghaṭān guruḥ ||

sudhābījena mūlena pūrayetkuṅkumodakaiḥ |
brahmaghoṣādikairghoṣaiḥ vāgīśākhyaghaṭaṃ dvijaḥ ||

deśikādi samādāya praviśedgarbhamandiram |
vāgīśīsūktamantreṇa liṅgaṃ saṃsicya bhaktitaḥ ||

pūjāṃ baliṃ cotsavaṃ ca bhojanaṃ pūrvavaccaret |
tatkṣapāyāñca susnātvā liṅgaśuddhiṃ vidhāya ca ||

secaye vividhaiḥ sūktaiḥ nāmapūjādibhiḥ param |
mahākālyādidevīśca yathākalpaṃ yajedguruḥ ||

naivedyamuktavaddevyāḥ kusumāni niśākṣatān |
samarcya mūlamantreṇa vastradānamataḥ param ||

bhojayedvipramukhyāṃśca kanyakāśca suvāsinīm |
<p. 120> dampatī bhojayitvānte balidānādyathotsavam ||

sabhāpūjāvidhācānte pūrṇayāgaṃ samārabhet |
tavāgre kaṭakaṃ nyastvā yaṣṭvā mūlena śaṅkari ||

rocanaṃ kuṅkumaṃ gandhaṃ niśācūrṇaṃ ghanaṃ guruḥ |
tavāgrasthe ca kaṭake lolayecca śubhodakaiḥ ||

pūjayetprārthayedbhaktyā cūrṇaṃ mūlena mantravit |
navarātravratasthānāṃ saṃpūrṇaphaladāyikī ||

navadurgātmake devi namastubhyaṃ śubhaprade |
cūrṇotsavaṃ kariṣyāmi gṛhāṇa suravandite ||

iti saṃprārthya saṃjapya cūrṇatoyaṃ manūttamaḥ |
mūlaliṅgaṃ ca bimbaughaṃ pālake vāhanaṃ dhvajam ||

digīśānmātaraścaiva balideviścatatparam |
kṣetreśīmaṅkuraṃ devi parivārāmarān pṛthak ||

dvārakumbhādikān sarvān tadyantraṃ kuṇḍadevatāḥ |
pṛthak saṃprokṣya tāḥ sarvāḥ sabhābimbaṃ punarguruḥ ||

tadbimbamanunā prokṣya svakaṃ dehaṃ tataḥ param |
<p. 121> dhanadāya sadasyāya brahmaṇe ca kramāt priye ||

mūrtipebhyaḥ pāragebhyo jāpakebhyastataḥ param |
pradadyāccūrṇasalilaṃ bhūsurebhyaḥ pṛthak punaḥ ||

nṛpādibhyaśca sarvebhyaḥ tattoyaṃ caiva sampade |
bhūtabalyutsavaṃ kuryādgrāmasīmāntameva vā ||

grāmāntaṃ rathavīthyāntaṃ muṣṭinā vikiredbalim |
mahābaliṃ samāpyāryaḥ prākāraṃ ca praveśayet ||

āsujālātmikāmante tanmūlamanunā prabhuḥ |
pūjya santarpya santāḍya nālikeraphalādikaiḥ ||

bimbe ca garbhagehāntaṃ nyastvā liṅgasya dakṣiṇe |
taccaitanyaṃ mūlaliṅge bhojayitvā subhaktitaḥ ||

bhrāmyatāṃ himadīpena prasannārghyaṃ pradāpayet |
tato mantrī sarvaghoṣaiḥ yāgagehaṃ praveśya ca ||

svakuṇḍāgniṃ ca saṃprokṣya vahnimantreṇa deśikaḥ |
jātādi pūrṇahomāntaṃ mayādattāhuti prabho ||

svīkṛtya vāñchitārthaṃ me datvā gaccha hutāśana |
<p. 122> ityuktvāgniṃ visṛjyātha yantraṃ saṃprārthya bhaktitaḥ ||

ramādevi namastubhyaṃ rājyamaṅgaladāyakī |
pūjāṃ gṛhāṇa kṛpayā rājyaiśvaryaṃ prayacchame ||

iti yantraṃ prārthayitvā yāgeśākhyaghaṭaṃ param |
yāgeśvari namastubhyaṃ sarvayāgaphalaprade ||

gṛhāṇa tava kumbhārcāṃ dehi me dhanasampadam |
mūlena prārthayitveti pūjayecchāradāghaṭam ||

sarasvati namastubhyaṃ vidyasampatpradāyikī |
ghaṭārcanaṃ gṛhāṇedaṃ sarvavidyāḥ prayaccha me ||

mahālakṣmi namastubhyaṃ dhanarājyapradāyinī |
kośaṃ vīyaṃ jayaṃ dehi gṛhāṇedaṃ kṛtārcanam ||

mahākāli namaste'stu tava kalpoktapūjanam |
gṛhītvā putradārādi vaṃśavṛddhiṃ ca dehi me ||

namastubhyaṃ mūlamāye nijānandasvarūpiṇī |
vividhān vibhavān dehi gṛhaṇa tava pūjanam ||

namo vidyeśī nāmākhyaḥ sadā kumbheṣu saṃsthitaḥ |
<p. 123> kumbhāṣṭakārcanaṃ yūyaṃ gṛhṇantu suranditāḥ ||

kumbheśvari namastubhyaṃ sarvakumbhādhivāsinī |
sarvakumbhārcanaṃ devi gṛhāṇa kulavṛddhide ||

dvārakumbhādikān sarvān iti prārthya visarjayet |
karma dvijaiścārakādyaiḥ vedamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ ||

vādyajālaravaissārdhaṃ hāsakairgītanartakaiḥ |
chatracāmarasaṃyuktaiḥ ghaṇṭānādairjanaissaha ||

cārakumbhādikān sarvān tadyantraṃ ca tadaṅkuram |
samuddhṛtya kramādbhaktyā malamantraṃ vadan tadā ||

pradakṣiṇavaśādgehaṃ nītvā garbhaṃ praveśya ca |
yantraṃ pīṭhe ca vinyasya tanmantraṃ pūjayecchatam ||

prāgghaṭasthodakairādau siñcetpāṭājanāpadam |
dvārakumbhajalaiḥ pīṭhaṃ pīṭhamantreṇa bhaktitaḥ ||

tatkoṇaṃ koṇakumbhasthaiḥ koṇamantreṇa caṇḍike |
granthimantrerantarālaṃ gaḍḍukasthodakaiḥ param ||

liṅgaṃ prāguktasūktena vidyeśyādighaṭodakaiḥ |
<p. 124> kālikādyaistribhiḥ sūktaiḥ tattrikumbhodakaiḥ param ||

devīsūktaṃ vadan paścānmūladurgāghaṭodakaiḥ |
kautukādi parityajya mūlabimbādi mānini ||

nyastvā liṅgottare bhaktyā kautukaṃ mūlamuccaran |
yāgakumbhasthodakena cūrṇayātrā ghaṭodakaiḥ ||

balibimbaṃ ca tadyantraṃ yāgamantreṇa secayet |
ramāsūktena tatsecya daśasaṅkhyopacārakaiḥ ||

yaṣṭvā bījāṅkuraiḥ paścātsudhābījena cāryayet |
bhrāmayedbahudīpaughān mantraiḥ puṣpākṣaṇān punaḥ ||

samarcya mūlamantreṇa navamyantaṃ gurūttamaḥ |
prasannārthaṃ punaḥ pūjāṃ vidhāya prārthya śāmbhavīm ||

namo devyai mahāmāye sarvadānandarūpiṇī |
navarātroktakarmaughaṃ navamyantaṃ mayā kṛtam ||

samyak svīkṛtya tatsarvaṃ kriyāpūrṇaṃ phalaṃ śriyam |
dehi me putradārādi vaṃśavṛddhiṃ nagodbhave ||

ityuktvā mūlamanunā kriyāssarvāssamarpayet |
<p. 125> dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayettatra gurvādibhyaḥ kramāt priye ||

navaniṣkaṃ deśikāya pradadyāddhanadāya ca |
pañca tribrahmaṇe devi sārdhaniṣkaṃ ca niṣkakam ||

hotṛbhyaḥ cārakebhyaśca jāpakebhyaśca tatparam |
niṣkārdhaṃ sūktajāpibhyaḥ secakebhyastathaiva ca ||

tathā tatpūjakebhyaśca tattarpaṇa kṛte tathā |
niṣkārdhārdhaṃ prabhū bhaktyā cārakebhyastathaiva ca ||

dakṣiṇāṃ ca pṛthak datvā tāsāṃ te bhūridakṣiṇam |
śaktyā vittānusāreṇa karmopakaraṇādikam ||

dadyāttanmaṇṭapaṃ sarvaṃ mantriṇe maṅgalāya ca |
gobhūmyādīni dānāni bhūsurebhyaḥ śubhāya ca ||

dānānte bhūmipādīnāṃ putradārādikāṅkṣiṇām |
pādodakaṃ cāṅkurāṇi kautukāni pradāpayet ||

sahadevīṃ samabhyarcya savastraṃ kañcukādikam |
suvāsinyai ca datvānte gurukuṇḍastha pāvakam ||

gṛhītvā dhanadaḥ paścāddeśikādi dvijaissaha |
<p. 126> vedaghoṣairvādyaravaiśśaṅkhaghaṇṭāravādikaiḥ ||

prabhūttamādikaissārdhaṃ praveśyāryālayaṃ punaḥ |
deśikāgnau tu saṃyujya pūjayedupacārakaiḥ ||

navamyāntaṃ kṛtaṃ karma mayetyuktaṃ tavānaghe |
sarvapāpaharaṃ divyaṃ sarvadā maṅgalapradam ||

rājyadurgapradaṃ rājño dhanakośabalapradam |
putrapautraprapautrādi vaṃśavṛddhipradaṃ priye ||

āyurārogyadaṃ puṃsāṃ dhairyaṃ vīryaṃ manojayam |
yaśaḥ pradaṃ śokaharaṃ sarvadāridryanāśanam ||

navarātroktakarmedaṃ caturvarṇodbhave nare |
pratyabdaṃ caiva yaḥ kuryātsadbhaktyā śuddhacetasā ||

vipraścedaihike jñānaṃ śriyaṃ bhogān bahūnapi |
mokṣamāpnoti dehānte rājyaiśvaryaṃ jayaṃ yaśaḥ ||

dhanāni ratnajālāni sarvasampadbalāni ca |
dravyajñānāni dharmāṇi sārūpyaṃ kṣatriyottamaḥ ||

vaiśyaśceddhanadhānyāni sāmīpyaṃ labhate dhruvam |
<p. 127> śūdrasukhaṃ sadārogyaṃ vṛddhiṃ sālokyamāpnuyāt ||

itthaṃ jñātveti karmaughaṃ navamyantaṃ payaḥ pumān |
samyak kurvīta sampattyai vaṃśavṛddhyai śubhāya ca ||

navadurgākramo hyevaṃ tūlākarma vadāmyaham |

[iti śrīvātulottare durgābhedeśivapārvatīsaṃvāde kāmyakāṇḍe 
navarātriyajanavivaraṇaṃ nāma ekonatriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||




devī (-)

śrutaṃ deva mayā sarvaṃ tvatprasādānmaheśvara |
idānīṃ micchāmi tulābhāraṃ kada vadā ||

kiṃ nimittaṃ tadārohaḥ kiñca dravyaṃ ca kiṃ phalam |
tadvidhānaṃ kathaṃ samyak vistareṇa vada prabho ||

īśvaraḥ (-)

<p. 128> sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ mahādevi tvayā yatparibhāṣitam |
tatsarvaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu śvekāgramānasā ||

purā gaṅgānadītīre ṛṣidevagaṇāśrite |
puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ ca papraccha loke ko hyadhiko bhavet ||

ahaṃ puṇyasyādhika syāt loke'haṃ pāpapūruṣaḥ |
parasparavirodhena viṣṇuṃ śaraṇamāgatau ||

kimarthamāgatau brūtamiti prāha janārdanaḥ |
iti tadvacananaṃ śrutvā mā virodhaḥ parasparam ||

tataḥ sarvagato viṣṇuḥ śūlārohaṇamabravīt |
tatra puṇyaṃ vādhikaṃ syādathavā pāpapūruṣaḥ ||

taṃ dṛṣṭvā tau viniścitya manaśśāntiṃ samāgatau |
kupitau sāntvayan tau hi tulāmuddhārayeddhariḥ ||

ubhau sthitau tulāpātre madhye dhṛtvā janārdanaḥ |
atholayadyathā nyāyyaṃ tulite tena viṣṇunā ||

puṇyaṃ samādhikatvacca bhūbhāgaṃ ca samāviśat |
pāpaṃ ca lāghavādgauri tacchatvāducchritaḥ khilā |

<p. 129> tulāyāmadhikaṃ dṛṣṭvā lajjitaḥ pāpapūruṣaḥ |
avaruhya tulāpātrān svayaṃ bhūmiṃ samāgamat ||

tadārabhya tulaṃ dṛṣṭvā dūrādeva palāyate |
tasmāttulāmāruhanti devaśce manujāstathā ||

dharmādharmakṛtaṃ bhāraṃ tulayā vijigīṣayā |
etasmāt karaṇālloke tulābhāraṃ viśiṣyate ||

tasyā smaraṇamātreṇa sarvapāpavināśanam |
kadācicca tulārohe sarvamṛtyubhayaṃ nṛṇām ||

apasmārādi saṃprāptaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati |
brahmahatyādipāpānāṃ prāyaścittamidaṃ smṛtam ||

iti datvā varaṃ viṣṇuḥ tulāyai lokarakṣakaḥ |
śivālayādiṣu sthitvā tule tvaṃ pāpahāriṇī ||

nuda pāpaṃ nṛṇāṃ nityaṃ bhaktānāṃ ca hitaprade |
ityājñayā mayā viṣṇuḥ tulābhāramidaṃ priye ||

taddravyaṃ ca pṛthagbhedaṃ tadvidhaṃ tatphalaṃ śṛṇu |
krameṇa saṃpravakṣyāmi nimittāni pṛthak pṛthak ||

<p. 130> svarṇena vittaśaktaścettulāmārohayettadā |
cakravartī nṛpaśśreṣṭhaḥ sa bhavetsaptajanmani ||

tadante śivasāyujyamihāpi sukhamaśnute |
rājate vyādhināśaḥ syāt putrapautrādisaṃyutaḥ ||

ciraṃ bhuktvā bahūn bhogān viṣṇuloke mahīyate |
tāmreṇa śatrunāśaṃ syāt ihaloke sukhaṃ bhavet ||

tadante brahmalokaṃ syāt phalaṃ guhyaṃ varānane |
kāṃsyena jvaranāśaṃ syāt sarvaśāntikaraṃ nṛṇām ||

trapuṇā roganāśāya kīrtaye dhanavṛddhaye |
sīsena pāpanāśāya apasmāravināśanam ||

dāruṇā sarvanāśaḥ syāt śilayā svakulakṣayam |
mṛṇmayaṃ putranāśāya taddravyaṃ varjayetpriye ||

kṣīreṇa sarvasaubhāgyaṃ hṛdrogādi vināśanam |
daghnā ca dhanavṛddhiḥ syāt sarvaśatrujayo bhavet ||

āyurvṛddhikaraṃ cājye subuddhiśca manohare |
mākṣikeṇa tulārohe bhūtagrastādi śānti yuk ||

<p. 131> guḍaśarkarayā vāpi sthiralakṣmīmavāpnuyāt |
pañcagavyatulārohe brahmahatyādināśanam ||

tilatailasamārohe apamṛtyuvināśanam |
nālikeraphalārohe śīghraṃ putrādisaṃpadaḥ ||

kadalīpanasāmrādyaiḥ putramitrasamṛddhidam |
drākṣākharjūrakaistuṣṭaḥ ihaloke paratra ca ||

candanāgarukarpūratulārohe maheśvari |
śīghraṃ śriyamavāpnoti sarvatāpanivāraṇam ||

kramukaṃ nāgavallībhiḥ tulārohe śubhapradam |
śvetavastratulārohe tuṣṭipuṣṭipradaṃ śive ||

etaddravyāṇi sampādya yathā śaktyānusārataḥ |
sāmbaliṅgādikai kṣetre harīśe śumbhavidviṣe ||

ṣaṇmukhe ca gaṇādhyakṣe tatsamāruhya bhaktitaḥ |
brahmakṣatriyavaiśyairvā śūdraiśca manujottamaiḥ ||

ṛṣidevagaṇairdevi māmuddiśya yathoktavat |
sudinaṃ caiva niścitya candratārānukūlakam ||

<p. 132> devālayapurobhāge śāṅkare vā viśeṣataḥ |
maṇṭapaṃ kārayedbhaktyā kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile'pi vā ||

puṣpamaṇṭapikāṃ vāpi dvāratoraṇa bhūṣitam |
stambhaṃ tatra dṛḍhībhūtaṃ sāradārumayaṃ tu vā ||

pañcalohena tulayā rajvā patrai prayujya ca |
tulāṃ saṃyojya kūṭasthaṃ viśvakarmokta śāstrataḥ ||

tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya nityakarma samāpya ca |
brāhmaṇaiḥ saha śuddhātmā maṅgalasnānamācaret ||

vastrāharaṇamālyaiśca alaṅkṛtya varānane |
devālayaṃ samāgatyā maṇṭapaṃ praviśetpriye ||

gaṇeśvaraṃ samārādhya nāndīpuṇyāhamācaret |
madhuparkaṃ tataḥ kuryādvarayedbhūsurottamān ||

ācāryaṃ yajamānaṃ ca ṛtvijaṃ vedapāragam |
brāhmaṇaṃ ca sadasyaṃ ca japapārāyaṇa dvijān ||

suvarṇaṃ ca phalaṃ vastraṃ datvā ca varayet priye |
tato niśīthe saṃprāpte saṃskṛte maṇṭape guruḥ ||

<p. 133> dvāratoraṇakumbhāṃśca pūjayecca manohare |
vāsturākṣoghnahomaṃ ca baliṃ vāstossamācaret ||

japapārāyaṇaṃ kuryāccaturastraṃ tathaiva ca |
gaṇeśakṣetrapālaṃ ca mṛtyuñjaya navagraham ||

ācārya yajamānādyairupavāsaṃ ca jāgaram |
pañcagavyaṃ ca pītvā tu jitendriyadṛḍhavrataḥ ||

tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya nityakarma samāpya ca |
tato maṇṭapamāsādya gaṇeśaṃ pūjayedguruḥ ||

homayenmūlamantreṇa aṣṭottarasahasrakam |
mṛtyuñjayaṃ diśāhomaṃ gṛhahomaṃ samācaret ||

caturdikṣu krameṇaiva samidājyacarūn kramāt |
tilaṃ ca sarṣapaṃ mantrī homayedṛtvijaissaha ||

īśānye pūrvabhāge ca kalaśān sthāpayet guruḥ |
yāvadvarṣamitaṃ mugdhe śivakumbhaṃ ca vardhinīm ||

tāvatsaṃkhyān susaṃpūjya homānte secayeccamām |
aṣṭagandhaissamālipya vastrābharaṇapuṣpakaiḥ ||

<p. 134> naivedyaṃ dīpajālaiśca toṣayecca sureśvari |
prārthayedyajamānastu svarṇapuṣpakarasthitaḥ ||

devadeva jagannātha saṃsārābdhi bhayāpaha |
tulāmāroḍhumicchāmi tvatprītyarthaṃ tvadājñayā ||

iti puṣpāñjaliṃ datvā tulāmaṇṭapamāviśet |
saṃpūjya ca tulāṃ mantrī brahmāviṣṇuśivādikān ||

viṣṇumāyāṃ jagaddhātrīṃ pratimāṃ svarṇanirmitām |
tulākūṭe ca madhye ca sthāpayetpūjayecca mām ||

dakṣiṇe viṣṇumabhyarcya vāme brahmāṇamarcayet |
dikpālakāṃśca sampūjya paritaśca yathākramam ||

tulāpātre ca rajvośca aśvinyau candrabhāskarau |
śūlapāṇiṃ paraśupāṇiṃ caturvedān yathākramam ||

mṛtyuñjayaṃ tulādravye pūjayetsvasvanāmabhiḥ |
gandhapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya naivedyāntaṃ sureśvari ||

prārthayedyajamānastu kṛtāñjalipuṭa sthitaḥ |
namaste jagadādhāre namaste sarvasākṣiṇe |

<p. 135> māmuddhara tulādevi rakṣa rakṣa namo'stute |
iti saṃprārthayedbhaktyā phalasvarṇakara svayam ||

pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā vedamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ |
tulādravyaṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ svasya dakṣiṇato nyaset ||

vāme tulāṃ samāropya upaviśya gurūttamaḥ |
kiñciddravyaṃ viśeṣeṇa svasmādbhāraṃ sureśvari ||

karatālairvedaghoṣaiḥ govindeti samuccaret |
kṣaṇamātraṃ tata sthitvā manasā cintayecca mām ||

trirāvṛtyā ca santolya tulāpātramadho nayet |
avaruhyaṃ phalaṃ svarṇaṃ gurave ca samarpayet ||

alaṅkāraṃ ca vastraṃ ca gobhūmyādi viśeṣataḥ |
pūjayecca yathā bhaktyā dānāni vividhāni ca ||

brāhmaṇebhyaśca datvā tu savatsāṃ gāṃ sureśvari |
dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedbhaktyā karmakṛdbhyaśca deśikaḥ ||

tulādravyaṃ ca vibhajeddevatārthaṃ vibhāgakam |
bhūmidānārthamekaṃ ca dāpayecca pṛthak pṛthak ||

<p. 136> brāhmaṇān bhojayetpaścāt yathāśaktyā varānane |
viprāśīrvacanaṃ gṛhya tadante ca samarpaṇam ||

tulābhāraṃ mayetyuktaṃ devatārthaṃ sureśvari |
tava snehena vaktavyaṃ kimanyat śrotumicchasi ||

[iti śrīvātulottare durgābhede śivapārvatisaṃvāde kāmyakāṇḍe 
tulābhārakriyāvivaraṇaṃ nāma dvātriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] |





|| svasti ||

vande vighneśvaraṃ devaṃ sarvavighnādidaivatam |
tannamāmi gaṇādhyakṣa vande vāñchitasiddhaye ||

devyuvāca (-)

bhasmoddhūlitasarvāṅga jaṭājūṭa digambara |
paṣapradoṣakāleṣu viṣṇubrahmādikaiśśiva ||

sarvadevagaṇaiśśambho siddhavidyādharaissaha |
kinnarairyakṣagandharvairmunisaṅghaissamāvṛta ||

parivāragaṇaissārdhaṃ sarvalokahitāya ca |
guhyādguhyatamaṃ samyak rahasyaṃ suradurlabham ||

paripṛcchāmi deveśa prasannātman jagatpate |
aṣṭāviṃśatimantrāṇi vātulāntāni śaṅkara ||

tvayeritāni gopyāni tāni bhedāni samyak |
yeṣa tantreṣu malliṅgalakṣaṇādi kathaṃ vada ||

lakṣaṇaṃ pañca sūtrāṇāṃ sakṛnmalliṅgapīṭhayoḥ |
liṅgāṅgakalpanaṃ samyaggehabandhanalakṣaṇam || 6 ||

<p. 138> liṅgagehādimānāni maṭapaṃ prāṅkaṇādikam |
prākāradvāraharmyādi mūlaprāsādalakṣaṇam ||

dvārādibalidevānāṃ vāhanasya dhvajasya ca |
teṣāṃ pṛthak padaṃ deva vāpī kūpataṭākayoḥ ||

paritaḥ parivārākhyavimbānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ katham |
teṣāṃ ca nilayānāṃ cedāyataṃ connataṃ vada ||

dravyāmbara nāgāragehe bandhana lakṣaṇam |
snāna yāga sabhāsthāna kuṇḍānāṃ caiva lakṣaṇam ||

āyudhānamathāstrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tatpadaṃ vada |
prāsādagopurāgrādi kumbhanyāsakramaḥ katham ||

deśikādi dvijātīnāṃ padaṃ tadgehabandhanam |
nṛpādīnāṃ ca sarveṣāmālayānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam ||

gośālādi gajāśvānāṃ kathamāpaṇikādinām |
cārāṇāṃ sevakādīnāṃ vādyakānāṃ gṛhādikam ||

kathaṃ ratisthalaṃ deva vaiśyānāṃ nilayaṃ vada |
svarṇakārādijātīnāṃ kāṃsyatāmrakṛtāṃ padam ||

<p. 139> rajakādyambarakṛtā cakriṇāṃ dhvajināṃ gṛhān |
śilpināmambarāṇāṃ ca padaṃ sandhikṛtāṃ vada ||

puṣpodyānasthalaṃ kutra sūtrasopānalakṣaṇam |
puṣpanyāsasthalaṃ deva tulasyā vāla lakṣaṇam ||

śibikādirathāṅgānāṃ nilayaṃ lakṣaṇādikam |
ātitheyānna saṃsthānaṃ dīpastambhasya lakṣaṇam ||

dīpajālādimālānāṃ rathavīthyādi lakṣaṇam |
sopānasyopavīthīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ ca kathaṃ vada ||

pūjakāsparśakāścaiva liṅgasyā sparśakāṃ pada |
ācāryalakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ vā nityakarmakramaḥ katham ||

naimittikārcanaṃ karma pavitrāropaṇaṃ katham |
nityanaimittike kāmye snānakarmakramaḥ katham ||

tavotpattiḥ kathaṃ bhedaṃ navadurgāvidhiḥ katham |
tulābhārakriyāṃ deva kṛtikādīpalakṣaṇam ||

vatsarodyutsavaṃ kālaṃ rathārohakramaṃ vada |
damanāropaṇaṃ deva vasantotsavalakṣaṇam ||

<p. 140> bandhotsavakriyājālaṃ bandhanatpeṣaṇaṃ vadā |
taddravyaṃ sāmpradāyaṃ ca bandhe jīrṇe kathaṃ vada ||

jayaghaṇṭā mahāghaṇṭā kṣudraghaṇṭādināṃ hara |
karakāpātrajālādi karaṇānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam ||

bherīpaṭahaśaṅkhādi vādyānāṃ mauriṇāṃ tathā |
vīṇāmṛdaṅgatālādi tittivīṇādinādakam ||

nityādi karmalopānāṃ prāyaścittavidhiḥ katham |
liṅge bimbe tadaṅgecedbhinne naṣṭe hṛte katham ||

mūlaprāsādaśikhare maṇṭape gopurādike |
tadagrakalaśe deva prākāre prāṅkaṇādike ||

dīpastambhe dhvaje dvāre parivāre ca vāhane |
balipīṭha gaṇe yatra pātrajālādike'thavā ||

deśikādijanastome cārake sevakādike |
vādyajāle vādake cetpatite ca mṛte katham ||

halite khaṇḍite bhinne palite dahane yathā |
kadācidadbhutotpāte pararājabhaye katham ||

<p. 141> yasmin tantre ca yadguhyaṃ mantramudrādilakṣaṇam |
devatāvivṛtiṃ sarva karmaṇāṃ naitrikādinām ||

etattantre ca tatsarvaṃ vistarā prāṇavallabhā |
prītyā mudā mahādeva mama sānnidhya hetave ||

sasāmpradāya yuktātman brūhi me bhogadāyaka |

[iti śrīparamarahasye vātulottare śivaṃ prati pārvatīsaṃvāde praśne 
nirūpaṇa paṭalaḥ prathamaḥ] ||




athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava liṅgādilakṣaṇam |
kalābhedaṃ codbhavaṃ ca sakṛttvalliṅgapīṭhayoḥ ||

lakṣaṇaṃ pañcasūtraṃ ca gehabandhanalakṣaṇam |
tadaṅge kalpanaṃ devi krameṇa śṛṇu pārvati ||

layaṃ gacchanti bhūtāni saṃhāre tvakhilaṃ yataḥ |
sṛṣṭikāle yathā sṛṣṭistasmālliṅgamudāhṛtam ||

dvividhaṃ liṅgamākhyātaṃ niṣkalaṃ sakalaṃ priye |
<p. 142> niṣkalaṃ mama rūpaṃ ca sakalaṃ tava rūpakam ||

ubhayossaṃpuṭaṃ yatra liṅgākārasamudbhavam |
parātparataraṃ hyasmāt śivaśaktessamudbhavam ||

niṣkalaṃ hi kalāhīnaṃ sakalaṃ tu kalānvitam |
sakalaṃ niṣkalopetaṃ liṅgākāraṃ maheśvari ||

munidevamanuṣyaiśca pūjitaṃ liṅgamādarāt |
yogināṃ rakṣaṇārthāya śāstrāṇāmudbhavāya ca ||

prāṇināmupakārāya devatānāṃ hitāya ca |
rākṣasānāṃ vadhārthāya tasmālliṅgasamudbhavam ||

svayambhuvaṃ sāmbaliṅgaṃ durlabhaṃ ca varānane |
svarṇarekhā ca madbhedaṃ tava bhedaṃ ca cihnitam ||

tava bhāge cādhike ca sarvaliṅgottamottamam |
sarvābhīṣṭapradaṃ liṅgaṃ tava loke praśasyate ||

rājataṃ yatra dṛśyante cihnaṃ viṣṇośca śaktayaḥ |
pravālaṃ yatra dṛśyante brahmaśaktyā sarasvatī ||

ūrdhvarekhā bhavecchaktiḥ tiryagrekhā śiva smṛtaḥ |
<p. 143> iti cihnāni vartante durlabhaṃ śṛṇu pārvati ||

tryaṅgulordhve yadā liṅgaṃ dṛśyate ca maheśvari |
pañcāṅgulaṃ navāntaṃ ca bhuktimuktipradaṃ nṛṇām ||

phakvajambūphalākāraṃ nālikerākṛtiṃ śive |
vartulaṃ paṅkajākāraṃ tvalliṅgaṃ putrapautradam ||

nadītīre saṅgame ca udyāne parvate taṭe |
eṣu sthāne samudbhūte navaliṅge maheśvari ||

sarvābhīṣṭapradaṃ loke rājño rājyābhivṛddhidam |
liṅgalakṣaṇamākhyātaṃ pīṭhalakṣaṇamucyate ||

liṅgasya vistaraṃ pīṭhaṃ unnataṃ vā maheśvari |
tatsamaṃ caturaśraṃ syāttannālaṃ ca tadardhakam ||

unnataṃ ca tadardhaṃ syāt ekaditri ca paṭṭikām |
vāṇīṃ lakṣmīṃ mahākālīṃ brahmāviṣṇuṃ ca rudrakān ||

tava pīṭhaṃ ca śaktiśca āśritaṃ ca ubhau sthitaḥ |
liṅgamūlena saṃsthāpya sarvalakṣaṇa lakṣitam ||

ūrdhvabhāgaṃ ca tyaktvādau liṅgaṃ saṃyojya pīṭhake |
<p. 144> pūrvāsyaṃ ca sthitaṃ liṅgaṃ praṇālaṃ cottare nyaset ||

pratyaṅmukhasya liṅgasya praṇālaṃ dakṣiṇe nyaset |
udaṅmukhasya liṅgasya paścimābhimukhe nyaset ||

dakṣiṇābhimukhe liṅge pūrvasyābhimukhe nyaset |
pīṭhādhike bhavenmṛtyuḥ hīne liṅge maheśvari ||

pañcasūtravihīnaścet kulakṣayakaraṃ nṛṇām |
pīṭhasya lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ gehabaddhaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu ||

prītasya triguṇaṃ garbhaṃ tadardhaṃ cāntaraṃ priye |
dvāradvayasamāyuktaṃ lakṣmīmaṇṭapabāhyataḥ ||

garbhasya dviguṇaṃ kuryāt taddvāraṃ ca prakalpayet |
maṇṭapasya triguṇitaṃ prākāraṃ parita priye ||

vāhanaṃ maṇṭapaṃ kuryāt tadardhāṃ ca pārvati |
prākāraṃ ca triguṇitaṃ bahiḥ prākārakalpanam ||

maṇṭapasyādhikaṃ dvāraṃ bahidvāraṃ tu pārvati |
prathame liṅgabhittisyāddvitīye dvārabhittikā ||

tṛtīye gopure devi caturthe pīṭhabhittikā |
<p. 145> bahi prākārabhitti syāt pañcamāvaraṇaṃ priye ||

garbhagṛhe catustambhaṃ tadbāhye cāṣṭabhiḥ priye |
maṇṭape dvādaśastambhaṃ prākārādiṣu sarvataḥ ||

aṣṭottaraśatastambhaṃ dviśataṃ triśataṃ priye |
mūtiyuk paṭṭikādīni antaraṃ ca tadantaram ||

gehārdhaṃ śikharaṃ kuryāt tadardhaṃ kalaśe nyaset |
svarṇaraupyāditāmrādyaiḥ kumbhaṃ kṛtvā ca śilpinā ||

eka tripañca kumbhaṃ syāt saptakumbhaṃ nyaset priye |
prāsādādiṣu sarvatra tatkumbhaṃ sthāpayedguruḥ ||

śilpiśāstroktamārgeṇa gehaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ |
yadi hīne kṛtaṃ gehe lakṣaṇaṃ pūrvamuktavat ||

pūjā nārhati talliṅgaṃ piśāca brahmarākṣasaiḥ |
sa bhavecca mahāmārī tadgrāme pīḍyate sadā ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena lakṣaṇaṃ vīkṣya muktavat |
upadeśadgurośśilpī kurvīta tava cālayam ||

śilāyā nirmitaṃ gehaṃ kulakoṭi samuddharet |
<p. 146> ante mama puraṃ prāpya ihaiva sukhamaśnute ||

ceṣṭake dārujaṃ vāpi putrapautrādi sampadaḥ |
sarvabhogāṃśca labhate sarvatra vijayo bhavet ||

dehānte mama lokaṃ tu āpnotyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
lakṣaṇaṃ cālayasyoktaṃ tadāṅgāni nigadyate ||

kumbhaṃ pīṭhaṃ ca vāstuṃ ca vāhanadhvajasaṃyutam |
etatpañcāṅgamityuktaṃ tatra doṣo mayocyate ||

kumbhanāśe kīrtināśaṃ pīṭhanāśe dhanakṣayam |
vāstuvāhananāśe tu dhvajanāśe bhavedyadi ||

nityādi sarvakarmāṇi pūjā nārhanti ca tadālaye |
dharmārthaṃ kāmamokṣāṇāṃ na praśastaṃ ca pārvati ||

devatāṃ sannidhānārthamasurāṇāṃ ca nāśanam |
tasmāt pañcāṅgayuktaṃ ca ālayaṃ parikalpayet ||

ālayaṃ cāṅgamityuktaṃ dvārapālādikān śṛṇu |

[iti śrīvātulottare nityakāṇḍe liṅgalakṣaṇādi 
gehabandhanalakṣaṇavidhirnāma dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ] |




ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi dvārapālādi lakṣaṇam |
vāhanaṃ dhvajapīṭhaṃ syāt parivārālayaṃ śṛṇu ||

śilāditāmrakaṃ vāpi kāṃsyaṃ vā pittalaṃ priye |
rājataṃ svarṇajaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tava liṅge viśeṣataḥ ||

liṅgasya pañcaguṇitaṃ ucchritaṃ ca maheśvari |
tripañcahastamānaṃ vā tava bimbākṛtiṃ guruḥ ||

haste daṇḍaṃ ca dhṛtvātha śaṅkhacakrādidhāriṇaḥ |
antardvāre gopure ca dvārasyobhayapārśvataḥ ||

caṇḍikāmambikānnāma dvārapāle maheśvari |
ślādinī mohinī nāma gopurobhaya pārśvataḥ ||

kātyāyinī jayantī ca lakṣaṇena samanvite |
sudṛḍhaṃ sthāpayeddevi yathā vidhyukta mārgataḥ ||

tava liṅge dvārapāle lakṣaṇaṃ kathitaṃ priye |
vāhanaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi śilādi dravya bhedataḥ ||

liṅgasyordhvaṃ ca kurvīta vistāraṃ sārdhahastakam |
liṅgasya triguṇaṃ vātha pañcasyo lakṣaṇoktavat ||

<p. 148> sthāpayedvidhinā mantrī pīṭhamaṇṭapayordhvaje |
iti bhedasthale devi vāhanasya tu lakṣaṇam ||

dhvajasya lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye tava liṅge maheśvari |
śilādaṇḍaṃ candanaṃ ca devadāruṃ ca gandhajam ||

padmasāraṃ garbhasāraṃ dṛṣṭvā daṇḍaṃ parigrahet |
śikharastha ghaṭāgrasya midaṃ daṇḍāyataṃ priye ||

pañcahastādhikaṃ kṛtvā khātvā stambhaṃ ca bhūtale |
caturaśraṃ daṇḍamūle ekadvitriṃ ca paṭṭikām ||

tadūrdhve cāṣṭadhārāśca tasyordhve vartulākṛtiḥ |
mastake vāhanaṃ nyasya dhvajavediṃ prakalpayet ||

śilāceṣṭakayā baddhvā sārdhahastapramāṇataḥ |
pañcatrayāntaraṃ kuryāt sthāpayedvidhinā guruḥ ||

śilādaṇḍaṃ sthāpanaṃ ca vaṃśavṛddhikara priye |
dārujaṃ sarvasaṃpattiḥ tāmrādyaiḥ kavace kṛte ||

sarvaśatrujayaṃ nṝṇāṃ svarṇaṃ rājyapradaṃ śive |
dhvajalakṣaṇamityuktaṃ balipīṭhaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu ||

<p. 149> śilādi nirmitaṃ pīṭhaṃ tripañcāṅgulavistṛtam |
tasyordhvaṃ ca prakurvīta caturaśraṃ ca vṛttakam ||

tattanmūrtyaṅkitaṃ pīṭhaṃ padmaṃ vātha maheśvari |
caṇḍikādyā devatāśca kalpaye sthāpayedguruḥ ||

devatāṃśca tato vakṣye śṛṇu devi yathākramam |
caṇḍikāmambikāṃ siṃhaṃ dvārasyobhaya cāgrataḥ ||

dakṣiṇe gaṇanāthaṃ ca śaṃkaraṃ ca maheśvari |
svasvadikṣu ca dikpālān brahmāṇaṃ śāṅkare diśi ||

anantaṃ nair(-)ṛte sthāne prathamāvaraṇe śive |
dvitīye saptamātṛśca pīṭhalakṣaṇamucyate ||

hastamātraṃ ca vistāraṃ ūrdhvaṃ saptāṅgulaṃ priye |
vīrabhadraṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca tatpīṭhe dvāradeśataḥ ||

paṅktirūpaṃ ca tatpīṭhe kamalākṛtisaptakam |
tadbahiśca caturdikṣu ūrdhvādhaśca sthale śive ||

mātṛbhyo lokamātṛbhyaḥ sarvamātṛgaṇeṣvapi |
sarvamātṛgaṇādhyakṣo lakṣmī bhūcari khecarī ||

<p. 150> yamadeśe ca saṃprāpya śāstāraṃ ca samīpataḥ |
vāyośca purato durgā durgāsāmīpyato guham ||

dhanadaṃ somasāmīpye sthāpayecca maheśvari |
someśānantare madhye muṇḍinīṃ sthāpayedguruḥ ||

liṅgapīṭhākṛtiṃ kuryāt saptāṅgulasuvistaram |
saptāṅgulaṃ tadūrdhve tu pañcasūtraṃ salakṣaṇam ||

gopuradvārapārśve tu bahirantaśca sthāpayet |
gopurāgre mahākālīṃ karālīṃ vahnigocare ||

yamadeśe ca virajāṃ daityadeśe ca mandarām |
vindhyākhyāṃ caiva vāruṇyāṃ suprabhāṃ vāyugocare ||

saumyasthāne siṃhavaktrāṃ śāṅkare daityamardinīm |
prāgādi sthāpayetpīṭhaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ ca śāṅkare ||

vistāraṃ hastamātraṃ ca caturaśraṃ supaṅkajam |
tripīṭhaṃ ca samāyuktaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ hastamātrakam ||

kṣetrapālaṃ ca saṃsthāpya mahāpīṭhaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu |
sārdhahastamitaṃ kuryāt caturaśraṃ savistaram ||

<p. 151> tadūrdhvaṃ ca samaṃ liṅgaṃ aṣṭapatraṃ ca mūrdhani |
tripīṭhaṃ ca samāyuktaṃ paṭṭikāṃ kārayet priye ||

adhaḥ pīṭhe cāṣṭadikṣu kamalākṛtimuktavat |
liṅgagre dhvajasāmīpye gopuradvāra bāhyataḥ ||

sthāpayecca mahāpīṭhaṃ devatāḥ parikalpayet |
brahmaghoṣīṃ sughoṣīṃ ca yatheṣṭaṃ sukharāgiṇīm ||

sumukhīṃ subhujāṃ caiva pramodihyātmanīṃ punaḥ |
prāgādi paritaḥ pīṭhe kalpayecca maheśvari ||

piṅgalākhyāṃ tvadhaḥ pūjya sukeśīmūrdhvataḥ priye |
evaṃ saṃsthāpya tatpīṭhe tava gehe guruḥ śive ||

pīṭhasya lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ parivārālayaṃ śṛṇu |
bahiḥ prākāraparitaḥ īśānādi yathākramam ||

garbhagṛhaṃ maṇṭapaṃ ca pañca saptakaraṃ priye |
caturaśrāyataṃ kuryāddhastamātraṃ taducchrayam ||

bhityantaradvayaṃ kuryāttatra dvāraṃ prakalpayet |
catustambhaṃ cāntarāle tadbahiścāṣṭabhiḥ priye ||

<p. 152> kūṭākāraṃ ca kurvīta śilpiśāstroktamārgataḥ |
tadagre kalaśaṃ nyasya kumbhamekaṃ ca svarṇajam ||

sthāpayetparivārāṃśca vīreśādi yathākramam |
ṣaṇmukhaṃ ca gaṇeśaṃ ca gopālaṃ mārutātmajam ||

tattanmūrtiṃ prakurvīta śilādidravyabhedataḥ |
ekahastaṃ trihastaṃ vā pañcahasta nava priye ||

lakṣaṇena samāyuktaṃ tattadāyudhabhūṣaṇam |
tattadvāhanasaṃyuktaṃ śilpiśāstre yathoktavat ||

sthāpayedvidhinā mantrī yathāpūrvoktamārgataḥ |
parivārālayaṃ śreṣṭhamanyaiśca bahubhiḥ śive ||

kārayedbahudhā bhaktyā tattanmūrtiṃ tadālayam |
sthāpayedvidhinā mantrī bahiḥ prākāradeśake ||

parivārālayaṃ proktaṃ kūpasthānaṃ śṛṇu priye |
tavālaye caiśabhāge prākāre ca maheśvari ||

bhūmiṃ khātvā jalāntaṃ vā caturaśraṃ ca vṛttakam |
vistāraṃ ca tripañcā vā badhvā ceṣṭaśilādibhiḥ ||

<p. 153> puruṣāntaṃ jalaṃ cāntaḥ dvitripūrṣamitaṃ priye |
matsyaḥ kūrmaṃ paṅkajaṃ ca svarṇaṃ vā rājataṃ priye ||

nikṣipejjalamadhye tu chādayetphalakaṃ priye |
darśanaṃ ca na kurvīta śodhayedvarṣavarṣake ||

kūpalakṣaṇamityuktaṃ taṭākālakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu |
tavālayāgrataḥ kuryāt paścime vātha cottare ||

aśītihastaṃ khātvātha caturaśra samantataḥ |
sopānaṃ ca caturdikṣu śilābhiśceṣṭakaiḥ priye ||

badhvā ca paṭṭikāṃ śilpī tripañcanavakaṃ pṛthak |
jalāntaṃ ca dṛḍhaṃ vadhvā jalaṃ ca puruṣāvadhi ||

pañcatrīṇimitaṃ khātvā jalasthānaṃ prakalpayet |
matsyaṃ kūrmaṃ paṅkajaṃ ca kārayellakṣaṇānvitam ||

vinyasecca tato mantrī madhye maṇṭapalakṣaṇam |
kārayettava liṅgāgre taṭākaṃ ca manoharam ||

yaḥ kuryāddevadeveśi nṛpo vā bhūsurottamaḥ |
vaiśyaḥ śūdro'thavā devi sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt ||

<p. 154> anye ca mama sāmīpyaṃ satya satyaṃ varānane |

[iti śrīvātulottare nityakāṇḍe dvārapālādi taṭākalakṣaṇavidhirnāma 
tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ] |




athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dravyāgārādilakṣaṇam |
dhanāgārāmbaragṛhaṃ snānayāgagṛhaṃ pṛthak ||

namaste devadeveśa sarvadevanamaskṛta |
liṅgasaṃpūjakākke ca rathādyutsavakarmaṇi ||

ācāryatve ca ka śreṣṭhaḥ ke janāsparśakā vada |
tathaivā sparśakā deva garbhāgārādi bhedataḥ ||

tatra doṣaṃ phalaṃ śāntiṃ śrotumicchāmyahaṃ guro |

śivaḥ (-)

śṛṇu vakṣyāmyahaṃ devi sarvaṃ lokahitāya ca ||

rājyakośābhivṛddhyarthaṃ rahasya suradurlabham |
caturaṅgabalārthāya subhikṣārthaṃ manohare ||

dīkṣāvān sakulīnaśca vedaśāstrāgamānvitaḥ |
<p. 155> vaṃśavān tattvavedī ca sa dvijo liṅgapūjakaḥ ||

kuṭumbī satyavādī ca liṅgārcanarataḥ śuciḥ |
liṅgasānnidhyavettā ca mitabhāṣī jitendriyaḥ ||

yājñikaścāstiko dharmī kṛtasaṃskārapūrvakaḥ |
varṇāśramācāraparaḥ tava liṅgārcanārhakaḥ ||

madamātsaryarahitaḥ kṛtadīkṣodhidīkṣitaḥ |
dayāvān dānaśūraśca sadā santuṣṭavān kṣamī ||

janānusāramatimān sāmbaliṅgārcane varaḥ |
sthiraliṅgādiliṅgānāṃ pūjakā sparśakā ime ||

ācāryalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu devi varānane |
āryāvartodbhava śrīmān sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ ||

yatra viprāssadācārāḥ yatayaśca tapodhanāḥ |
santi vedāstathā yajñāḥ āryāvarta iti smṛtaḥ ||

vayaṣṣoḍaśakādūrdhvamadhassaptatirucyate |
kriyādijñānaparyantamekatantre suniścitam ||

adhyāpanaṃ ca kartavyamaṣṭāviṃśati saṃhitāḥ |
<p. 156> uktāni pratisiddhāni punaḥ saṃbhāvitāni ca ||

sāpekṣa nirapekṣāṇi mama vākyāhyanekadhā |
pratiṣṭhādyutsavannṛtta karma kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||

devāgnipūjako nityaṃ gurubhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
sāṃpradāya viśuddhātmā dīkṣāṃ dadyācca śiṣyake ||

devatāvyāptitatvajñaḥ ṣaḍvidhādhva viśāradaḥ |
āstikaḥ karma kurvīta ācāryaḥ śreṣṭha ucyate ||

anyathā tu kṛtaṃ yattu ābhicārastathaiva ca |
tatphalaṃ devadeveśi karturvaṃśasya nāśanam ||

devī (-)

ācārya vaṃśa deveśa bhavedbahujane yadi |
tatkarmakena kartavyaṃ vadaśambho mayi priya ||

śivaḥ (-)

śṛṇu devi varārohe tava snehānmayādhunā |
pravakṣyāmi samāsena śivadaṃ vṛddhidaṃ priye ||

vayovṛddhena tadvaṃśe tāntrikaṃ tu samācaret |
<p. 157> tadanyena kṛtaṃ cettu śatrabhirbhayamādiśet ||

rogabādhā bhavetsadyo tadrāṣṭre tu viśeṣataḥ |
tasmādyatnena girije vayohīno na kārayet ||

tadvṛddhenaiva kurvīta kīrti śrīrājyasiddhaye |
rājavaṃśe tathā devi prabhuvaṃśe tathaiva ca ||

gṛhiṇā yajvinā vaṃśe tattadāryeṇa kārayet |
anyathā doṣamāpnoti tasmādyatnena varjayet ||

iti samyak samākhyātaṃ praśnottaramahaṃ priye |

devi (-)

yadyakasmādvaya śreṣṭho pūjāñcotsavakarmaṇi ||

na kṛte cettadādeka kathaṃ karmakṛtaṃ vada |
deśikendreṇa na kṛte yadyakasmānmaheśvara ||

utsavādīni karmāṇi kartavyaṃ kena ca prabho |

śivaḥ (-)

utsavādīni karmāṇi na kṛte ca guruḥ priye ||

deśikendrasya sahajaḥ kaniṣṭho vā maheśvari |
<p. 158> tatputro vātha pautro vā bāndhavo vā tadājñayā ||

sadīkṣitāstadādeśāḥ āgamārtha viśāradāḥ |
sagotrajāṃśca kurvīta noktadoṣaḥ kathañcana ||

āryakenārcite mūḍhaiḥ gāyatryākṣara samanvitam |
yāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi tavannirayamaśnute ||

tatkarma niṣphalaṃ devi na sānnidhyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |

devī (-)

idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi ācāryasya jaṭādhara ||

brahmahatyādi pāpe ca yadi prāpte kathaṃ vada |

śivaḥ (-)

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi ācāryasya dvijasya ca ||

brahmahatyādi pāpaṃ vā upapātakameva vā |
jñānādajñānato vāpi yadi prāpte varānane ||

tatra karma na kurvīta ācāryaḥ sarvakarmasu |
prāyaścittaṃ kadā nāsti brahmahatyādi saṃbhave ||

yadyakasmātparokṣeta pātake copapātake |
<p. 159> prāyaścittena śuddhiḥ syāt yathā doṣabalābalāt ||

tatpāpaṃ ca nivṛtyātha nṛpasyānujñayā śive |
tataḥ karmārhako devi tatra karma samācaret ||

prāyaścittādvinā tantra karma kuryānna buddhimān |
na spṛśelliṅgabimbe vā nārcayecca maheśvari ||

yadi bimbaṃ spṛśelliṅgaṃ pūjāṃ cotsavamācaret |
na sānnidhyaṃ bhavelliṅge lakṣmīrgrāmādbahirgatā ||

caturaṅgabalaṃ hanti tadrājā mṛtyumāpnuyāt |
śatrubhiḥ pīḍyate devi jvarāpasmārakādibhiḥ ||

tasmād jñātvā guruḥ samyak prāyaścittaṃ samācaret |
ācāryalakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ aspṛśyān śṛṇu pārvati ||

liṅgasyā sparśakān devi kṛtadoṣajanānapi |
adīkṣitorcite liṅge tvasānnidhyaṃ bhaveddhruvam ||

adīkṣitaścaturvedī na spṛśeccāpi mārcayet |
bubhukṣurvā mumukṣurvā samayī sādhako'pi vā ||

ācāryo dīkṣito devaṃ pūjayetsvarthasiddhaye |
<p. 160> yathā mṛgapatiṃ dṛṣṭvā bhīto yāti vane gajaḥ ||

adīkṣite'rcite liṅge tathā bhīto maheśvari |
śraddhāhīno'ti kṛpaṇo bahubhāṣitadīkṣitaḥ ||

kṛtaghno vedahīnaśca durācārī ca durjanaḥ |
liṅgamasparśakāścaite satyahīnādayo dvijāḥ ||

tava liṅgādikaṃ liṅgaṃ sthirākhyaṃ sampadātmakam |
tasmād jñātvā dvijā hyete na spṛśeyuḥ kadācana ||

pramādā sparśayeyuste tvasānnidhyaṃ bhaveddhruvam |
tasmāditthaṃ muhurjñātvā śāntikarma samācaret ||

gehapraveśakān vakṣye mukhanāsyādi bhedataḥ |
śrotriyāssukhanāsyantaṃ gantuṃ yogyaṃ varānane ||

ramāmaṇṭapamadhyantaṃ dvijāssarve'pi vanditāḥ |
pramādādagragatiṣu mūlaṃ prāṇamanuṃ japet ||

pṛthak sahasrasaṃjapya śatakumbhaiśca secayet |
dvijetarā khā(pā)likāntaṃ kṣatriyā vāhanāntakāḥ ||

vaiśyāḥ śūdrāḥ prāṅkaṇāntaṃ tadagragamanādiṣu |
<p. 161> mahāśāntiṃ prakartavyaṃ nāndī gavyādi māntrikaḥ ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācya varayeddeśikaṃ ca hiraṇyadam |
catvāro hotṛkānvedapāragāṃśca tathā guruḥ ||

jāpakaṃ daśaviprāṃśca secakān rudrasaṃkhyakān |
varayenmūlamantreṇa liṅgapañcāmṛtādikaiḥ ||

rudramantraiśca sūktādyaissalilairgandhasaṃyutaiḥ |
homayettava mūlena tavāstreṇa kalādhare ||

manunā śāntidurgāyā ghoradurgādimantrakaiḥ |
samidājyaṃ caruṃ kṣaudraṃ tuṭikaṃ sarṣapaṃ tilam ||

gokṣīraṃ caiva dūrvāgramayutārdhāhuti kramāt |
japapārāyaṇaissārdhaṃ homayennanuvittamaḥ ||

tavamūlaṃ tavāstraṃ ca pañcadurgāmanūn kramāt |
gaṇeśaṃ yoginīmantrān caṇḍīn saptaśatīn guruḥ ||

ayutārdhaṃ sahasraṃ ca pṛthak mantraṃ japettadā |
punaḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ kuryātsampātaṃ ca samarpayet ||

sahasrārdhaghaṭaissiñcet tasyārdhaiśca tadardhakaiḥ |
<p. 162> bahudhā pūjayeddevīṃ śālyādyannaṃ nivedayet ||

dīpajālaṃ paribhrāmya dānaṃ daśavidhaṃ punaḥ |
datvā vipragaṇān bhojya kriyājālaṃ samarpayet ||

dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedante praveśāghaharāya ca |
śāntiṃ vināśadurbhikṣaṃ sannidhānaṃ vinaśyati ||

tasmācchāntiṃ prakartavyā guruḥ prāguktamārgataḥ |
rajako nāpitaścaiva karmakārī dhvajī priyā ||

citratāraḥ kulālaśca punarambarasandhikṛt |
kaivartakaḥ svarṇakārī dhvajāntaṃ praviśetsadā ||

doṣabhāgagragamane rāṣṭranāśo balakṣayaḥ |
rājā ca nidhanaṃ yāyāranāvṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati ||

mūlena ghoradurgāyā mantreṇa ca gurūttamaḥ |
śāntiṃ prāguktavatkuryāt sahasrārdhaghaṭaiḥ śivām ||

vipramukhyāṃśca saṃbhojyāṃ mahābhūtabaliṃ haret |
mṛtajātādikotpannasūtakāviṣṭamānuṣe ||

balidvārādiliṅgāṅgaṃ praviṣṭe cetkathaṃ vada |
<p. 163> prākāre prāṅkaṇe devi vāhanākhye ramā sthale ||

nāsyaṅgabhīsabhāsthāne parivārālayādike |
dravyambara dhanāgāramātitheyānnasadmani ||

naivadyabhakṣaśālāyāṃ prāsādaśikharādike |
dhvajālaye mahāpīṭhe gopure dharmamaṇṭape ||

tavālaye ca sarvatra rathavīthyāṃ ca māninī |
vṛddhikṣaya maghāriṣṭajane caiva praveśite ||

pṛthak doṣaṃ pravakṣyāmi vistareṇa nagodbhave |
durbhikṣaṃ rogabādhā syācchatrubādhā bhavetsadā ||

pararājabhayaṃ rājño rājasainyaṃ vinaśyati |
tadrājā mṛtimāpnoti balanāśo dhanakṣayaḥ ||

nidhanaṃ kośanāśaśca rājadāni vinaśyati |
dhānyanāśaṃ ca sasyādi rājyanāśaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

kīrtināśo bhūtabādhā mahāmārī bhayaṃ nṛṇām |
sampajjālādikaṃ sarvaṃ nāśamāpnotyasaṃśayaḥ ||

ityadyāṃśca mahurjñātvā dhanarajyābhivṛddhaye |
<p. 164> sūtakāviṣṭamanujaḥ kadācinna praveśayet ||

sakṛpa (**) kṣyāmi śāntiṃ vighneśārcanapūrvakam |
nāndīgavyādi puṇyāhaṃ vācayettadanantaram ||

guruṃ ca dhanadaṃ caiva hotṛkān ghoṣakān pṛthak |
caturo varayeddevi jāpakān navasaṃkhyakān ||

sūtakāṃśca tathā viprān secakārudrasaṃkhyakān |
liṅgaśudhyan gavyamantrairādau gavyena secayet ||

punaḥ puṇyāhatoyena liṅgaṃ pañcāmṛtaiḥ param |
rudramantraiśśuddhajalaiḥ sūktaiśca navabhiḥ śivām ||

sūktanāmāni vakṣyāmi śrīsūktaṃ tava saṃjñakam |
pañcadurgādisūktāṃśca sūktaṃ pratyaṅgirākhyakam ||

ramāsūktādibhiḥ snigdhe sūktanāma mayeritam |
gavyamantreṇa tadgavyaṃ prāṇākhyena payo guruḥ ||

carubhiḥ samidājyādyaiḥ gavyena payasā hunet |
tathā sahasrasaṃkhyāntaścāhutirmanuvittamaḥ ||

sahasrārdhāhutibhirvā śatamaṣṭottarāhutiḥ |
<p. 165> punaḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ kuryāt sampātājyaṃ samarpayet ||

sūtakāviṣṭamatye cet yatra yatra gamiṣyati |
tattanmūlena hotavyaṃ pṛthagevaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

śatakumbhe tadā siñcet sarṣapaṃ lavaṇaṃ tilam |
kūśmāṇḍaṃ māṣadhānyaṃ ca bhūsurebhyaḥ pradāpayet ||

bahuviprāṃśca saṃbhojya pūjayenmūla devatā |
sādhāraṇakramo hyevamadhipaṃ procyate'dhunā ||

garbhe nāsyāṃ ramāsthāne pratiṣṭhe sūtakānvite |
dvije caiva mahāśāntikriyā prāguktamārgataḥ ||

prāṇamantreṇa mūlena tavāṅgamanubhistataḥ |
ayutārdhaśca tasyārdhatadardhaścāhutīrhunet ||

snapaye ghaṭasāhasraissahasrārdhestadardakaiḥ |
gobhūmyādīni dānāni dadyāttaddoṣa śāntaye ||

viprāṃścaiva bahūn bhojyā śeṣaṃ karma puroktavat |
brahmahā madyapā svarṇasteyī ca gurutalpagaḥ ||

tatsaṃyogādayo viprāḥ liṅgagehaṃ nagātmaje |
<p. 166> na viśeyurna sandeho pramādāddevatālayam ||

yadi praveśayeyuste prākārādiprabhedake |
pṛthak doṣaṃ ca tacchāntiṃ śṛṇu devi yathākramam ||

brahmahatyāpite mantye prākārānta praveśite |
rājño rāṣṭrasya bhaṅgatvaṃ durbhikṣa syānna saṃśayaḥ ||

tajjanaṃ ca parityajya vidhivacchāntimācaret |
tatra śānti prakurvīta akṛte bahudoṣa bhāk ||

maṇṭape sukha nāsye cet praviṣṭe brahmaghātake |
pararājabhayaṃ rājño virājyatvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

dhanadārādi nāśasyāt rājabhogastathaiva ca |
tasmājjñātveti doṣaughān tatkṣaṇāttaṃ parityajet ||

śāntiṃ ca vidhivat kṛtvā mahābhūtabaliṃ haret |
brahmahā garbhagehāntaḥ praviṣṭe cenmaheśvari ||

pramādālliṅgasaṃsparśe sannidhānaṃ vinaśyati |
rājño mṛtirbhavetsadyo rājadānī tathaiva ca ||

śatrubādhā virājyatvaṃ rājavaṃśakṣayo bhavet |
<p. 167> tasyāmātyāssainyapādyāḥ vinaśyanti na saṃśayaḥ ||

mahāmārībhayaṃ nṝṇāṃ prajānāśo bhaviṣyati |
parityajya tadai śū brahmahādi janān guruḥ ||

punaḥ śāntiṃ prakartavyā vakṣyamāṇena mārgataḥ |
tacchāntiṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi saṃkṣepeṇa kalādhare ||

vināyakaṃ samabhyarcya nāndīkarma tataḥ param ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra pavamānādimantrakaiḥ |
prākāraveśanī devamaṣṭottaraśatairghaṭaiḥ ||

secayettadvidhānena na prāṅkaṇe ca praveśite |
snapaye dviśataiḥ kumbhaiḥ aṣṭāyugmādhikaiḥ śivān ||

maṇṭape bhūsurābhye ca sukhaśālāṃ praveśite |
sahasrārdhaghaṭaiḥ siñcaiḥ deva devīṃ ca śāmbhavīm ||

liṅgagehaṃ praviṣṭaścelliṅgasaṃsparśane yadi |
vidhivadghaṭasāhasraiḥ sāmbaliṅgaṃ tavātmakam ||

tato homaṃ prakurvīta mama gāyatrimantrataḥ |
mūlamantreṇa ghoreṇa prāṇamantreṇa deśikaḥ ||


<p. 168> ayutārdhārdhāhutibhiḥ ayutārdhāhutirhunet |
ayutaṃ maṇṭapenāsyāṃ liṅgagehapraveśane ||

samidājyādibhirdravyaiḥ hunet sārdhāyutāhutiḥ |
gāyatrīṃ mūlamantraṃ ca ghoramantrīṃ navākṣarīm ||

brahmamantraṃ ca dikpālān parivāramanūnapi |
japeddhomāvasānāntaṃ daśadānaṃ tataḥ param ||

annadānādikaṃ dadyāttaddoṣaśamanāya ca |
bhojayedbahuviprāṃśca prākārādi sthale śucā ||

[iti śrīvātulottare durgābhede nityakarmakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
liṅgapūjakādi nirūpaṇaṃ nāma aṣṭādaśa paṭalaḥ] |




mahādeva namastubhyaṃ mahīpati hitā (sukhā)ya ca |
namaste jagadādhāra namaste phālalocana ||

malliṅge naittikaṃ karma ghaṭibhedena śaṅkara |
tatkarmanivṛtirnāma ghaṭibhedena vistarāt ||

<p. 169> uṣatkālādi rātrāntaṃ guruḥ kālādi māninī |
śṛṇu vakṣye viśeṣeṇa sampadrājyābhivṛddhaye ||

uṣaḥkāle kamutthāya svaguruṃ cintayedbudhaḥ |
namaste devadeveśa sarvadevanamaskṛta ||

liṅgasaṃpūjakāḥ ke ca rathādyutsavakarmaṇi |
ācāryatve ca kaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ ke janā sparśakā vada ||

tathaivāsparśakā deva garbhāgārādi bhedataḥ |
tatra doṣaṃ phalaṃ śāntiṃ śrotumicchāmyahaṃ guro ||

śrīśaṅkaraḥ (-)

śṛṇu vakṣyāmyahaṃ devi sarvalokahitāya ca ||

rājyakośābhivṛddhyarthaṃ rahasya suradurlabham |
caturaṅgabalārthāya subhikṣārthaṃ manohare ||

dīkṣāvān satkulīnaśca vedaśāstrāgamānvitaḥ |
vaṃśavān tattvavedī ca sa dvijo liṅgapūjakaḥ ||

kuṭumbī satyavādī ca liṅgarcanarataḥ śuciḥ |
liṅgasānnidhyavettā ca mitabhāṣī jitendriyaḥ ||

<p. 170> yājñikaścāstiko dharmī (iduhindi sammandha) kannaḍalipi
mahādeva namastubhyaṃ mahīpatisukhāya ca ||

mahājanamudārthāya rājyakośābhivṛddhaye |
namaste jadādhāre namaste phālalocana ||

malliṅge naittikaṃ karma ghaṭibhedena śaṃkara |
tatkarmavivṛtirnāma kathaṃ karmamitaṃ śiva ||

viprasaṃkhyāndaiśikādi pṛthak teṣāṃ ca dakṣiṇām |
tattanmantraṃ tatpalaṃ ca śrotumicchāmyahaṃ vada ||

śaṅkara uvāca (-)

tvalliṅge naittikaṃ karma ghaṭibhedena vistarāt |
uṣaḥkālādi rātrāntaṃ guruḥ kālādi māninī ||

śṛṇu vakṣye viśeṣeṇa sampadrājyābhivṛddhaye |
uṣaḥkāle samutthāya svaguruṃ cintayedbudhaḥ ||

cārakādi dvijaissārdhaṃ nadīṃ gatvāruṇodaye |
śaucācāravidhiṃ nītvā snātvā vipragaṇaissaha ||

nāḍyāñca nava pañcāśat svanityāni samāpayet |
<p. 171> tavālayaṃ samāgatya viprabṛndādikaissaha ||

pālike prārthayitvāryaḥ kavoṭodghāṭanaṃ kriyāt |
jayaghaṇṭādi nādena śaṅkhabheryādi nissvanaiḥ ||

sarvavādyaravaissārdhaṃ stotraṃ gītaṃ ca nartanam |
vīṇīveṇuravairdevi vādayettava bodhane ||

samyak prabodhya dīpāṃśca dīpamantraṃ vadan budhaḥ |
deśikānujñayā viprāḥ liṅgaṃ pīṭhaṃ ca tadgṛham ||

saṃśodhya kṣālayeyuste siddhisādhanamasūyakaiḥ |
saṃmārjya vāyubījena prokṣaṇaṃ saudhabījataḥ ||

kṣālanaṃ vāruṇāstreṇa tava mūlena śailaje |
sukhanāsiṃ maṇṭapaṃ ca vāhanākhyaṃ ca gopuram ||

sabhāmaṇṭapaharmyādi parivārālayādikam |
ṣaṣṭināḍyāṃ dvijādyāśca varṇabhedena cārakāḥ ||

tatra tatra pṛthak kuryuḥ bahirdvārāvadhi priye |
tavāgre dvāradeśe ca dharmaśravaṇa maṇṭape ||

ghaṭisthāne sabhākhye ca parivāra niketane |
<p. 172> rañjayedraṅgavālyādyaiḥ rajobhiḥ pañcabhiḥ param ||

naḍikā prathamādau tu ghaṭikāpātramarcayet |
tanyastvā sauramantreṇa vedaghoṣādibhi svanaiḥ ||

nadītoyaṃ samānīya vardhanyāṃ yojya mūlataḥ |
svaśākhokta dvijaissārdhaṃ tavavandanapūrvakam ||

gururantaḥ praviśyātha liṅgadakṣiṇa bhāgake |
maṇḍalāni likhedādau samārādhya vināyakam ||

nāndī karmādi puṇyāhaṃ mūlaliṅgagṛhāditaḥ |
sūpadhānyādi sarvatra vāpayedvarayeddvijān ||

guruṃ pitāmahaṃ dravyadāyakaṃ bimbavāhanam |
sadasyaṃ caturo vedaghoṣakān pañcasūktakān ||

śrīrudrasevakan pañca pūjakān jāpakān tathā |
pradakṣiṇa kṛtastrīṃśca nṛtyarthaṃ trīn janān tadā ||

cārārthaṃ daśaviprāṃśca pṛthak tanmūlamantrataḥ |
pavamāna ghaṭaṃ nyasya kumbhaṃ yaṣṭvā vidhānataḥ ||

kālī ghaṇaṃ(ṭaṃ) rudrakumbhaṃ yajetpañca sudhārasān |
<p. 173> gavyakumbhastha gavyena gavyamantramudīrayet ||

pāvamānena sūktena tadghaṭasthodakena ca |
siñcelliṅgañca gaṅgāyāḥ sahasrairnāmabhiḥ param ||

yaṣṭvā pañcopacārādyaiḥ tava bījasamanvitaiḥ |
sārdhadvināḍyā dadhyannaṃ samarpya manunā tava ||

tāmbūlaṃ dāpayeddevyāḥ savatsāgāṃ nirīkṣayet |
pañcāṅgaśravaṇaṃ paścāt nīrājyaṃ bhrāmayettataḥ ||

mantraiḥ puṣpaṃ samabhyarcya punaḥ pañcāmṛtaiḥ śivām |
tairmantraissecayelliṅgaṃ rudramantreṇa tatparam ||

rudrasaṃkhyāvṛtaṃ rudraṃ tvadantaḥ secya bhaktitaḥ |
kālīsūktena durgāyāḥ sūktenāryābhidhena ca ||

śūlinīsūktamantreṇa secayitvā pṛthak guruḥ |
rudrakumbhodakenānte kālīghaṭajalena tu ||

tavālaya caturdikṣu vedapārāyaṇādikam |
kālīmanuṃ ca sauraṃ ca natikaṃ tarpaṇaṃ tadā ||

durgāmanuṃ ca vahnyastraṃ śūlinimanumuttamam |
<p. 174> japettavopahārāntaṃ pṛthak pallavasaṃyutam ||

pradakṣiṇaṃ natiṃ caiva balidānāvadhi priye |
kālīmālāmanuṃ paścāddurgāmālābhidhaṃ guruḥ ||

āryā bhagavatī mālāṃ tava mālāmanuṃ kramāt |
japan sahasramardhaṃ vā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tu vā ||

tvalliṅgadakṣiṇe bhāge paścime vātha cottare |
koṇe koṇāntarāle vā mūlagehe tvathāpi vā ||

maṇṭape vāhanākhye ca pṛthaṅmantrān japet tadā |
karvīta manunā prārthya kālīkalpoktavadguruḥ ||

yaṣṭvā tannāmabhirliṅgaṃ sahasraiḥ bījasaṃyutaiḥ |
durgāryaśūlinīnāṃ ca sahasrairnāmabhiryajet ||

pañca puṣpāñjalirdatvā sāmpradāyoktavadyathā |
upacāraiṣṣoḍaśabhiḥ tava mūlaṃ japecchatam ||

mudgānnaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ sūpaśākarasānvitam |
madhuśarkarayā yuktaṃ bālacūtaphalairyutam ||

pāyasaṃ bhakṣapānīya lehyacoṣyādi saṃyutam |
<p. 175> pañcanāḍyañca mūlena dhūpadīpādikaṃ guruḥ ||

nivedayettato bhaktyā dvāramācchādya vastrataḥ |
homagehaṃ praviśyātha mūlenāṣṭottaraṃ śatam ||

hutvā sampātamādāya tava mūlan vadan budhaḥ |
prakṣālya pādāvācamya ghaṇṭātāḍanapūrvakam ||

tyajedācchādanaṃ paścānnivedyāni visarjayet |
prasannārghyāṃ ca datvānte tāmbūlārpaṇamāditaḥ ||

bhrāmayeddīpajālāṃśca nīrājyāntaṃ ca buddhimān |
nīradīpaṃ darśayitvā mantrapuṣpārcanaṃ tataḥ ||

parivārān samabhyarcya jayaghaṇṭāravādikaiḥ |
bhūtānāhūya mūlena mālāmantreṇa deśikaḥ ||

balidānaṃ ca ṣaṇṇāḍyāmutsavaṃ ca pracārayet |
padmadīpaṃ samarcyeśi pādaprakṣālanāditaḥ ||

tava bimbaṃ praviśyāntaḥ candradīpaṃ pradarśayet |
karmajālaṃ samarpyānte sadbhaktyā mantravittamaḥ ||

karma saṃpūrṇatāvācyai kṛcchraṃ dadyātparaṃ guruḥ |
<p. 176> pañcottaraṃ ṣaṣṭikarma prātaḥkāle mayeritam ||

sarvabhogapradaṃ divyaṃ sarvāghaughavināśanam |
subhikṣadaṃ śokaharaṃ sarvaśatrujayapradam ||

vaṃśavṛddhikaraṃ sadyaḥ kāmitārthapradaṃ priye |

[iti śrīvātulottare durgā bhede nityakarmakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
ghaṭibhedena durgāḥ prātaḥkāla nityakarma vivṛtividhānanirūpaṇaṃ 
nāma ekonaviṃśati paṭalaḥ] |




madhyāhna naimittika karma pravakṣyāmyatha sundari |
saṅgavī saptanāḍyādi dvādaśāntaṃ manūttamaḥ ||

śuddhātmā bhūsuraissārdhaṃ svanityāni samāpya ca |
deśakālau ca saṃkīrtya tava putraṃ yajetparam ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācya varayeduktavadbhūsurottamān |
guruṃ ca dhanadaṃ caiva sadasyaṃ sarvato'dhikam ||

secakān rudrasaṃkhyāṃśca vāhakaṃ vedavādikam |
<p. 177> sūktakān pūjakān devi pṛthak pañca dvijottamān ||

mantrapallavasaṃyuktān jāpakān pañcaviṃśatiḥ |
pradakṣiṇa janān pañca natyarthaṃ pañca bhūsurān ||

dharmaśāstra pravaktāraṃ jyotiṣajñaṃ janapriye |
varayettava mūlena kṛcchraṃ dadyātsvaśaktitaḥ ||

rudrasaṃkhyadvijaissārdhaṃ yaṣṭvā rudraghaṭaṃ punaḥ |
sudhārasairamākumbhaṃ nyastvā tadvidhinārcya ca ||

liṅgaṃ sudhārasaissiñcet te sarve nyāsapūrvakāḥ |
rudramantrāmbadantasthe pṛthagaikādaśāhutiḥ ||

secayitvā janairliṅgaṃ mantrajālān japet kramāt |
caturvedān gehadikṣu vidikṣvastramanūn punaḥ ||

brahmāstraṃ vāruṇāstraṃ ca tavāstraṃ cakrasaṃjñakam |
ramāmantraṃ kumāryākhyamambikā manusaṃyutam ||

navadurgāmanuṃ devi vāhanākhyaṃ gaṇeśvaram |
skandaṃ ca vīrabhadraṃ ca vāyuputramanuṃ guruḥ ||

pālike parivārākhyaṃ tava mūlamanuṃ tadā |
<p. 178> caṇḍīṃ saptaśatīṃ tatra vivārṇajñā dvijottamāḥ ||

tava saṃkhyāśca te proktāḥ sāmpradāyavidastadā |
ramākumbhasya pūrvādi paritaḥ pariveṣṭitāḥ ||

japeyustaṃ spṛśeyuste secanāntaṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ |
nati pradakṣiṇaṃ caiva dīpāntaṃ parivartayet ||

dharmaśāstra purāṇāni vācayitvā tadagrake |
rudrakumbhodakenānte liṅgaṃ secya manūttamaḥ ||

naivedyaṃ vividhaṃ sthāpya dīpajālāṃśca darśayet |
trayodaśe ca nāḍyādau siñcetpañcāmṛtaiḥ punaḥ ||

pañcapuṣpaissamabhyarcya ramāsūktena tatparam |
kumāryāścaiva sūktena devīsūktena mantravit ||

ambikāsūktamanunā ghanasārodakādikaiḥ |
vanākhyāyāśca sūktena ramākumbhodakena ca ||

ramānāmasahasrādyaiḥ kumārīnāmabhistadā |
sahasrairambikāyāśca mahiṣāntakyāścanāmabhiḥ ||

gāyatrīnāmabhiḥ paścāt sahasraiḥ kusumākṣataiḥ |
<p. 179> yaṣṭvā saṃprārthya bahudhā liṅgamudrāṃ pradarśayet ||

naivedyaṃ dhūpadīpādyamarpayitvā vidhānataḥ |
ghaṭikātithisaṃkhyāntaṃ bhaktyā pūjāṃ vidhāya ca ||

mahāpūjā prakartavyā ramākalpoktavadguruḥ |
pūjayetsopacārādyaistava mūlā janā tadā ||

śālyānnaṃ sūpaśākādyaṃ ghṛtabhakṣasamanvitam |
pāyasaṃ lehya coṣyādi ṣaḍrasaiḥ saṃyutaṃ priye ||

devasyatveti mantreṇa puṣpākṣatajalānvitaḥ |
nivedayettato devyai dvārācchādanapūrvakam ||

pālike vāhanaṃ yaṣṭvāhavirdadyātsvanāmabhiḥ |
brahmaṇā dhanadenāryaḥ sadasyena sadehavat ||

yāgabhūmiṃ tato gatvā kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍilādike |
samidājyaiśca payasā sarpiṣā caruṇā saha ||

tava mūlena juhuyāt aṣṭottaraśatāhutiḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ hunedante homakarma samāpya ca ||

taccheṣaṃ ca samādāya mūlamantraṃ vadan guruḥ |
<p. 180> liṅgagneyyāṃ ca nikṣipya sampātājyasamanvitaḥ ||

saṃkṣālya hastau pādau ca ācamya saha bhaktitaḥ |
ghaṇṭātāḍana pūrvādi dvārasyācchādanaṃ tyajet ||

naivedyaṃ visṛjedante tatra saṃkṣālya tatparam |
prasannārghyaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ tatvārcya gururācamet ||

kapilāṃgāṃ samānīya pradakṣiṇavaśāttataḥ |
savatsāṃ maṇḍanaiyuktāṃ mūlenāgre yajettadā ||

nirīkṣyārcya havirdatvā prārthayitvā vidhānataḥ |
vedamaṅgalaghoṣādyaiḥ pañcavādyaravaissaha ||

sadbhaktairgāyakādyaiśca vandimāgadhapāṭakaiḥ |
prabhūttamairjanaissārdhaṃ dīpaughān bhrāmayedguruḥ ||

dhūpaṃ dadyātpaṭākāraṃ dīpaṃ vṛttena bhrāmayet |
trivāraṃ caikadīpaṃ ca pañcavāraṃ śarākhyakān ||

sapta śeṣākhyakān devi cakradīpāṃstathaiva ca |
navavāraṃ bhaumadīpān daśa nīrājanaṃ tataḥ ||

trivāraṃ caiva candrākhyaṃ tattanmantrān vadan budhaḥ |
<p. 181> pṛthak saṃbhrāmayedante mantrapuṣpārcanānvitaḥ ||

parivārān samabhyarcya dhūpadīpādikaiḥ param |
bhūtānāhūya ghaṇṭādyaiḥ nāḍyānaṣṭādaśa kramāt ||

dvārādi balipīṭhāntaṃ baliṃ dadyāt svanāmabhiḥ |
kṣetrapāya tato datvā tattanmantreṇa deśikaḥ ||

tadante cotsavaṃ kuryādutsavānte tadagrake |
tava bimbaṃ paribhrāmya padmadīpena suvrate ||

tatpāṅkaṇaṃ praviśyātha niśāmantreṇa mantravit |
niśātoyaṃ samādāya tava beraṃ triśaḥpunaḥ ||

tava mantraṃ vadan bhrāmya visṛjedvāmagocare |
tava bimbaṃ praveśyāntaḥ pādaprakṣālanāditaḥ ||

liṅgasya dakṣiṇe nyastvā taccaitanyaṃ niyojayet |
arghyaṃ datvā prasannārthaṃ candradīpaṃ pradarśya ca ||

karmajālaṃ samarpyānte tava mūlaṃ japecchatam |
pādodakaṃ tu nirmālyaṃ viprāṇāṃ karmacāriṇām ||

bhaktānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ dadyādvāhana dakṣiṇe |
<p. 182> valabhyāṃ bandhayedvāraṃ nāḍyaṃ viṃśati deśikaḥ ||

caturviṃśati nāḍyānta ātitheyādi bhūsurān |
dampatiṃ kanyakāścaiva vipramukhyān nagātmaje ||

kṛśānandhāṃśca badhirān daridrāṃśca kuṭumbinaḥ |
ananyagatikāṃścaiva bhojayettoṣya bhaktitaḥ ||

karmasaptati vikhyātaṃ madhyāhnāntaṃ naittikam |
sarvapāpaharaṃ divyaṃ rājyakośābhivṛddhidam ||

sarvarogaharaṃ saukhyaṃ tāpatrayavināśakam |
trailokyavaśyadaṃ devi bhūpatervibhavapradam ||

dehānte tava rūpatvapradaṃ sāyujyadaṃ priye |

[iti śrīvātulottare durgā bhede nityakarmakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
ghaṭibhede navadurgamadhyāhna nityakarmavivṛti vidhānanirūpaṇaṃ 
nāma viṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ] |




<Page ṇo. 182 is missing>

<p. 183> sāyādi bhūrūrātrāntaṃ karmajālaṃ ca naittikam |
pravakṣyāmi nagodbhūte vistarācchṛṇu sāṃpratam ||

viprottamaiścārakādyaiḥ sārdhaṃ snātvā gurūttamaḥ |
tava gehaṃ praviśyātha bherīśaṅkharavādibhiḥ ||

saptaviṃśati nāḍyāśca dvārabandhaṃ tyajetpunaḥ |
saṃmārjya prokṣya garbhādi sarvatra kṣālayettadā ||

prājāpatyādi mantreṇa puṇyāhaṃ vācya bhūsurān |
varayeddeśikādīṃśca tava mūlena suvrate ||

deśikaṃ dhanadaṃ caiva brahmāṇaṃ sarvabodhakam |
tvaritākhyaṃ rudrasaṃkhyān balyarthaṃ bimbadhārakam ||

sūktārtha nāmapūjārthaṃ pañca pañca pṛthak dvijān |
pradakṣiṇārthaṃ natyarthaṃ ṣaḍghanān varayet param ||

dharmaśāstrapurāṇāya nyāyaśāstrāya caṇḍike |
dvau viprau varayetpaścāccārārthaṃ daśa bhūsurān ||

tvaritākhyaghaṭaṃ yaṣṭvā śāradākhyaṃ sudhārasān |
samarcyoktavidhānena rudrasaṃkhyādvijāstadā ||

<p. 184> namo rudrebhya ityādi vadanti nyāsapūrvakam |
siñcetsudhārasairliṅgaṃ punaḥ śuddhodakairdvijāḥ ||

tadghaṭasthodakenānte gurustadvidhinārcayet |
ardhāmbikeśamantreṇa sahasrairnāmabhiḥ param ||

bahūnyantāni naivedyaṃ vividhaṃ bhakṣasaṃyutam |
pāyasaṃ sūpaśākādyaṃ kṣīramadhvājyasaṃyutam ||

nāḍyāṃ caikonaviṃśatyāṃ samarpya tava mūlataḥ |
darśayeddīpajālāṃśca mantraiḥ puṣpārcanaṃ param ||

tato godhūlikākāle triṃśannāḍyāvasānake |
ghaṭiyantraṃ samuddhārya tanmūlamanunā guruḥ ||

gosūktaṃ pravadan secya samarcoktavidhānataḥ |
bhūṣitāṅgān samānīya lakṣaṇairvatsasaṃyutām ||

gehaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samarcyokta havirdadet |
naivedyamarcya mūlena dīpajālāṃ pradarśayet ||

pañcapuṣpārpaṇāntā ca balidīpanatiṃ tadā |
punaḥ sudhārasaiḥ siñcedrātrisūktena mantravit ||

<p. 185> caṇḍikāyāśca sūktena vāṇīsūktena tatparam |
sūktena bhuvaneśvaryāḥ liṅgaṃ saṃsecayetpṛthak ||

vāṇīghaṭodakenānte secayitvārcayet tataḥ |
vāṇīkalpoktavanmantrī pūjākarma samācaret ||

pārāyaṇaṃ dikṣu kuryātpurāṇa paṭhanādikam |
vidiṅmantran caṇḍikādi vāṇī vāgīśasaṃjñakān ||

pratyaṅgiraḥ pañcadurgāṃ mṛtyostulyaṃ ca sundarīm |
rakṣāghnaṃ vāstumantraṃ ca dhāraṇākhyāṃ ca bālakām ||

indrākṣīṃ śivakavacaṃ tu sādhyanāmā sahaiva tu |
natiṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ caiva bhūtāhvānāntamucyate ||

caṇḍikānāmabhiścādau śāradāyāśca nāmabhiḥ |
sahasrairvākyanāyakyā lalitānāmabhiḥ param ||

sahasraiśca pṛthak pūjya japenmūlamanuṃ gatam |
tato nāḍyā trayastriṃśe naivedyaṃ vividhaṃ guruḥ ||

samarpya tava mūlena dīpaughān bhrāmayettadā |
pañcapuṣpāṇi mūlena datvā natvā vidhānataḥ ||

<p. 186> tadante ca mahāpūjāṃ nyāsajālādi māntrikaḥ |
mātṛkāṃ golakānyāsamadhyanyāsaṃ ca pīṭhakām ||

ṣoḍānyāsādi sarvāṇi vidhāya tava mūlataḥ |
yaṣṭvā liṅgaṃ ca bahubhiḥ patraiḥ puṣpaistilākṣataiḥ ||

upacāratvasaṃkhyāyāṃ dhūpadīpasamanvitam |
nivedayettato devyā homagehaṃ praviśya ca ||

mūlena samidājyādi havi vrīhīn payastilān |
dūrvāgraṃ ca hunedagnau śatamaṣṭottarāhutiḥ ||

vyāhṛtyā mūlamanunā punaḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ hunet |
homaśeṣaṃ gave datvā sampātājyasamanvitaḥ ||

prakṣālitāṅghrī hastau ca mantreṇācamanīyakam |
nītvā dvāraṃ ca yaṣṭvāryaḥ tadvastraṃ ca visarjayet ||

havirudvāsya taccheṣaṃ muṇḍinyai ca pradāpayet |
prasannārghyaṃ ca datvānte tāmbūlānyarpya bhaktitaḥ ||

raṅgapūjā prakartavyā dhvajāntaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
purāntaṃ gopurāntaṃ vā gehadvārāvasānakam ||

<p. 187> pārśvayoḥ paritaścaiva bahudīpān prabodhayet |
bodhayeccandradīpāntaṃ dīpajālān vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

mantraiḥ puṣpaṃ samarcya(rpyā)nte parivārān yajettadā |
jayaghaṇṭādikairvādyairbhūtānāhūya mūlataḥ ||

aṣṭatriṃśadguruṃ nāḍyāṃ balidānaṃ svanāmabhiḥ |
datvā samāpya balyante vīṇākāhalanisvanaiḥ || 

pañcavādyaravaissārdhaṃ dutsavaṃ bahirāvṛtau |
nītvā bimbaṃ cotsavānte nijabhaktajanaissaha ||

catvāriṃśadvināḍyādau praviśyāsthānamaṇṭapam |
tadantarādibhedena pravakṣyāmi manohare ||

devāntaraṃ ca dīpākhyaṃ tadante deśikāntaram |
viprāntaraṃ ca daṇḍākhyaṃ pañcāntaramṛdāhṛtam ||

devāntaraṃ ca deveśi nyastvā saṃpūjayetpunaḥ |
dīpastambhau ca dīpākhye tava dakṣiṇavāmayoḥ ||

vinyasettāṃ devasaghyau dīpapātratrayānvitau |
sa gurorantare devi tathaiva guruvāhakau ||

<p. 188> pratiṣṭhetāṃ gurorvāme dhanadaśca pitāmahaḥ |
jyotiṣajña sama(da)syādi vipramukhyā vyavasthitāḥ ||

tava pṛṣṭhe gurośśiṣyāścārā vāhakadakṣiṇe |
punarviprāntare devi dakṣiṇe vedapāragāḥ ||

bimbapṛṣṭhe jāpakādyāḥ tadvāme stotravācakāḥ |
tavāgre pārśvayoścaiva dharmaśāstraprabodhakau ||

daṇḍāntare svarṇadaṇḍabibhrakau stotravācakau |
vyavasthitau ca taddakṣe pṛṣṭhe vāmapradeśake ||

vyavasthitāḥ śucirbhūtāḥ balidīpadharā janāḥ |
daṇḍāntarādadhasthāne tavāgre ca samasthale ||

pañcahastāntaraṃ tiryagghitvā bhūmiṃ sureśvari |
tatpārśvayordvayordevi paṅktirūpeṇa vāgyatāḥ ||

ardhahastāntaraṃ madhye paṅktisthānānyathākramāt |(mam) |
nītvā pṛthak pṛthak tatra sevakādi prabhedataḥ ||

chatrakau vāyupaṭakau maṅgalāyudhasaṃyutau |
daṇḍavījanamartyau ca gāyakau vāyubījakau ||

<p. 189> rūpayauvana saṃpanna cāmarastrīkadambakau |
tāmbūlabibhrake devi tāmbūlocchiṣṭapātrake ||

bibhrake jalapātre ca punarādarśadarśake |
balidīpadharau martyau puṣpavṛṣṭikarau narau ||

viprottamā dakṣapaṅktau vāmapaṅktau nṛpādikāḥ |
vādyakā nāṭyakā snigdhe gaṇikā nṛttavedikāḥ ||

vyavasthitāḥ payauḥ paṅktau gajāśvādyā dhvajānvitāḥ |
bhaktā bhaṭā darśakādyā tayoragre vyavasthitā ||

tato devīṃ samārādhyā caṇakaiśca phalādikaiḥ |
pakvaiśca vividhaissārdhaṃ dhūpadīpāyutaṃ guruḥ ||

naivedyaṃ cārpayettasmāttāmbūlaṃ dāpayetparam |
bhaumākhyān cakradīpāṃśca padmanīrājasaṃjñakam ||

himāntaṃ bhrāmayeddevīṃ manupuṣpārcanaṃ tataḥ |
sevājālaṃ ca vedādi śrāvayetprārthanādikam ||

namastubhyaṃ mahākāli siddhagandharvasevite |
rājño rājyābhivṛddhyarthaṃ ṛggṛcaṃ śṛṇu pārvati ||

<p. 190> mahālakṣmi namastubhyaṃ brahmādi surasevite |
dhanakośābhivṛddhyarthaṃ yajurvedamanuṃ śṛṇu ||

sarasvati namastubhyaṃ sarvadevagaṇānvite |
kīrtikānti subhikṣārthaṃ sāmagānaṃ śṛṇu priye ||

mūrtitrayātmakaṃ devi namastubhyaṃ maheśvari |
atharvaṇa ṛcaṃ śrutvā vāñchitārthaṃ prayacchame ||

kramādvedamanūn śrāvya puṣpaṃ dadyāt pṛthak pṛthak |
vedānte gāyakau gāne śaṅkhanādaṃ tadantare ||

maukhīcakravādyāni pañcavādyāni tatparam |
vādayedgativādyāni nartanaṃ bharatoktataḥ ||

nartako mardakaścaiva gāyako vaṃśakastathā |
tato mauralikāścaite pañcācāryāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||

rūpayauvanasaṃpannāstrī nāṭye kuśalānvitāḥ |
nartako mardakaścaiva tava dakṣe vyavasthitau ||

gāyako vaṃśakākhyaśca vāmabhāge vyavasthitau |
agre mauralikā saumye vādayenmukhaṃ tathā ||

<p. 191> gaṇikā nartanaṃ kuryuḥ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāḥ |
nartanante padmadīpān vakṣyamāṇa vidhānataḥ ||

svarṇadaurpaṇa tāmrāttha dīpapātrāṇi kārayet |
tālamānāni vṛttāni dalāṣṭakayutāni ca ||

karṇikābhiḥ saṃyutāni teṣāṃ madhye vicakṣaṇaḥ |
navapātrāṇi vā sapta pañca trīṇyekameva vā ||

teṣu dīrghān prabodhyānte trivāraṃ bhrāmayetpriye |
divā rātrau sahārcāyāṃ gaṇikā nṛttalālasāḥ ||

gānaśāstroktamārgeṇa bhrāmayeyuḥ striśaḥ param |
dīpānte sarvavādyāni vādayeyuśca vādakāḥ ||

caṇakādiphalaṃ pakvaṃ navadhā vibhajetpunaḥ |
tava nirmālyadānānte deśikādi prabhedataḥ ||

ekabhāgaṃ deśikāya bhāgārdhaṃ vāhakāya ca |
dvau bhāgau vipramukhyebhyaḥ nṛpādibhyastathaiva ca ||

trīn bhāgān sevakādibhyaḥ pradadyāddaṇḍadhārakaḥ |
ardhabhāgaṃ svayaṃ gṛhya sevājālaṃ samāpya ca ||

<p. 192> sevājālaṃ samanteta (**********) |
(***) tava bimbaṃ ca prāṅkaṇaṃ praviśedguruḥ ||

pradakṣiṇavaśāttatra mūlamantraṃ vadan priye |
tato gānena niśśabdaiśśanairgehaṃ tavātmakam ||

catvāriṃśatpañcanāḍyāṃ bimbamantaḥ praveśya ca |
tava bimbātu caitatanya mūlaliṅge niveśayet ||

punaḥ pañcopacārādyairarcayitvā maheśvarīm |
kṣīrānnaṃ ca kaśāyotthaṃ jalatāmbūlasaṃyutam ||

samarcya tava mūlena nīrājyaṃ himasaṃjñakam |
dīpaṃ pradarśayitvāryaḥ prasannārthaṃ yajetvaram ||

kriyānyūnātiriktārthaṃ suviprāya svaśaktitaḥ |
kṛcchraṃ datvā pratoṣyātha karmajālaṃ samarpayet ||

bhaktānāṃ parivārāṇāṃ sevakādi nṛṇāṃ priye |
kpāthodakaṃ tu nirmālyaṃ dadyāt tvalliṅgavāmake ||

catvāriṃśat saptanāḍyāṃ kavāṭaṃ bandhayedguruḥ |
parito geharakṣārthaṃ śatasaṃkhyajanāḥ param ||

<p. 193> vaseyuśca vinidrāste gṛhaṃ gacchenmanuttamaḥ |
viṃśottaraṃ śataṃ karma niśārdhe samyagīritam ||

sampajjālapradaṃ saukhyaṃ rājño rājñāṃ ca vaṃśadam |
nityaṃ kālatrayepyevamuktaṃ karma nagodbhave ||

niyamenaiva yaḥ kuryāt kasyaṃ puṇyaṃ vadāmyaham |
aihike ca bahūn bhogān subhikṣaṃ ca jayaṃ yaśaḥ ||

prāpnuyātsārvabhaumatvaṃ saptajanmasya saṃśayaḥ |
dehānte tava rūpatvaṃ mamatvaṃ ca manohare ||

rahasyaṃ tatra vakṣyāmi trikāle nāḍi bhedataḥ |
kāle kāle prakartavyamuktaṃ karmavicakṣaṇaḥ ||

kālātīte niṣphalaṃ syāddurbhikṣaṃ rājyanāśanam |
tasmād jñātvā mayoktena nāḍī bhedena pārvati ||

kāle samyagguruḥ kuryāt kriyājālamatandritaḥ |
saptadeveṣvidaṃ karma kartavyaṃ manuvittamaḥ ||

pṛthak tanmantrabhedena kriyābhedena śāmbhavi |
karmapūrṇaphalāvāptyai rājñaḥ śreyo'bhivṛddhaye ||

<p. 194> tvalliṅge nityakarmārthaṃ pratyabde dakṣiṇāṃ kramāt |
dhanadādi pravakṣyāmi sevakāntaṃ maheśvari ||

viṃśottaraśataṃ niṣkaṃ dhanadāya pradāpayet |
śataniṣkaṃ deśikāya karmopadraviṇaissaha ||

niṣkasaptatikaṃ devi brahmaṇe dāpayetparam |
śatārdhaṃ ca sadasyāya sarvabodhakakarmiṇe ||

catvāriṃśadvāhakāya jyotiṣajñāya tatparam |
ghaṭikāsthāpanārthāya triṃśaniṣkaṃ kalādhare ||

dharmaśāstrapurāṇāni vaktṛbhyāṃ pañcaviṃśati |
viṃśanniṣkaṃ caturvedaghoṣakebhyaḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||

śrīrudrajāpakebhyaśca niṣkaṃ caikonaviṃśakam |
dadyātteṣāṃ sevanārthaṃ pṛthak dadyāt prabhūttamaḥ ||

niṣkaṃ cāṣṭādaśa proktaṃ sūktakebhyaḥ sureśvari |
tvannāmapūjakebhyaśca niṣkamaṣṭādaśaṃ pṛthak ||

saptaśatyādi viprebhyaḥ niṣkasaptadaśa kramāt |
jāpakebhyaḥ kalāsaṃkhyaniṣkaṃdadyācca tatparam ||

<p. 195> pradakṣiṇa namaskāra kṛdbhyaśca tithisaṃkhyakam |
pṛthak kumbhārcakebhyaśca taddravyai saha buddhimān ||

niṣkaṃ caturdaśaṃ proktaṃ cārakebhyaśca tatparam |
pṛthak daṇḍadharābhyāṃ ca niṣkaṃ dvādaśa ucyate ||

daśaniṣkaṃ chatrakebhyaḥ paṭakebhyastathaiva ca |
daṇḍavījanamartyānāṃ nava niṣkaṃ ca dāpayet ||

viṃśanniṣkaṃ gāyakābhyāṃ vījakābhyāṃ navaṃ pṛthak |
vārastrīṇāṃ cāmarādi pṛthak ṣaṇṇiṣkamucyate ||

balidīpajanebhyaśca pañcaniṣkaṃ punaḥ param |
dhūpadhūmakarābhyāṃ ca caturniṣkaṃ prabhūttamaḥ ||

niṣkatrayaṃ puṣpavṛṣṭikṛdbhyāṃ datvātha sundari |
vandibhyo māgadhebhyaśca pāṭakebhyaḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||

daśaniṣkaṃ pradātavyaṃ niṣkaṃ sārdhaśataṃ punaḥ |
vādyakebhyo nṛttavidbhyaḥ pṛthak dadyācca vetanām ||

vaijantī bibhṛkebhyantrī niṣkaṃ caiva vallabhe |
rakṣakebhyo gajāśvādīn pṛthak dvādaśasaṃkhyakān ||

<p. 196> dvārādi pālakebhyaśca dadyācca manusaṃkhyakam |
dravyāmbara dhanāgāra pālakebhyastrayodaśa ||

nava niṣkaṃ cātitheyādyannakṛdbhyaḥ pradāpayet |
paritaḥ parivārāṇāmarcakebhyaścaturdaśa ||

niṣkaṃ pṛthak pradatvānte toṣayedbahudhā janān |
tvadgeharakṣakādibhyaḥ saptaniṣkaṃ nageśvari ||

sarvamuktaṃ mayādevi dakṣiṇā karmacāriṇām |
cārādi sevakādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ vetanaṃ kramāt ||

pratyabdaṃ niyamānetthaṃ datvā toṣya mahājanān |
bhaktyā natvā natyakarma kartavyamiti vācayet ||

karma nyūnaṃ na kartavyaṃ kāle kāle ca deśikaḥ |
yadi nyūne tu saṃprāpte rājasampatkṣayo bhavet ||

tasmānmantrī muhurjñātvā sarvalokahitāya ca |
dhanadhānyādi vṛddhyarthaṃ subhikṣārthaṃ varānane ||

sampadrājyasamṛddhyarthaṃ sarvabhogārthameva vā |
nyūnaṃ vinā prakartavyaṃ kriyājālaṃ nagātmaje ||

[iti śrīvātulottare durgābhede nityakarmakāṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde 
ghaṭibhede navadurganiśi karmavivṛtinirūpaṇaṃ nāma ekaviṃśati 
paṭalaḥ ]




devadeva jagannātha lokānugrahakāraka |
malliṅge prātarārabhya nityārcanavidhiḥ śrutaḥ ||

samyaguktastvayā deva sarvalokahitāya ca |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi kāmyārthaṃ sādhakāya ca ||

naimittikārcanavidhiḥ vaktavyaḥ parameśvara |

śivaḥ (-)

tatsarvaṃ tava vakṣyāmi yattvayā paripṛcchitam ||

tvalliṅge ca viśeṣeṇa kāmyārthaṃ tava pūjanam |
vidhānaṃ vistarāddevi śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasaḥ ||

sarvavṛddhikaraṃ loke śrīkaraṃ putrapautradam |
akālamṛtyuśamanaṃ śatruṇāṃ vijayo bhavet ||

<p. 198> sarvavyādhipraśamanaṃ āyurvṛddhikaraṃ nṛṇām |
sādhakānāṃ tvayā devi kalau sidhyati nānyataḥ ||

smaraṇāddarśanāccaiva trayolokaścakampire |
bhaktyā saṃpūjite liṅgaṃ trailokyaṃ ca vaśī bhavet ||

cintitārtha pradaṃ nṝṇāṃ bhogamokṣapradaṃ śive |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kāmyārthaṃ tava pūjanam ||

kasyāṃ tithau ca pūjāyāṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati |
māghe vā phālgune māse caitre vaiśākhamāsake ||

jyeṣṭhamāse cāśvayuje kārtikeṣṭaśubhe dine |
dvitīyā pañcamī caiva aṣṭamī navamī śubhā ||

daśamī ca trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ ca paurṇamī |
somo bṛhaspatiḥ śukraḥ bhaumavāre śubhaṃ bhavet ||

candratārānukūle ca pūjādravyaḥ samāharet |
prātarutthāya vidhivat nityapūjāṃ vidhāya ca ||

brāhmaṇaiḥ sahasaṃyuktaḥ deśikādi janaiḥ priye |
nadīsnānaṃ prakartavyaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ bījapūrvakam ||

<p. 199> likhatvā tīrthamadhye tu snānārthaṃ tarpaṇaṃ caret |
tava prītyai ca saṃkalpya nimajjecca trivārakam ||

dhautavastrāmbaracchannaḥ vibhūtyādyairalaṅkṛtaḥ |
mūlamantraṃ japettatra aṣṭāviṃśatisaṃkhyakaiḥ ||

svarṇapātrādi saṃpūrya tava mūlena caṇḍike |
tattīrthaṃ ca samādāya aghamarṣaṇamantrataḥ ||

vedamaṅgalapāṭhaiśca nṛttagītādi vādyakaiḥ |
devālayamalaṅkṛtya phalapallavatoraṇaiḥ ||

siñceyurgandhavāribhyaḥ sthalaśuddhiṃ vidhāya ca |
bhūṣayitvā gṛhaṃ gatvā vandayetparamāṃ śivām ||

namaste devi kalyāṇi vāñchitārthapradaṃ śive |
naimittikasya pūjārthaṃ anujñāṃ bhaktavatsale ||

iti saṃprārthya sadbhaktyā tadagre sthāpayetphalam |
nirvighnārthaṃ ca saṃpūjya gaṇeśaṃ ca samārabhet ||

nāndīṃ saṃvācya puṇyāhaṃ dravyaśuddhiṃ tu kārayet |
karmārthaṃ varayetprājñaḥ deśikādi dvijottamān ||

<p. 200> karmajālaṃ prakartavyaṃ śrīrudraṃ pāvamānakam |
lakṣmīsūktaṃ prakartavyaṃ tattatkalpoktamārgataḥ ||

secayettava liṅgaṃ ca pañcāmṛtavidhānataḥ |
prasthādi droṇamānāntaṃ dravyasaṃkhyamitīritam ||

vidhinā secayenmantrī liṅgaśuddhiridaṃ bhavet |
devāgre cerābhāge ca racaye padmamuttamam ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ devi tadardhaṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ |
nava dvādaśa pañcaiva sthāpayetkumbhamuttamam ||

vidhinā pūrayetpaścāt ratnāṇīṃ gandhavāribhiḥ |
kṣīraṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ ca kaṣāyaṃ ca sutailakam ||

phalodakaiśca vividhaiḥ savastrapratimānvitaiḥ |
caturthyante ca mantreṇa devamāvāhya pūjayet ||

kalāmantrairmūlamantrai spṛṣṭvā japya gurūttamaḥ |
gaṇeśaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ ca mṛtyuñjayanavagraham ||

adhorāstraṃ saptaśatiṃ navadurgāmanuṃ śive |
tava mūlena mantreṇa sahasraṃ saṃjapedguruḥ ||

<p. 201> pradakṣiṇanamaskāraṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ priye |
kuṇḍaṃ vā sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryāccaiśabhāge viśeṣataḥ ||

jananāgniṃ ca saṃsthāpya mūlamantreṇa homayet |
śāntidurgāmanuṃ paścāttadardhaṃ juhuyādguruḥ ||

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ devi sahasraṃ cottamaṃ bhavet |
samidājyaṃ pāyasaṃ vā saptadravyeṇa homayet ||

arpaṇaṃ mūlamantreṇa sugandhapayasāmbubhiḥ |
pūrṇahutiṃ tataḥ kuryāt abhiṣekaṃ samācaret ||

snāpayedghaṭatoyena tava liṅgaṃ manūttamaḥ |
snānaṃ vicitratailena sugandhena manoharaiḥ ||

udvartya piṣṭacūrṇaiśca niśāpūrṇairmaheśvari |
uṣṇodakena saṃsthāpya kastūrīkuṅkumodakaiḥ ||
