aṣṭagandhaṃ samālipya akṣatāṃśca manoharān |
tilakaṃ ca sa kastūrī haridrācūrṇamañjanaiḥ ||

paṭṭavastraiḥ svarṇayutaiḥ navatantusamudbhavaiḥ |
vastradvayaṃ tato dadyāt kañcukaṃ ca samarcayet ||

<p. 202> muktāvaiḍūryakhacitatāṭaṅkaṃ bhūṣaṇādibhiḥ |
sugandhapuṣpamālābhiḥ pūgapuṣpaṃ sarpayet ||

āvāhanādi saṃpūjya ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ |
sahasrairnāmabhiḥ pūjya praṇavādi samanvitaiḥ ||

bilvapatraiśca kamalaiḥ droṇapuṣpaistilākṣataiḥ |
mallikādyaiśca puṣpaiśca pūjayenmantravittamaḥ ||

tadante svarṇajaṃ devi yathā śaktyā samarpayet |
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ ṣaḍrasānnaṃ sabhakṣakam ||

phalaṃ nānāvidhaṃ devi sūpaśākasamanvitam |
pānīyaṃ ca sakarpūraṃ tāmbūlaṃ ca samarpayet ||

nīlāñjanaṃ mantrapuṣpaṃ stotrapāṭhaṃ gurūttamaḥ |
balidānaṃ tato dadyānnaimitye ca viśeṣataḥ ||

brāhmaṇān bhojayeddevi homasaṃkhyān maheśvari |
dampatīn pūjayetpūrvaṃ navasaṃkhyasuvāsinīḥ ||

sahasraṃ cottamaṃ devi śatamaṣṭottaraṃ pṛthak |
gurupatnīṃ samāyukta pativratadharāstriyaḥ ||

<p. 203> tailābhyaṅgaṃ vidhāyātha śucirbhūtvā pṛthak pṛthak |
tavālayasya paritaḥ paṅktirūpaṃ supīṭhake ||

upaviśya yathānyāyaṃ gurupūjāṃ samācaret |
mama rūpaṃ ca saṃpūjya tava rūpaṃ samanvitam ||

śivaṃ śaktiṃ samāvāhya pādyamācamanādikam |
vastrālaṅkārakaṃ dadyādubhayośca pṛthak pṛthak ||

yathāvibhavasāreṇa pūjayitvā yathākramam |
upāyanaṃ tato dadyātparamānnaṃ saśarkaram ||

apūpaṃ ca phalaṃ devi caṇḍikā priyatāmiti |
tataḥ suvāsinīpūjāṃ kuryādbhaktiyutaḥ priye ||

āvāhanārghyapādyādi pṛthak sahasranāmabhiḥ |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vāpi navasaṃkhyāni pārvati ||

tavamaṣṭottaraśataṃ navadurgāṃśca pūjayet |
nāmabhiśca caturthyantaiḥ pṛthak saṃpūjya dāpayet ||

nālikeraphalaṃ devi nāgavallidalairyutam |
pūgapuṣpaṃ niśāmūlaṃ taṇḍulaṃ kañcukādibhiḥ ||

<p. 204> suvastraṃ ca dadedbhaktyā mūlamantraṃ vadan guruḥ |
tava prītyā ca datvātha āśiṣaṃ pratigṛhya ca ||

bhojayedbhakṣa bhojyādyaiḥ tāmbūlaṃ ca dadat pṛthak |
rātrau saṃpūjayet paścāt raṅgapūjāṃ samācaret ||

gehalī toraṇādyeṣu dīpamugdhodaye priye |
balidānaṃ tato dadyāt naimitye cāhutirdadet ||

rathe vā śibikāyāṃ vā vāhane vā vibhūṣite |
utsavaṃ ca tataḥ kuryāt grāmasīmāntameva vā ||

praviśya devīṃ saṃpūjya kriyājālaṃ samarpayet |
tataḥ prāta samutthāya snānasandhyāditarpaṇam ||

vidhāya devatāgāraṃ tava liṅge maheśvari |
saṃpūjya snapayenmantrī karmalopārthamambike ||

tavamaṣṭottaraśataṃ japenmūlamanuṃ guruḥ |
savatsāṃ gāṃ tato dadyāt dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedguruḥ ||

bhūridānaṃ tato dadyāt kṛcchradānaṃ tataḥ param |
ityevaṃ kārayedbhaktyā brāhmaṇaśca nṛpottamāḥ ||

<p. 205> kāmitārtha pradaṃ śīghraṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ mayoditam |
manasā cintitaṃ kāryaṃ pūjayāśca prabhāvataḥ ||

sarvān kāmānavāpnoti vidhinā pūjite priye |
mahāpūjāvidhiḥ samyak tavasnehena coditam ||

[iti śrīvātulottare durgābhede śivapārvatīsaṃvāde nityakarmakāṇḍe 
naimittikapūjāvidhirnāma dvāviṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ] ||




|| svasti ||


devī (-)

devadeva mahādeva sarvajña karuṇānidhe |
acinyaguṇa rūpāḍhya śiva sarvajagatprabho ||

saptakoṭiṣu mantreṣu pratiṣṭhādyutsaveṣu ca |
vahnihomavidhiḥ proktaḥ tvayā guhyaḥ sadāśiva ||

pṛthak vidhānaṃ vahneśca havanaṃ ca kathaṃ vada |
kinnimittaṃ kathaṃ homaḥ kiṃ ca dravyaṃ ca tatphalam ||

<p. 206> tvanmukhācchrotumicchāmi vistareṇa vada prabho |

śivaḥ (-)

sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā devi guhyādguhyatamaṃ mahat ||

agnikāryaṃ suraśreṣṭhaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam |
mama tejomayo vahniḥ tavarūpaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

jiṃhvārūpeṇa nau devi tattaddravyaṃ hi bhujyate |
anyatra bhujyate dṛṣṭvā tṛpti bhavetpriye ||

etasmātkāraṇācchreṣṭhaṃ vahnihomaṃ ca kārayet |
rahasyaṃ paramaṃ devi muktidaṃ ca dvijanmanā ||

havyadattaṃ tu bhuñjīta yadi śreṣṭhaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
śūdratvaṃ karmahīnatvaṃ patitatvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

sāyaṃ pratarnityahomaṃ prakurvīta dvijottamaḥ |
ihāpi sukhamāpnoti ante sāmīpyamīśvari ||

lakṣahomādi koṭyantaṃ kāmyakarmaṇi kārayet |
homāddaśāṃśaṃ tanmantrajapasaṃkhyā maheśvari ||

śatāṃśaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryātsahasraṃ kumbhapūjanam |
<p. 207> ayutaṃ bhojayedviprān pradakṣiṇanamaskṛtim ||

dānāni vividhaṃ dadyāt gobhumyādi viśeṣataḥ |
itthaṃ kuryādvidhānajñaḥ sarvamṛtyunivāraṇam ||

sarvaśāntikaraṃ caiva śatrūṇāṃ nāśakāraṇam |
āyurārogyadaṃ dehe dejobalavivardhanam ||

lakṣmīpradaṃ viśeṣeṇa santānādi vivardhanam |
tuṣṭidaṃ puṣṭidaṃ rājñāṃ rājyakośavivardhanam ||

gajāśva sainyadurgādi vṛddhidaṃ kīrtidaṃ tathā |
divyantarikṣādyutpātadussvapnādi vināśanam ||

grahabhūtapiśācādi vināśanakaraṃ param |
bhuktidaṃ muktidaṃ caiva sarvavāñchāphalapradam ||

amunā homavidhinā bahuprīti bhavenmama |
brahmādyā mayi saṃtuṣṭe tuṣṭā devā na saṃśayaḥ ||

itthaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ homakarma kalau siddhipradāyakam |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kārayeddhomamarthavān ||

tadvidhānaṃ pravakṣyāmi lakṣahomavidhaiḥ pṛthak |
<p. 208> tatkālaṃ tatsthalaṃ kuṇḍamaṇṭapādi salakṣaṇam ||

dinasaṃkhyāṃ ca tatkarmaṇyācāryādīṃśca ṛtvijaḥ |
homadravyaṃ tatphalaṃ ca vistareṇa śṛṇu priye ||

kārtike māsi kurvīta paurṇamyādinduvāsare |
māghamāse kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ maheśvari ||

phālgune māsi kurvīta aṣṭamī daśamī dine |
caitre māsi trayodaśyāmekādaśyāmathāpi vā ||

vaiśākhe ca tṛtīyāyāṃ jyeṣṭhamāsi śubhe dine |
anyatpuṇyatithau mantrī grahaṇe ravisomayoḥ ||

amauḍhye nādhimāse ca candratārānukūlake |
śubhalagnagrahayute duṣṭagrahavivarjite ||

kartuścaivānukūlarkṣe śubhagrahanirīkṣite |
evaguṇayute kāle lakṣahomaṃ ca kārayet ||

sāmbaliṅgaśivakṣetre ārāme parvate taṭe |
udyāne śrotriyāgāre saṅgame ca nadītaṭe ||

anyatpuṇyasthale devi homaṃ kuryā nṛpottamaḥ |
<p. 209> tatsthalaṃ ca samāvīkṣya pūrve vahne tathaiśake ||

khananādi prakurvīta śodhayedduṣṭavarjite |
caturaśraṃ samaṃ kṛtvā ṣaṭtriṃśaddhastamātraka ||

ekaviṃśati hastaṃ vā saptottaradaśa priye |
tāvanmaṇṭapavistāraṃ yāvatkuṇḍamitirbhavet ||

ratnimātrasamutsedhaṃ darpaṇodarasannibham |
tattribhāgaikabhāgena maṇṭapasya maheśvari ||

kuṇḍāni racayenmantrī nava pañcaikameva vā |
pūrve ca caturaśraṃ syādāgneyāṃ tu bhagākṛtiḥ ||

ardhacandraṃ tu yāmyāyāṃ nair(-)ṛtyāṃ tu trikoṇakam |
vāruṇe vartulākuṇḍaṃ vāyavyāṃ ca ṣaḍaśrakam ||

uttare padmakuṇḍaṃ tu īśānyāmaṣṭakoṇakam |
aindra śāṅkarayormadhye vṛttakuṇḍaṃ tu kārayet ||

caturdikṣu ca catvāri caturaśraṃ tu kārayet |
vṛttakuṇḍaṃ tathaiśānyāṃ pañcakuṇḍe'thavā śive ||

kuṇḍamekaṃ ca kurvīta vṛttaṃ vā caturaśrakam |
<p. 210> īśāne pūrvabhage vā kalpayecca maheśvari ||

caturhastaṃ caikakuṇḍaṃ trihastaṃ pañcakuṇḍakam |
dvihastaṃ navakuṇḍaṃ ca tāvaṃ khātapramāṇataḥ ||

vistāraṃ tāvadūrdhvaṃ syādūrdhvamekhalayā saha |
kuṇḍasya yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ tādṛśī mekhalā bhavet ||

trimekhalaṃ ca yuktaṃ syādaṣṭaṣaṭcaturaṅgulaiḥ |
ṣaḍbhiścaturbhiśca tathā dvikara trikarasya tu ||

mekhalopari madhye tu yoniḥ pippalapatravat |
ṣaḍaṅgulasya vistārā vistārārdhaṃ tadūrdhvataḥ ||

muṣṭerevaṃ tathānyeṣāmaṅgulāṅgulavardhanāt |
ekāṅgulaṃ samutsedhamaṣṭāṅgulasavistṛtam ||

praṇītā sthānametaddhi brahmasthānaṃ tathā bhavet |
caturaśraṃ praṇītāyāṃ brahmaṇe padmameva ca ||

itthaṃ kuṇḍaṃ prakalpyātha lakṣaṇena maheśvari |
sthaṇḍile ca na kartavyaṃ devatāśāpadaṃ bhavet ||

alakṣaṇakṛtaṃ kuṇḍaṃ kulakṣayakaraṃ bhavet |
<p. 211> pūrvoktana vidhānena kuṇḍaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ ||

tanmadhye vedikāṃ kuryāddhastamātraṃ tadūrdhvataḥ |
catustambhaṃ ca tanmadhye parito dvādaśa kramāt ||

tadbahirviṃśatistambhāḥ unnataṃ cāṣṭa ṣaṭkaraiḥ |
tadbahi pañcahastaṃ syāt sthāpaye potikādibhiḥ ||

ekabhaumaṃ tribhaumaṃ vā kārayenmaṇṭapaṃ śive |
catudvārasamāyuktaṃ catustoraṇabhūṣitam ||

prāgdvāre pītavarṇaṃ ca dakṣiṇe kṛṣṇavarṇakam |
paścime raktavarṇaṃ ca uttare śvetavarṇakam ||

cakratriśūlaḍamarupadmanandivicihnitam |
evaṃ dvāraṃ prakurvīta pūrvādikramataḥ priye ||

caturdikṣu dhvajaṃ kuryātpatākāsyurdiśi sthitāḥ |
daśastodhvaje daṇḍaḥ caturaśraśca vṛttakaḥ ||

saptahastāḥ patākāḥsyuḥ dīrghadaṇḍapramāṇataḥ |
saptahastadhvajapaṭaḥ vasanā nūtanena ca ||

tattadvāhanacihnāni lekhayecchilpinā guruḥ |
<p. 212> viṃśatyaṅgulavistārāḥ patākā syurmaheśvari ||

lokapālānuvarṇena vāhanāyudhalāñchanaiḥ |
digvidikṣu prakurvīta sthāpayedvidhinā guruḥ ||

vastrairācchādayestuṅgān vitāna phalaśobhitān |
dvāre dvāre ca kumbhau dvau kumbhau tāraṇamūlakau ||

cūtapallavaśobhāḍhyau pratyekaṃ kaṇṭhavāsasau |
kadalīstambhavinyāsai sragdāmā cūtapallavaiḥ ||

śobhayitvā bahiḥ paścāddarbhadāmnā ca veṣṭayet |
evaṃ maṇṭapakuṇḍāni kurvīta vidhinā priye ||

navāhaṃ saptarātraṃ vā pañca tryekāhameva vā |
lakṣahomaṃ prakurvīta uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet ||

adhamaṃ ca na kurvīta dinasaṃkhyavidhau śive |
vapanaṃ snānamaunaṃ ca śuklāmbaradharaḥ śuciḥ ||

kṣīrānnabhojanaṃ nityaṃ dussaṅgādi vivarjitaḥ |
brahmacaryavrataṃ kuryādṛtvigbhiḥ saha deśikaḥ ||

itthaṃ dīkṣā prakartavyā navarātre maheśvari |
<p. 213> trirātre caikarātre ca dīkṣākarma na kārayet ||

yāgadravyāṇi sampādya hotṛbhiḥ jāpakān dvijān |
dīkṣāvān karmatatvajñaḥ satyavādī jitendriyaḥ ||

sapatnīkān dvijaśreṣṭhān āhūya yajamānakaḥ |
tailābhyaṅgādikaṃ kṛtvā ācāryartviksamanvitaḥ ||

sutithau ca suniścitya pūrvoktena maheśvari |
prātarutthāya vidhivaddantadhāvanapūrvakam ||

prātaḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta ācāryāya dvijottamaiḥ |
sandhyākarma prakurvīta nityakarma samāpya ca ||

mṛttikāsnānapūrvaṃ ca kuśagomayavāriṇā |
sarvaghoṣasamāyuktastattanmantreṇa lepayet ||

nānāmaṅgalavādyaiśca vedaghoṣaiśca suktakaiḥ |
snānaṃ kṛtvā tato mantrī homārthaṃ ca dvijottamaiḥ ||

dhautavastrāmbaracchannaḥ vibhūtyādyairalaṅkṛtaḥ |
devarṣitarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā namaskṛtvā divākaram ||

tato devālayaṃ kṛtvā phalapuṣpasuvarṇakaiḥ |
<p. 214> mamāgre vinyaseddevi yajamānaḥ subhaktitaḥ ||

pañcopacārairabhyarcya prārthayecca kṛtāñjaliḥ |
śivādi sarvadevānāṃ sadyaḥ tuṣṭikaraṃ bhavet ||

lakṣahomaṃ karomīti svāmivijñāpanaṃ caret |
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ praviśedyāgamaṇṭapam ||

prakṣālya pāṇipādau ca pūrve vā caiśakoṇake |
padmapīṭhaṃ ca saṃsthāpya navavastroparinyaset ||

tasyopari nyasau (*) bhān āsaneṣūpaviśya ca |
guruvandanapūrvādi prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret ||

dinamuddiśya saṃkalpya yathāvibhavavistaram |
vighneśamarcya nāndyādi puṇyāhaṃ pañcagavyakam ||

madhuparkaṃ vidhāyātha varayeddeśikādikān |
ācāryandhanaṃ devi brahmāṇaṃ ca sadasyakam ||

bhavādi mūrtipānaṣṭau hotṛbhiśca yathoktavat |
dinasaṃkhyamitān jñātvā varayecca pṛthak pṛthak ||

japārthaṃ tu śatārdhaṃ syādvedapārāyaṇaṃ śive |
<p. 215> ṣoḍaśa brāhmaṇaḥ kuryāt tarpaṇārthaṃ daśa dvijān ||

kumbhārcane caikamevaṃ namaskārapradakṣiṇe |
daśa viprāṃśca varaye"rarcārtha dvādaśa dvijān ||

ācāryādīn svaśākhādi pravaraprocya gotrakam |
śarmātha vṛṇuyātkartā ślokairebhiśca tān kramāt ||

sarvakarmadhurīṇastvaṃ śivatuṣṭyai dvijottamaḥ |
tvāmāhaṃ gurumācāryamasmin homavidhau vṛṇe ||

kartuḥ pratinidhitve tu dvijamanyaṃ tadājñayā |
ācāryo vṛṇuyādatra ślokenaiva tato'munā ||

kartuḥ pratinidhitvena karmakārayituṃ dvija |
tvāmahaṃ yajamānārthaṃ tasmin homavidhau vṛṇe ||

yathā caturmukho brahmā svargeloke pitāmaha |
brahmāṇaṃ cāmahaṃ sākṣāttasmin homavidhau vṛṇe ||

mantratantrādilopāṃśca draṣṭuṃ nasyuryathā tathā |
asmin homavidhau vipra sadasyaṃ tvāmahaṃ vṛṇe ||

homārthaṃ mūlamantrāṃśca tattanmantraṃ śivasya tu |
<p. 216> ṛtvijo'smin homavidhau dvijā yuṣmānahaṃ vṛṇe ||

tarpaṇārthaṃ ca pūjārthaṃ kalaśasya maheśvari |
mūlamantrajapārthāya vedapārāyaṇārthakam ||

pradakṣiṇa praṇāmārthaṃ dvijā yuṣmānahaṃ vṛṇe |
ityevaṃ varaṇaṃ kṛtvā tebhyaḥ pādyādikaṃ dadet ||

pādyamarghyaṃ tathācāmaṃ madhuparkaṃ tathaiva ca |
kuṇḍale cāṅgulīyādi bhūṣāśca vividhā api ||

rudrākṣamakuṭādyaiśca svarṇayajñopavītakaiḥ |
dukūlapādukaṃ devi chatravījanajāmaraiḥ ||

ācamaṃ jalapātrāṇi yāgopakaraṇāni ca |
phalaṃ svarṇaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ dāpayenmanunā śive ||

mūlena mama gāyatryā pañcabrahmāṇubhistathā |
ācāryāya pradatvauvaṃ gandhādyaistaṃ samarpayet ||

evaṃ brahma sadasyādīn tathaivānyān dvijān kramāt |
yathā yathocitaṃ vastrabhūṣaṇādyaiśca toṣayet ||

aṅkurārpaṇapūrvaṃ ca mṛttikāmāharettataḥ |
<p. 217> sadyoṅkuraṃ vā kurvīta dinasaṃkhyamitaṃ śive ||

dhvajasthāpanapūrvoktaṃ patākān sthāpayetpriye |
vṛṣayāgaṃ tataḥ kuryātpatākadhvajadevatāḥ ||

sampūjya vidhivanmantrī tattanmantreṇa pārvati |
pañcagavyaṃ hunedante gaṇeśaṃ ca navagrahān ||

tattanmūlena hotavyaṃ tattaddravyaṃ pṛthak pṛthak |
etatkarma divākāle guruḥ kuryānmaheśvari ||

sāyaṃ sandhyāmupāsyātha śucirbhūtvā gurūttamaḥ |
prakṣālya pāṇipādau ca mūrtipādyairdvijottamaiḥ ||

praviśya paścime dvāraṃ yāgagehaṃ maheśvari |
astradṛṣṭyā vilokyātha dikpatīn rakṣoṇadyattat ||

nai(-)ṛtyāṃ diśamāsādyā upaviśyāsane guruḥ |
guruvandanapūrvādi diśadigbandhanaṃ śive ||

bhūtaśuddhiṃ nyāsajālān saṅkalpaṃ ca vidhāya ca |
gaṇeśa sthaṇḍile pūjya sugandhakusumādibhiḥ ||

sthāpayetkalaśaṃ devi yojayetpañcagavyakaiḥ |
<p. 218> ekāṃśakaṃ tu gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ ca tadardhakam ||

saptāṃśakaṃ tu gokṣīraṃ tryaṃśakaṃ tu dadhi smṛtam |
ājyamekāṃśamityevaṃ sadyojātādi pañcakaiḥ ||

astramantreṇa saṃspṛṣṭvā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet |
puṇyāhaṃ vācayetpaścādṛtvigbhiḥ sahito guruḥ ||

maṇṭapaṃ mārjayeddevi vāyumantreṇa deśikaḥ |
pālāśapallavairdarbhaiḥ vahnyādīśāvasānakam ||

mama gāyatrimantreṇa lepayedgomayāmbhasā |
astramantraṃ śatāvṛtyā tato rājīśca deśikaḥ ||

vikīrya mārjayenmuṣṭyā maṇṭape manuvittamaḥ |
prakṣālyaṃ hastau deveśi puṣpāṇi daśa dikṣu ca ||

nikṣipya mama gāyatryā cakramudrāṃ pradarśayet |
sasūtradarbhamālāṃ ca yuktaṃ bodhidupallavaiḥ ||

astramantraśataṃ japtvā veṣṭayaṃ maṇṭapaṃ śive |
abhyukṣya pañcagavyena mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ ||

vikīrya mārjanaṃ mantrī punaḥ pūrvoktamantrataḥ |
<p. 219> gandhatoyena saṃprokṣya gururāpaḥ punantviti ||

dvārapūjantataḥ paścādakṣataiḥ kusumādibhiḥ | kaiḥ |
astratoyena saṃprokṣya pūrvādrikramataḥ priye ||

śānti bhūti balārogyā saṃjñābhistoraṇe nyaset |
yugmātvidānyajedūrdhve śūleṣvarkapuroditān ||

praśāntaśiśirādīṃśca pratyūṣādi vasūnapi |
bhūrbhuvādikalokāṃśca somabhaumādikān grahān ||

śāntirnidrānivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhādvāramūrdhani |
gaṇeśaṃ bhāratīṃ lakṣmīṃ sarvadvāropari nyaset ||

pūrvadvāraṃ samārabhya śāyayordakṣavāmayoḥ |
nandyādidvārapālāṃśca gaṅgādisaritastathā ||

saṃpūjya tān caturthyantān śivajñāṃ śrāvayedathā |
yajñasya rakṣaṇārthena sthātavyaṃ mahadādaraiḥ ||

vajrādīni patākā syuḥ kumudādi dhvajeṣu ca |
pūrvoktān vinyaseddevi sarvavighna bhayaṅkarān ||

dvāratoraṇasaṃpūjya prārthayenamunā guruḥ |
<p. 220> maṇṭapastambhapūjāṃ ca prārabhe tu tataḥ priye ||

antastambheṣu sarveṣu ādhārādi yathākramam |
praṇavādi namontaiśca dharmādiṣu samarcayet ||

vidyeśādi ghaṭaśceṣu dvāratoraṇakumbhakān |
yāgeśvaraṃ ca dikpālān sthāpayetpūjayetpriye ||

niṣkaṃ niṣkārdhahemnā vā pratimān vinyasedguruḥ |
suvastraṃ suphalaṃ nyastvā prārthayeddeśikottamaḥ ||

bho bho śakra tvayā svasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntaye |
sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ sthātavyaṃ tu śivājñayā ||

sarveṣāṃ lokapālānāṃ anekakramayogataḥ |
iti vijñāpya dikpālān yāgeśaṃ prārthayettataḥ ||

śaṅkarāya namastubhyaṃ viśvarūpadharāya ca |
mayā pravartitaścāyaṃ lakṣahomaḥ kṛte tava ||

lakṣaṇīyaḥ tvayaitarhi samāptiryāvadasya hi |
iti saṃprārthya yāgeśamakṣatairavakīrya ca ||

ataḥ saṃpūjya vāstuṃ ca rākṣoghnaṃ vāstuhomakam |
<p. 221> baliṃ ca vikireddevi svasvamantrairgurūttamaḥ ||

tataḥ pratisaraṃ badhvā yāgārthaṃ ca yathoktavat |
ācāryaṃ yajamānaṃ ca brahmāṇaṃ ca sadasyakam ||

mūrtipān tarpaṇaṃ devi yāgeśapratimādiṣu |
badhnīyurūrṇāsūtraiśca tanmantraṃ ca puroktavat ||

vahnisādhanakaṃ paścāt hotṛbhiḥ saha deśikaḥ |
sūryakāntodbhavaṃ śreṣṭhakāṣṭhajño madhyo bhavet ||

śrotriyāgārajo vahniradhamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
sūryakāntaśilāyāṃ vā bodhivṛkṣādivā śive ||

ādāya mantaḥ sārāṇi kṛtvā maraṇikadvayam |
saṃyogaḥ kramataḥ kuryāt śilpiśāstroktamārgataḥ ||

trihastarajjunā veṣṭyā sudṛḍhaṃ triguṇīkṛtam |
gomayālepitaṃ bhūmiṃ śatapatraṃ sukarṇikam ||

likhet saṃpūrayeccūrṇaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ vibhedataḥ |
śālisaṃpūrayetpīṭhaṃ droṇamātraṃ maheśvari ||

araṇiṃ vinyasetpīṭhe darbhadāmnā ca veṣṭayet |
<p. 222> gandhapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya ācāryo ṛtvijaiḥ saha ||

āvayośca samāvāhya naivedyāntaṃ maheśvari |
mūlamantrajapaṃ kuryātpañcabrahmādi sūktakaiḥ ||

mathitvā praṇavenāgniḥ tuṣakārpāsakādibhiḥ |
karīśaṃ ca samāyojya agnijātakaṇādikaiḥ ||

jvalayecca tato mantrī tāmrapātre śarāvake |
agnibījena caitanyaṃ hṛdā saṃhāramudrayā ||

ādāya yojayedbindau pūrakeṇa tato hṛdi |
sthāpayenmanunā devi agniśca citimantrataḥ ||

tejorūpamahaṃ vahniṃ sākṣādbrahmasvarūpakam |
evaṃ dhyātvā tato mantrī saṃpūjya ca hutāśanam ||

dāpayedguruṇā paścāt yajamānāya bhaktitaḥ |
devasyatvā tatoddhṛtvā ṛtvijāṃśca tato dadet ||

rakṣaṇīyaṃ dvijaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ homārthaṃ ca maheśvari |
ityuktvā yajamānāya kṛtāñjalipuṭa sthitaḥ ||

ācaryaṃ pūjayeddatvā śayyāvāhanabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
<p. 223> dhenuśca ṛtvijāṃ dadyāt athavā vāsasīṃ dadet ||

itthaṃ datvā tu deveśi vahniḥ suprītado bhavet |
tato jāgaraṇaṃ paścāt vedapāṭhaṃ dvijottamaiḥ ||

itthaṃ kṛtvā tu deveśi punaḥ sūryodayāntike |


[iti śrīvātulottare kāmyakāṇḍe umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde 
lakṣahomamaṇṭapādi vidhirnāma pañcacatvāriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] |




athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi karaṇānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam |
kuṇḍaśodhanapūrvaṃ ca vahnerjananakarma ca ||

pātrāṇāṃ sādhanavidhiṃ krameṇa śṛṇu pārvati |
ācāryastu śucirbhūtvā kṛtvā caivāhnikakriyām ||

ṛtvigbhiḥ saha deveśi yāgagehaṃ praviśya ca |
sampūjyādau gaṇeśānaṃ phalādyaistoṣayettataḥ ||

puṇyāhavācanaṃ kuryāt mūrtipaiśca dvijottamaiḥ |
gatvā kuṇḍāntikaṃ mantrī viṣṭare pūrvadiṅmukhaḥ ||

<p. 224> upaviśya samānīya sādhanānyuñcitāni vai |
guruvandanapūrvādi ātmaśuddhiṃ samācaret ||

tato juhvāṅgamanunā nyāsajālaṃ vidhāya ca |
tataḥ saṃpūjayenmūrdhni svagurūn deśikottamaḥ ||

hṛtpadme pūjayeddevaṃ gaṇeśānamathorcayet |
kuṇḍaśuddhiṃ prakurvīta vakṣyamāṇena vartmanā ||

mūlamantran japan mantrī tatkuṇḍaṃ ca samīkṣayet |
astramantreṇa saṃtāḍya daśadigbandhanaṃ tataḥ ||

saṃprākṣya varmaṇā mantrī tāreṇakhananaṃ śive |
mṛttike hana mantreṇa uddhare mṛttikāṃ tataḥ ||

pūjayecca hṛdā mantrī samīkṛtidṛśāṇunā |
secayedamunā kuryāt kuṭṭayedastramantrataḥ ||

mārjayetkavacenātha lepanaṃ hetināṇunā |
kalātmakaṃ kalābhistu trisūtrīkaraṇaṃ śive ||

kavacenārcayenmantrī hṛnmantreṇa ca pārvati |
astreṇa vajrīkaraṇaṃ hṛnmantreṇa kuśaistataḥ ||

<p. 225> catuṣpathaṃ tanutreṇa tanuyādakṣapāṭanam |
ityete kuṇḍasaṃskārāḥ aṣṭādaśa maheśvari ||

caturaśraṃ purā kṛtvā kuṇḍamadhye ca vālukaiḥ |
devasyatveti mantreṇa hrādakena śalākayā ||

ādāya ca likhedrekhāṃ pūrvāgre cottarāgrake |
tisraḥ pūrvamukhā rekhāstadvatsaumyamukhāstathā ||

brahmā vaivasvataḥ somo madhye dakṣiṇavāmayoḥ |
rudro viṣṇuśca śakraśca madhye paścimapūrvayoḥ ||

brahmajajñānamantreṇa nāke manu yathākramam |
ṣaḍrekhāṃ ca likhenmantrī bhūśuddhyarthamiheśvari ||

gandhapuṣpādibhiścaiva tato nāmnaiva pūjayet |
tato yantraṃ samuddhṛtya pūrvāgre cottarāgrake ||

bhavatyekona pañcāśatpadādyantra tadāsphuṭam |
bahirīśānakoṣṭhādi tanmadhya ca maheśvari ||

mātṛkāṃ varṇasaṃlekhya pradakṣiṇyena deśikaḥ |
tanmadhye'ṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ karṇikākesarairyutam ||

<p. 226> viṣṭarārthaṃ sukūrcaṃ tu viṃśaddarbhakṛtaṃ tataḥ |
pīṭhapūjāṃ ca tatraiva āvāhya puruṣaṃ param ||

tataḥ pārśve mahādevyā cintayecca guru priye |
dvinetrāṃ dvibhujāṃ ramyāṃ pīnīnnatapayodharām ||

muktābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ padmahastāṃ sulocanām |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśāṃ raktāvāhogribhūṣitām ||

ṛtusnātāṃ sukūrcasthāṃ vāgīśasya ca vāmake |
kāraṇaṃ jagatāṃ devaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvasākṣiṇam ||

anādinidhanaṃ śuddhaṃ sākṣādbrahmasvarūpiṇam |
evaṃ dhyātvā tato mantrī samarcya ca maheśvari ||

devadevau ca saṃpūjya mūlamantreṇa pārvati |
pādyamarghyādikaṃ dadyāt kuṅkumaṃ raktacandanam ||

raktapadmāni saṃpūjya dhūpadīpādikaṃ priye |
śivabījena tārādi namontena tataḥ param ||

tadekatāṃ samāpannāṃ bhūvayetnaṃ vicintayet |
darśayodyoni mudrāṃ ca liṅgamudrāṃ tathā hṛdā ||

<p. 227> ityevaṃ manasā bhāvyā krīḍārthaṃ ca pradarśayet |
tadā ratistrayoryāvat tāvadagnimathāṅguruḥ ||

pāṇinā sāmbhasā yoniṃ sa mṛjyādastramantrataḥ |
paścāddevasya devyāśca dadyādācamanīyakam ||

vadhnīyāṃ garbharakṣārthaṃ darbhaṃ kaṅkaṇavatkare |
ekīkṛtya tradarbheṇa badhnīyānmūlamantrataḥ ||

sādhitaṃ ca tato vahniṃ bhasmadhūmādivarjitam |
gurupatnyā ca ṛtvigbhyaṃ ādāya ca maheśvari ||

kuṇḍayonau pravinyasya devasyatveti mantrataḥ |
vahnibījajapan mantrī kravyādāśaṃ parityajet ||

astramantreṇa saṃprokṣya dakṣadruṣpādi vīkṣaṇaiḥ |
śoṣadāhapravanāni tatanmantreṇa pārvati ||

akuñcanaṃ tato dīptaṃ bahiḥ piṅgalayā guruḥ |
vyavargasya dvitīyā tu bindunā bhūṣitaṃ ca yat ||

vahnibījamidaṃ proktaṃ trivāraṃ ca samuccaran |
bibhāvya nirgataṃ tatra yojayedāntarā (*) nam ||

<p. 228> spṛṣṭvā darbhadvayenaiva japtvā tāreṇa cāṣṭaśaḥ |
dhenumudrāṃ pradṛśyātha rakṣayedastramantrataḥ ||

avakuṇṭhya tanutreṇa mūlamantreṇa pārvati |
pañcopacārairabhyarcya jānuyugmaṃ (***) ||

kuṇḍordhvaṃ triḥ paribhrāmya mūlamantramanusmaran |
tanmadhye vinyasedagniṃ ātmano'bhimukhaṃ guruḥ ||

saṃprokṣya kāṣṭhaśirasā nikṣipecca maheśvari |
citpi"galaṃ ca manunā jvalayedanalaṃ śanaiḥ ||

vyajanaṃ dhamanīṃ vātha praṇayenmantravittamaḥ |
agniṃ prajvalitaṃ vande manunānena prāñjaliḥ ||

prārthayitvāpyupasthāya gurustanmupasīdatu |
guruvandana pūrvaṃ ca daśa digbandhanaṃ tataḥ ||

tato nyāsaṃ vidhāyātha kṛśāno manubhirnyaset |
jiṃhāṅgamūrtimanubhiḥ hiraṇyādi yathākramam ||

liṅgepāne tathā mūrdhni vadane ca gurūttamaḥ |
nāsikānayanadvandve sarvāṅgeṣu ca vinyaset ||

<p. 229> ṛṣyādimaṅgavinyāsaṃ hṛdayādi nyasedguruḥ |
jātavedāḥ saptajihvo havyavāhanasaṃjñakaḥ ||

dhruvāgnaye padādyāścandreṇamontāḥ kramādamūn |
mūrdhāṃsa pārśvakaṭyādi pradakṣiṇavaśādbudhaḥ ||

itthaṃ nyāsaṃ vidhāyātha prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret |
pañcasaṃkhya samadbhiśca sādhayecca maheśvari ||

tataḥ saṃkalpamuccārya juhuyācca pṛthak pṛthak |
sadyojātādibhirmantraiḥ samidbhiśca yathākramam ||

idhmabandhanarajjūṃśca badhvā caikīkṛtaṃ priye |
dakṣiṇe ca pravinyasya darbhamuṣṭiṃ tathottare ||

pālāśodumbarāśvattha plakṣanyagrodhajodbhavaḥ |
aṣṭādaśa punastisro kuṇḍamānasamucchritāḥ ||

aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā paristaraṇadarbhakaiḥ |
ṣaḍaṅgulasuvistāraṃ muṣṭidarbhaṃ śataṃ guruḥ ||

rajjudve tatvasaṃcaiśca prokṣaṇī saptadarbhakaiḥ |
praṇītārṇavadarbhaiśca dvādaśairbrahmakūrcakam ||

<p. 230> sruksruvau mānunaṃ sapta ājyasaṃskāra ṣaṭkuśaiḥ |
dvedarbhe caruśuddhiḥ syāt pātrāṇāṃ sādhanārthake ||

śatārdhaṃ ca tadardhaṃ vā kuśakuptividhīyate |
svarṇaṃ vā rājataṃ vāpi tāmraṃ vātha śarāvakam ||

pūritaprasthamekaṃ vā tanyūnaṃ ca na kārayet |
sruk sruvau hastamātrau ca śuktimātraprapūritam ||

etatpātrāṇi saṃpādya kālabhinnāni varjayet |
tataḥ parisamūhyāgniṃ juṣṭedāhutimantrataḥ ||

pariṣicya jalenātha astramantreṇa deśikaiḥ |
dikparistaraṇaṃ kuryāt devasyatveti mantrataḥ ||

dakṣiṇānuttarān devi nuttarānadharān guruḥ |
mekhalāpari madhye tu vinyasecca yathākramam ||

pātrāṇāṃ sādhayet paścāt kuṇḍasyottara pārśvage |
bhūtale kuśamāstīrya praṇītāṃ prokṣaṇāṃ tathā ||

ājyapātraṃ carusthālīṃ sruksruvau kuśamuṣṭikam |
idhmapravaścanaṃ devi sādhayeddeśikottamaḥ ||

<p. 231> dvandvarūpeṇa hastābhyāṃ mantreṇāsminnadhomukham |
arghyodakena saṃprokṣya darśayedastramudrikām ||

prokṣaṇīmanmukhaṃ kṛtyā sukūrcaṃ vinyasettataḥ |
jalerāpaḥ punantveti pūrayeddhenumudrayā ||

karābhyāṃ tattrirutpūya tajjalaiḥ prokṣya cāstrataḥ |
vidhmaṃ visrasya granthiṃ ca varmaṇā ca maheśvari ||

pātrāṇyetāni tāsukṣya unmukhīkṛtya prokṣayet |
dakṣiṇe padmapīṭhaṃ ca darbhakūrcaṃ nyasettataḥ ||

āvāhya ca dvijaśreṣṭhaṃ bhūpatetvatha mantrataḥ |
arvāvasoḥ samāvāhya gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet ||

tatpurastātpraṇītānāṃ nidhāyādbhiḥ prapūrayet |
brahmabījau mantreṇa siddhamantreṇa pārvati ||

āvāhya varuṇaṃ paścādbrahmāṇaṃ ca samarcayet |
dorbhyāṃ nāsāntamuddhṛtya brahmaṇaścāpyanujñayā ||

dhaneśeśvarayormadhye pūrvapīṭhaṃ kuśopari |
nidhāya darbhamācchādya (***) aṣṭavāraṃ guruḥ ||

<p. 232> ghṛtasthālīṃ samādāya prokṣayedastramantrataḥ |
tatra goghṛtamāpūrya netramantreṇa vīkṣayet ||

vāyavyāṃ diśi māsthāpya mekhalasya samīpataḥ |
tanutreṇa ca nīrājyaṃ tadagnau nikṣipedguruḥ ||

punardarbhadvaye mantrī astramantreṇa pārvati |
trivāraṃ darśayitvā tu manunāgnau vinikṣipan ||

ādāya yonau vinyasya aṅgārān saha yojayet |
darbhau prādeśamātreṇa madhye granthiyutena ca ||

śukramasīti mantreṇa vahnimadhye ca nikṣipet |
caruṃ ca śrapayetpaścāt nirvapāmīti mantrataḥ ||

agnaye vo juṣṭaṃ prokṣyāmi agnaye vo juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmiti devatā ca 
pradhānakam |

trivāramiti muccārya darbhau sandarśa gāmayet |
abhighāryottare nyasya jananāgnirna karma ca ||

sruksruvau śodhayetpaścāt dakṣavāmakaradvaye |
agramadhyamamūleṣu kuśayugasya śodhayet ||

<p. 233> agnau niṣṭapya saṃmṛjya mūlamantreṇa vai triśaḥ |
evameva srucaṃ śodhya nyasettatraiva tān punaḥ ||

paridhīṃśca gṛhītvātha varmanetrāsumantrakaiḥ |
paścime dakṣiṇe nyasya uttare ca maheśvari ||

tadūrdhvasamiddhanyasya kavacena dvayaṃ guruḥ |
anuyājasamittatra praṇītordhve nyasedguruḥ ||

pāvakaṃ gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ jiṃhvādi paripūjayet |
lokapālādikān pūjya kuṇḍamekhaladevatāḥ ||

brāhmī vajrī tathā caindrī dhanvirākṣī maheśvarī |
śabalā ca tathā vegā kramātkuṇḍāni devatāḥ ||

paridhiṃ paristaraṇaṃ devi brahmāṇaṃ ca yathākramam |
vāṇīṃ lakṣmīṃ pārvatīṃ ca kramādāvāhya pūjayet ||

samidhaṃ ca tato hutvā pariṣecanamārabhet |
aditetyādi mantreṇa paritaśca yathākramam ||

agnaye jātavedāśca arcayeduktamārgataḥ |
grāhyaṃ pañcadaśedhmaṃ tu gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet ||

<p. 234> praśnītāyāmimāṃ rajjuṃ nyasedvalayavattataḥ |
samitsamūhamājyena samyagāplāvya deśikaḥ ||

dviśīrṣaṃ caikahṛdayaṃ catuśrotra dvināsikam |
āsyadvayaṃ ca ṣaṇṇetraṃ piṅgalaṃ saptahastakam ||

trimekhalaṃ tripādaṃ ca saptajiṃhvābhirāvṛtam |
catuśṛṅgaṃ vṛṣārūḍhaṃ balādityasamaprabham ||

upavītasamāyuktaṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitam |
savyabhāge caturhastaṃ trihastamapasavyake ||

sruksruvau cākṣamālāṃ ca śaktiṃ dakṣiṇahastake |
tomaraṃ vyajanaṃ caiva ghṛtapātraṃ tu vāmake ||

evaṃ dhyātvā tato mantrī brahmaṇo'nujñayā purā |
idhmahomaṃ tataḥ paścāt sruveṇājyaṃ hṛdāṇunā ||

nāḍīsandhau tu vijñeyā vaktrodghāṭanamādarāt |
dakṣanetregnaye svāhetyudaran juhuyāt guruḥ ||

vāmatastvājyamādāya vahne vāmavilopane |
hṛdā juhotu somāya svāheti ca guruḥ priye ||

<p. 235> madhyanetra tato vahne gṛhītvā madhyato ghṛtam |
agnīṣomābhyāṃ svāheti sṛvanmantreṇa netrataḥ ||

ghṛtamādāya deveśi dakṣabhāgāddhṛdāṇunā |
vahnivaktre'gnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāheti homayet ||

vahnimantraṃ tato hutvā tāravyāhṛtibhistriśaḥ |
jiṃhvāṅgamūrtimanubhiḥ juhuyācca yathākramam ||

ṛṣi triṣṭubha mantrairṇaissaṃjapya ca guru priye |
ādau mūlena saṃpūjya upacāraiśca deśikaḥ ||

juhuyāt samidhaṃ paścāttanmūlamanuvittamaḥ |
aṣṭāṣṭājyāhutirhutvā krameṇa ca pṛthak pṛthak ||

garbhādhānādikāḥ kuryāt vivāhāntāḥ kriyāḥ kramāt |
garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantaṃ balijātake ||

syānnāmakaraṇaṃ paścādupaniṣkramaṇaṃ tataḥ |
annaprāśanakaṃ caulamupanītaṃ tato guruḥ ||

catvāri vedaprathitā vratāni snānakarma ca |
vivāhāntamiti proktāḥ garbhādhānādi karmaṇām ||

<p. 236> paścāt karma karomīti pratyekaṃ samidhaṃ hunet |
ājyena homayedante vahno pūrṇāhutiṃ hunet ||

tataḥ sūtakaśuddhyarthaṃ jātakarmavidhau kṛte |
tatparistaraṇādīni sādhanāni parityajet ||

saṃprokṣya gomayajalaiḥ tatkuṇḍaṃ ca maheśvari |
pūrvavatsādhanaṃ kuryādyathāvadanu deśikaḥ ||

jiṃhvāmantrairmūrtimantraiḥ daśabhedavibheditaiḥ |
mahāgaṇapatermantraiḥ pūrvapūrvāpitairhunet ||

pīṭhapūjyātha tadvahnau devaṃ dhyātvā tato guruḥ |
uddiśya devatārūpaṃ yajetsāvaraṇādikaiḥ ||

saṃmukhīkaraṇārthaṃ ca pañcaviṃśāhutirhunet |
devatāhyatanāmaikyaṃ bhāvayenmūlamantrataḥ ||

ekādaśāhutirnāḍī sandhānāya hunettataḥ |
ekaikamāvṛtīnāṃ ca devatābhyo ghṛtāhutiḥ ||

ityevaṃ ca guruḥ kuryāt vahnerjananakarmaṇi |
na kuryācca huneddhomaṃ taddhomo niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||

<p. 237> [iti śrīvātulottare umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde kāmyakāṇḍe 
agnijananavidhānaṃ nāma pañcacatvāriḥ paṭalaḥ] |



ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mūrtipaiḥ sahito guruḥ |
dravyahomādi pūrṇāntaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kumbhapūjanam ||

tattajjapaṃ tatphalaṃ ca krameṇa śṛṇu pārvati |
maṇṭapāntasthakumbheṣu mūrtipāścopaviśya ca ||

āsanādi viśuddhiṃ ca nyāsaṃ kuryānmaheśvari |
kuṇḍaśuddhiṃ prakurvīta sadyojātādi pañcakaiḥ ||

śodhanaṃ kṣālanaṃ caiva śoṣaṇaṃ pālanaṃ tathā |
abhyukṣaṇaṃ ca pañcaite stvanyakuṇḍeṣu saṃsmaraiḥ ||

ācāryakuṇḍādanalaṃ pūrvādi viharettataḥ |
agnimīleti mantreṇa pūrvopasthāya cānalam ||

iṣe tvorjeti mantreṇāgneyāñca nyasettataḥ |
dakṣiṇe ca tvamagneti upatvāgne ca nair(-)ṛte ||

<p. 238> vāruṇe jātavedādi vāyavye vyāhṛtiṃ priye |
agniṃ dūtaṃ cottare ca agnimagnīti caiśake ||

viharedanalaṃ paścāt prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret |
jiṃhvāṅgamūrtinyāsādi tataḥ saṃkalpapūrvakam ||

hutvā dravyāṇi saṃkhyābhiḥ devatānāmamoccaran |
abādhānādi deveśi gurumūrtidharā pṛthak ||

svasvakuṇḍe ca taissarvairuktavatkārayet kramāt |
caruṃ vinā na kartavyaṃ brūhitā brahmapakvakam ||

paridhiṃ ca maheśāni mūrtipaiśca na kārayet |
bhavādyaṃ mūrtimāvāhya pūjayetpīṭhamantrakaiḥ ||

ekaikaśaśca juhuyuḥ jiṃhvādi manubhistathā |
ṛtvijo deśikādyāśca ṛṣicchandāṃsi mūlataḥ ||

pakvāhutiṃ ca juhuyuḥ pañcabhiścāghṛtāhutim |
dravyāṇāmāhutiṃ paścājjuhuyurdeśikādikāḥ ||

hṛdaye vaṃśaśūnyaṃ ca udare vṛṣṭināśanam |
kaṭau tu sthānanāśaṃ ca jānumuccāṭanaṃ bhavet ||

<p. 239> maraṇaṃ bāhumūle tu jiṃhvāyāṃ sarvakāmadam |
tasmādagnerviśeṣeṇa jiṃhvāyāṃ homamācaret ||

hutvā mudrāṃ ca deveśi mṛgī haṃsī ca sūkarī |
mayūrī kukkuṭī caiva homadravyagṛhe priye ||

mudrāhīnastu yo homaḥ sa sarvo niṣphale bhavet |
ājyaṃ samiddhaviścaiva godhūmaṃ sarṣapaṃ śive ||

tilaṃ ca truṭikaṃ lājān saktuṃ kṣīraṃ daśa kramāt |
dvisahasraṃ goghṛtaṃ ca kuḍupaṃ ca maheśvari ||

pālāśī khādirī vātha ṣaḍaṅgulasuvistṛtā |
anādrā vakrahīnā ca śobhitā ca samidbhavet ||

śālyudbhavaistaṇḍulaiśca prasthaṃ pañcaśataṃ bhavet |
haridrāsaṃyutaṃ pakvaṃ kliṣṭadoṣādivarjitam ||

godhūmādi dravyamanyat tadardhaṃ parikīrtitam |
kṣālitaṃ śodhitaṃ pātre pūrayenmūlamantrataḥ ||

uddiśyadevatāmantraṃ praṇavādi yathākramam |
mūlamantraṃ ca juhuyāt gurumūrti dharāḥ pṛthak ||

<p. 240> sahasrordhvaṃ pañcaśataṃ cāṣṭāhuti guruṃ priye |
pradhānakuṇḍe juhuyāduddeśyatyāgapūrvakam ||

homayeyurmūrtipāste pṛthak kuṇḍāṣṭake punaḥ |
sahasraṃ triśataṃ devi daśasapta catustathā ||

mūrtipānāṃ pṛthak homoditaṃ pratididinaṃ prati |
pṛthak dravyaṃ lakṣamitaṃ pūrvājyaṃ cāparājyakam ||

uttamaṃ ca huneddhomo uddiśya divasāntakam |
daśa dravyāṇi vā devi hunerlakṣaṃ gurūttamaḥ ||

dravyānte homayedājyaṃ madhyamaṃ ca maheśvari |
sarvadravyaṃ hunerlakṣaṃ adhamaṃ parikīrtitam ||

tato vai vedikāmadhye pañcaviṃśottaraṃ śatam |
padaṃ pūrvoktavat kuryāt padmamālikhya deśikaḥ ||

pañcavarṇakacūrṇaiśca pūrayecca pṛthak pṛthak |
śālitaṇḍulamāpūrya droṇārdhaṃ ca tadardhakam ||

prasthaṃ prasthaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ pradhānādi yathākramam |
svarṇādikumbhaṃ saṃgṛhya pañcaviṃśottaraṃ śataṃ ||

<p. 241> droṇavāri dhṛtaṃ kumbhaṃ tadardhaṃ ca tadardhakam |
anyeṣāṃ pūritaṃ prasthaṃ chinnabhinnādi varjitam ||

kṣālitān kalaśān devi astramantreṇa deśikaḥ |
varmaṇā tantunāveṣṭyā dhūpaiḥ saṃdhūpya mūlataḥ ||

sthāpayetkalaśaṃ paścāt kavacena maheśvari |
adhomukhaṃ ca kumbhaṃ ca pūjayecca kalātmakam ||

astramantreṇa saṃprokṣya kumbhamuddhṛtya conmukham |
ṣaṭtriṃśadarbhakūrcaṃ ca pañcaviṃśatikurcakam ||

saptaviṃśati kūrcaṃ ca pañca pañca yathākramam |
darbhakūrcaṃ ca vinyasya payasā pūrayedghaṭam ||

vāriṇābhyaṣṭagandhaṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ kuśodakam |
pañcāmṛtaṃ kaṣāyaṃ ca pūrayennarmalodakam ||

navaratnaṃ pañcaratnaṃ vinyase mātṛkādikān |
daśaniṣkaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca pradhānakalaśe nyaset ||

aṣṭavidyeśakumbheṣu pratimāṃ pañcaniṣkakām |
niṣkeṇa pratimāṃ hemnāṃ bahirāvaraṇe nyaset ||

<p. 242> paṇena hemnā pratimāmanyeṣu kalaśeṣu ca |
vinyaset prāṇamantreṇa auṣadhāni vinikṣipet ||

cūtapallavameteṣu vinyase netramantrataḥ |
nālikeraphala nyastvā kavacena maheśvari ||

dukūlavāsasācchādya astramantreṇa pārvati |
vicitrairnavavāsobhiḥ śvetavastrairyathākramam ||

sarvakumbhān samācchādya kūrcaṃ śirasi mantrataḥ |
prāṇāyāmādi vinyāsaṃ pīṭhapūjāṃ samācaret ||

kumbheṣvāvāhya deveśān vidyeśādi yathākramam |
sarvān devān samāvāhya pra (*) māvaraṇādikān ||

pañcaviṃśottaraśatam āvāhyābhyarcya māntrikaḥ |
kalān saṃpūjya deveśi ṣaṭtriṃśattatvamantrataḥ ||

pañcabrahmamanūn paścādupacāraissamarcayet |
daśa viprāṃśca parito japeyurmūlamantrataḥ ||

sahasraṃ pañcaviṃśatyā ṛṣyādinyāsapūrvakam |
caturastraṃ caturdikṣu gaṇeśaṃ kṣetrapālakam ||

<p. 243> durgāmanuṃ ca vāṇīṃ ca prāsādaṃ ca triyambakam |
bhuvaneśīṃ ca vāstuṃ ca rākṣoghnaṃ ca navagrahān ||

śrīrudraṃ pāvamānaṃ ca śrīsūktaṃ viṣṇusūktakam |
bhūsūktaṃ brahmasūktaṃ ca dhṛtasūktamanuṃ tataḥ ||

japet saptaśatiṃ devi paritaśca yathākramam |
caturdvāre caturvedān vadeyurbhūsurottamān ||

tarpayet saumyadigbhāge daśāṃśaṃ ca payombubhiḥ |
daśaviprāṃścopaviśya padmamālikhya maṇḍale ||

śālitaṇḍulamāpūrya tatra pātrāṇi vinyaset |
payasā vāriṇāpūrya gandhapuṣpaṃ ca nikṣipet ||

āvāhya pūjayeddevaṃ tarpayettilataṇḍulaiḥ |
sahasraṃ dviśataṃ sārdhaṃ mantrī mūlāṇunā yutaḥ ||

yāvaddhomāvasānāntaṃ navaṃ kuryānmaheśvari |
homānte sviṣṭakṛddhomo yadasyetyaṇunā guruḥ ||

kṛtvānuyājasamidhaṃ rajjuṃ hutvā tataḥ param |
jayādiṃśca tato hutvā prāyaścittāhutiṃ tataḥ ||

<p. 244> tato mahāvyāhṛtibhirekaikāṃ sarpiṣāhutiḥ |
tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ devi brahmāṇaṃ ca visṛjya ca ||

hāmaṃ samāpayedante balidānaṃ samācaret |
navapiṇḍān prakurvīta ājyena haviṣā tataḥ ||

gurukuṇḍādikā nyastvā kāṣṭhadīpān prapadya ca |
vedikumbhān yajet paścādācāryo yajamānakaḥ ||

śiṣyamāhūya tatkumbhaṃ pradhānaṃ ca maheśvari |
śirasā dhārayenmantrī vedamaṅgalavisvanaiḥ ||

sarvān kumbhān samādāya mūrtipādi dvijottamaiḥ |
pradakṣiṇavaśānmantrī ghaṭamantaḥ praveśayet ||

liṅgaṃ saṃpūjya mūlena pradhānādi yathākramam |
tasyāḥ sūktena saṃsiñcet mūlamantreṇa pārvati ||

mahāpūjā prakartavyā prāguktena maheśvari |
sarvebhyaśca baliṃ datvā tatsarvaṃ ca samarpayet ||

sahasradviśataṃ sārdhaṃ bhūsurān bhojayet priye |
śālyānnaṃ saghṛtaṃ kṣīrasūpaśākasvādusamanvitam ||

<p. 245> bhakṣaṃ pāyasasukṣīraśarkarā madhusaṃyutam |
bhūkteṣu teṣu deveśi tāmbūlaṃ ca pradāpayet ||

mantrāśīrvacanaṃ grāhyaṃ yajamānena bhaktitaḥ |
evameva samuddiṣṭadināntaṃ ca dine dine ||

pūrṇāhutistadante tu kṛtvā cāvabhṛtakriyām |
visujya kaṅkaṇaṃ paścāt śuddhasnānaṃ samācaret ||

yāgagehaṃ praviśyātha gurukuṇḍasamīpataḥ |
bhūsurebhyaścopaviśya yajamāne guruḥ priye ||

gobhūmyādīni dānāni chāgaṃ ca mahiṣaṃ tathā |
khaḍgaṃ ca tilatailaṃ cānaḍvāhaṃ ca dāpayet ||

puṣpaṃ phalaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ pānīyaṃ śayanādikam |
dadhyodanaṃ yajñasūtraṃ kanyādānaṃ maheśvari ||

vijanaṃ pādukaṃ chatraṃ cāmaraṃ svarṇakumbhakam |
tadante guptadānaṃ ca kṛcchraṃ dadyānmaheśvari ||

bhūsurāṇāṃ deśikādi dakṣiṇāṃ vidhivadbudhaḥ |
pañcaviṃśatiniṣkaṃ ca vijanaṃ vāhanaṃ tathā ||

<p. 246> chatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ caiva tantropakaraṇaiḥ saha |
guptadānaṃ ca dāsaṃ ca deśikāya pradāpayet ||

taduktaṃ māntrikāyātha dadyādvibhavavistaram |
saptaṃ ca daśaniṣkaṃ ca paryaṅkaṃ śayyayāvṛtam ||

svayaṃ gṛhya maheśāni ācāryāya pradāpayet |
daśaniṣkaṃ suvastraṃ ca brahmaṇe dāpayet priye ||

saptaniṣkaṃ pādukaṃ ca sadasyāya pradāpayet |
mūrtigānāṃ pañcaniṣkaṃ pṛthak dadyācca kuṇḍalam ||

sārdhaniṣkaṃ dvijātibhyaḥ tarpakebhyaḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
sārdhaniṣkadvayaṃ vedaghoṣakāṇāṃ pradāpayet ||

jāpakānāṃ sārdhaniṣkaṃ mūlamantrasya pārvati |
anyeṣāṃ jāpakādīnāṃ cārāṇāṃ niṣkamucyate ||

pradakṣiṇakṛtāṃ caiva pūjakānāṃ tathaiva ca |
dīnāndhabadhirādīnāṃ yathāśaktyātha dakṣiṇām ||

iti datvā krameṇaiva saṃpūrṇaphalamaśnute |
uktārdhe ca phalārdhaṃ syāduktāṃśe ca phalāṃśakam ||

<p. 247> jñātvā dharmamiti prājño svavaṃśasyābhivṛddhaye |
lakṣahomamimaṃ devi yaḥ kuryācca nṛpottamaḥ ||

brahmahatyādi pāpaghnaṃ sarvamṛtyunivāraṇam |
sarvaduḥkhopaśamanaṃ sarvadā maṅgalapradam ||

sarvaśāntikaraṃ jñeyaṃ putrapautrābhivṛddhidam |
tuṣṭipuṣṭipradaṃ devi kīrtidaṃ kānti vṛddhidam ||

āyurvṛddhikaraṃ saukhyaṃ sarvatra vijayaṃ bhavet |
bhūmidānena yatpuṇyaṃ tatphalaṃ bahubhirmakhaiḥ ||

dehānte mama sāyujyaṃ trissaptakulasaṃyutaḥ |
kāmitārthaphalaṃ śīghraṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ mayoditam ||

[iti śrīvātulāgame umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde kāmyakāṇḍe 
lakṣahomavidhi karmavidhivivara vidhānaṃ nāma saptacatvāriṃśat 
paṭalaḥ] ||



|| svasti ||

yasmin deve bhaveddevi nityakāryabalidvayam |
tadā naimittike mantrī trikālaṃ balimāharet ||

nitye vā vartamāne tu trikālaṃ balikarmaṇi |
tadā naimittikaṃ kuryānnityabalyā sahaiva tu ||

athavānyaprakāreṇa bhave naimittike baliḥ |
prātareva vinirvatya baliṃ nityaṃ maheśvari ||

madhyāhne caiva sāyāhne baliṃ naimittikaṃ haret |
nityamādau vinirvartya baliṃ nāḍīdvayātparam ||

naimittikabalimantrī prakuryādevameva vā |
kāmye balividhau devi viśeṣaḥ kvacidiṣyate ||

madhyāhne vātha sāyāhne kuryāttaddvitaye'pi vā |
nityabalyā sahaivātra pṛthagvā pūrva tadbalim ||

evaṃ nityādike devi balikrama itīritaḥ |
parivārāmarāṇāṃ ca saṃtṛptirnityakarmaṇām ||

paripūrṇaphalāvāptirdevasya ca bhavedyataḥ |
<p. 259> sukhaṃ ca sarvajantūnāṃ tasmānnityaṃ caredbalim ||

brahmalokādilokeṣu saṃsthitā ye surādikāḥ |
teṣāṃ prītirbhavedyena bhūpateḥ sarvasampat ||

śivasyātipriyaḥ kuryāttasmānnaimittikaṃ balim |
kartuḥ kāmyaphalāvāptiḥ sarvaśatrunivāraṇam ||

sarvaśāntirbhavedyena tasmātkāmyabaliṃ caret |
pūrvāhve brahmaṇaḥ prītiḥ viṣṇormadhyandine bhavet ||

sāyāhne śaṅkarasyaiva balidānaṃ hi pārvati |
sṛṣṭyādi bhedataḥ kalpya baliḥkālatraye tridhā ||

atrādhidevā vijñeyā brahmaviṣṇuśivastathā |
dvivaraṃ ca trivāraṃ ca caturvāraṃ pṛthak kramāt ||

nitye naimittike kāmye devatābhyo baliṃ haret |

|| svasti ||

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kālabhedena sāmpratam ||

śivādīnāṃ ca devānāṃ krameṇa śṛṇu pārvati |
<p. 250> nityādi karmabhedeṣu balidānavinirṇayam ||

balibhugdevatāścāpi balidravyaṃ tathaiva ca |
balimantrāṃśca balyuktavādyāni trividhāni ca ||

indrāditālagānānāṃ nāmānyatra vadāmyaham |
tṛptyarthaṃ sarvadevānāṃ sukhārthaṃ sarvadehinām ||

rājñāṃ vijaya rājya śrīputrāyuṣyādi vṛddhaye |
sarvāriṣṭanivṛttyai ca sarvalokahitāya ca ||

balidānaṃ prakartavyaṃ tasmāddevi prayatnataḥ |
prātaḥkāle ca madhyāhne sāyāhne ca krameṇa vai ||

balidānaṃ prakurvīta nitye naimittike tathā |
kāmye cāpi vidhau mantrī yathāvidhi maheśvari ||

madhyandine ca sāyāhne nityakarmaṇi cāthavā |
baliṃ dadyānmahotpāte mahāsnapanakarmaṇi ||

prāyaścitte gurau caiva pratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
mahotsavādi kāleṣu trikālaṃ balimāharet ||

śaive śāstari caivaṃ syāt balidānavidhikramaḥ |
<p. 251> durgāyāṃ gaṇanāthe tu balinnityādi karmasu ||

madhyāhne caiva sāyāhne kuryādvācotsavādiṣu |
kālatraye ca viṣṇau tu ṣaṇmukhe śaṅkarācyute ||

dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ vā nityādiṣu baliṃ haret |
evaṃ śivādi devānāṃ nityanaimittikādiṣu ||

balikālavibhedoktā balidevān śṛṇu priye |
nandī caiva mahākālo gaṇeśo dakṣiṇeśvaraḥ ||

śūlapāṇiṃ paraśupāṇiṃ dikpālāḥ sasurādhipāḥ |
tataḥ paraṃ mahānandī vīrabhadro vināyakaḥ ||

brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā |
vārāhī ca tathendrāṇī cāmuṇḍāssaptamātaraḥ ||

mātaraśca tathā lokā mātarassarvamātaraḥ |
sarvamātṛgaṇā lakṣmīrbhūcarī khecarī tathā ||

śāstā durgā guhaḥ śrīdaścaṇḍanāthastathaiva ca |
tīkṣṇa dauṃṣṭra mṛgāsyau ca gopuradvārapālakau ||

bhṛṅgī caiva mahākālo bhūtanātho mahīdharaḥ |
<p. 252> parvateśo guheśaśca kālapāśakapālinau ||

tadantye kṣelapālaśca mahāpīṭhe tu tatparam |
bhūtido durnirīkṣaśca bhīmarūpaṃ sulocanaḥ ||

sumukhaśca subāhuśca kālapāśakapālinau |
adhaḥpiṅgalakeśāśca harasenastadūrdhvataḥ ||

ete balibhujo nitye devāḥ śaive samīritāḥ |
caṇḍaḥ pracaṇḍo garuḍo'nanto vighneśvaraḥ śivaḥ ||

dikpālavīrabhadraikadantau brāhmyādikastathā |
śāstā durgā ca dhanado viṣ.vakseno'tha gopure ||

cakrapāṇiḥ śaṅkhapāṇiḥ śrībhūmiḥ parito bahiḥ |
kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṇḍarīko'tha vāmanaḥ ||

śaṅkukarṇaḥ sarpanetraḥ sumukhaḥsaṃpratiṣṭhitaḥ |
kṣetreśontye cātha balipīṭhe kiṣkandhapūrvakāḥ ||

kiṣkindhaḥ śaṅkaro bhaumo bhīmaśca sukumārakaḥ |
tato bāhulakaścaiva vijayo'nantavikramaḥ ||

<p. 253> punaḥ piṅgalakeśākṣo harasenastu madhyataḥ |
dvārādi balipīṭhāntaṃ balidevā ime harau ||

caṇḍikā cāmbiko siṃho dakṣiṇe gaṇapeśvarau |
dikpā vīreśa vighneśamātṛkādyāḥ puroditāḥ ||

śāstā durgā ca dhanado muṇḍinī gopure tataḥ |
hlādinī mohinī dvāri vijayantī tataḥ param ||

kātyāyanī bahiratho kālyādyā bali devatāḥ |
kālī karālī virajā mandarā vindhyavāsinī ||

suprabhā siṃhavaktrā ca daityamardinyataḥ param |
kṣetreśo balipīṭhe tu brahmaghoṣyādi devatāḥ ||

brahmaghoṣī sughoṣī mā yatheṣṭī sukharāgiṇī |
saṃmukhī subhagā dikṣu pramodinyatha cātmani ||

brāhmī madhye caivamuktā durgāyā bali devatāḥ |
vajrapāṇiḥ śaktipāṇiḥ sukumārāhvayastataḥ ||

<p. 254> bhadrasenaḥ śikhī bhāsya dakṣiṇāmūrtilokapāḥ |
mātṛśāstrambikāḥskando dhanado dhūrtasenakāḥ ||

agniketuḥ sūryaketuḥ gopuradvāri tadbahiḥ |
devasenāpatiścāgninetro dagdhosidhāryatha ||

pāśahastāṃ hayaḥ ketuhastasaṃjño gadādharaḥ |
tataḥ śūlavilāsinyādīśānyantastathopari ||

viprapriyo'tha kṣetreśo balipīṭhe tataḥ param |
bhūtidādyāstu śaivoktā deva atraiva coditāḥ ||

bali devā iti proktāḥ skandasya paramātmanaḥ |
kṣurikā khaḍgapāṇyantadvārapālau bahistathā ||

vīrabāhurmahāvīro nīlāśmebhāsyaśaṃkarau |
dikpālamātaraḥ śāstā dhanado ghoṣavāṃstathā ||

tīkṣṇaśca tīkṣṇadantaśca gopure parito bahiḥ |
goptā ca piṅgalākṣaśca vīrasenaśca śāmbhavaḥ ||

tridik śūlī dakṣabhīmarūpā kṣetrapa eva ca |
<p. 255> vīrabāhurmahāvīro vidyudvāsavilāsinau ||

tīkṣaśca tīkṣṇadantaśca tārākṣaśca bhavodbhavaḥ |
adhaḥ piṅgalakeśākṣaḥ śāstrasenastathopari ||

balipīṭhe tvime śāstra balidevā itīritāḥ |
vajradantagajāsyau ca mahāpāśo vināyakaḥ ||

śivadikpā mātṛśāstṛdurgāskandāṃ hayāstataḥ |
kumbhodaro gopure tu caṇḍavikramasaṃjñakaḥ ||

tato vighnakaro dikṣu vajradanto gajāsyakaḥ |
bhīmaśca mahiṣāsyaśca meghanādastathaiva ca ||

virūpākṣaśca varadaḥ sarvabhakṣaka eva ca |
tadāntyakṣetrapālaśca gajavaktrādikastataḥ ||

gajavaktragaṇeśākhyau tataścaiva vināyakaḥ |
caṇḍavikramako vighnakaraśūrpāyumekhalāḥ ||

śūrpakarṇastato nāgadantī syādrudravaktrajaḥ |
balipīṭhasya dikṣu syuradha urdhve tvime smṛtāḥ ||

<p. 256> evaṃ dvārādi saṃproktā vighneśabalidevatāḥ |
nandī caṇḍa jayatsena viṣvaksena vṛṣāstathā ||

garuḍo'nanta vighneśau śiva dikpālamātaraḥ |
śāstā durgā guhaścaiva caṇḍaseno'tha gopure ||

śūlāriṇau bahirdikṣu nandī caiva samantataḥ |
mahākālāṃ hayaḥ paścādbhūtanātho mahīdharaḥ ||

kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṇḍarīko'tha vāmanaḥ |
kṣetrapālastathaiśānyāṃ balipīṭhe tataḥ param ||

āyāntu pāśadāḥ sarve śāṅkarā vaiṣṇavāśca ye |
balipīṭhe'tra santuṣṭā nivasantu yathāsukham ||

onnamo yukpārṣadebhya ityārādhya baliṃ haret |
itaḥ paraṃ ca śarvādi balidravyaṃ śṛṇu priye ||

ghṛtānnaṃ bilvapatraṃ ca jātīkusumameva ca |
śrīgandhaṃ cākṣataṃ vāri dhūpadīpau tathaiva ca ||

balidravyamidaṃ śaive dadhyannaṃ tulasī tathā |
<p. 257> kalhārakusumaṃ raktacandanādyaṃ samīritam ||

agaruṃ jambupatraṃ ca mallikākusumaṃ tathā |
kṣīrodanaṃ ca durgāyāmanyatsarvaṃ ca pūrvavat ||

skande gorocanopetaṃ gandhaṃ caiva guḍodanam |
śatapuṣpaṃ ca nirguṇḍī patrañcāpagaṇādhipe ||

kucandanaṃ ca madhvannaṃ punipuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca |
nyagrodhacchandanaṃ vātha mudgānnaṃ śaṅkaracyate ||

karpūrāgaruyuggandhaṃ droṇapuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca |
apāmārgadalaṃ proktaṃ śāstarcyatha nigadyate ||

kṛsarānnaṃ ca karpūramiśracandanameva ca |
nandyāvartasumaṃ bilvapatraṃ cānyat puroktavat ||

deveṣu nikhileṣūktadravyālābhe'thavaiṣu ca |
śuddhodanaṃ vā dravyeṣu yathālabdhāni kevalam ||

gandhapuṣpāṇi patrāṇi bhaveyurbalikarmaṇi |
balidravyamiti proktaṃ balimantrān śṛṇu priye ||

dhyātvādau devamabhyarcya mantrī gandhādi mudrikāḥ |
<p. 258> pradṛśya tattaduktaiśca balimantrairbaliṃ haret ||

gandhaṃ kaniṣṭhayā puṣpa aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa deśikaḥ |
dhūpaṃ dadyācca tarjanyā dīpaṃ madhyamayā tathā ||

anāmayānnamityuktā mudrāgandhādikāḥ kramāt |
onnamontaṃ savedādi devanāma jalaṃ tataḥ ||

arcayāmuccareddevaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ tathaiva ca |
evamannaṃ vadenmantrī vahnijāyāvasānakam ||

pṛthak pṛthak dvārapādi devānāṃ ca yathākramam |
balimantrā ime nityavidhau sarvāmareṣu ca ||

santi naimittikādye tu balikarmaṇi kenacit |
upasthānānnavaścaiva mālāmantrāḥ sadāṃ tathā ||

manavaḥ pārṣadāṃ caiva mātṝṇāṃ tatra tatra vai |
balidevagaṇādhīśāḥ kecitpārṣadādikāḥ ||

nocyaṃ tatra mayoktatvādutsaveṣu savistaram |
balimantrā iti proktā vādyāni śṛṇu sāmpratam ||

śreṣṭhaṃ dvādaśavādyaṃ syā saptavādyaṃ ca madhyamam |
<p. 259> adhamaṃ pañcavādyaṃ syā vādyāni trividheti ca ||

bherīśaṅkhamṛdaṅgāśca paṇavo maddalastathā |
vīṇāveṇuśca tālaṃ ca kahalaṃ nāgavādyakam ||

yallarī kambuvādyaṃ syādevaṃ dvādaśavādyakam |
śaṅkho mṛdaṅgaḥ paṇavo vīṇā tālaṃ tathaiva ca ||

kahalaṃ dundubhiśceti saptavādyaṃ prakīrtitam |
maddalaścaiva tālaṃ ca tathaiva jayaghaṇṭikā ||

śaṅkhaśca kalahaṃceti pañcavādyaṃ prakīrtitam |
indrādi tālanāmāni procyante'tha yathākramam ||

samatālaṃ sureśasya vahnerbandhāpaṇāṃhvayam |
yāmyaṃ syā bhṛṅgitālaṃ tu mallatālaṃ tu nair(-)ṛtam ||

vāruṇaṃ nava tālaṃ syānnāgatālaṃ tu mārutam |
kauśikākhyaṃ tu saumyaṃ syāddakkarīśānatālakam ||

vṛṣatālaṃ mahokṣasya mātṝṇāṃ śaśitālakam |
skandasyotkalatālaṃ syāddaurgaṃ taṭaprahārakam ||

caṇḍanāthasya viṣamamathagānaṃ samīryate |
<p. 260> prācyāṃ tu gānaṃ gāndhāraṃ kolli cāgneyagocare ||

dakṣiṇe kauśikī caiva naṭabhāṣā ca nair(-)ṛte |
vāruṇaṃ kāmaraṃ gānaṃ karkaśī vāyugocare ||

tattadrāgaṃ cottare tu śālāpāṇī ca gocare |
evaṃ tālāni gānāni nitye balyutsave vidhau ||

nityamūrtiṃ bhrāmayettu yadā naimittikādike |
naimittikyā sahaivaināṃ balimūrtyā gurusstadā ||

asurājena saṃyuktāmantaliṅgena cāthavā |


[iti parameśvaratantre aṣṭatriṃśatpaṭalaḥ] ||




oṅkāraṃ bhuvaneśvarīṃ kṣitipāraṃ gorocanāḍhyam
vande dvākāraṃ tu virūḍhasaṃpuraṃ padam |

nityotsavāya berāṇi kalpitāni vadhūni cet |
tadardhaṃ kalpayetteṣu yatheṣṭaṃ beramunnatam ||

ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā tricatuḥpañcakālam |
<p. 261> ṣaṭ saptavasukālaṃ vā sarvadā cārcayecchivām ||

nityetvevaṃ samādiṣṭasarvābhīṣṭaphalapradā |
prātaḥkāle tu madhyāhne sāyāhne yadi pārvati ||

balikarmaṇi hīne tu prāyaścittaṃ samācaret |
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu nāndīkarma vidhāya ca ||

aghorāstreṇa vaṅkuryādaṣṭottaraśataṃ tathā |
pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṅgaiśca homaṃ kuryādyathā vidhi ||

snāpayetkalaśairdevaṃ pañcabhirnavabhistu vā |
devaṃ sāvaraṇaṃ pūjyā nivedyaṃ madhuraṃ dadet ||

devatābhyo baliṃ dadyādbrāhmaṇān bhojayettataḥ |
evaṃ prātarvidhi prokto madhyāhne dviguṇaṃ bhavet ||

sāyāhne tatsamaṃ devi prāyaścitavidhikramāt |

iti candrajñāne |
